`AGENTS.md`:

```md
# AGENTS.md — entry point for automated contributors

> 中文版：[`AGENTS.zh-CN.md`](AGENTS.zh-CN.md)。若中英文不一致，以英文版为准。

This repository delivers **O3 Root**: a Shizuku-driven Android app that
exploits `CVE-2026-98260` on Xiaomi `yingtian` (Pad 9 Pro Max) / `lhasa`
(Xiaomi 18 Fold, HyperOS 4.0.26) with kernel `6.18.21`, loads a KernelSU LKM,
and leaves a root daemon for the current boot. Read this file first, then
`README.md`, `docs/AGENT_HANDOFF.md`, `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`.

## State

- Release **v3.0**, `release/o3root2-v3.0.apk`
  (`14,218,768 B`, `sha256 c80f499a6b06ddf92241aa8cb0aa8874767eccdd960e631b28dcff98e7c141dd`).
  v2.0 added the multi-LKM blob (KernelSU / KernelSU-Next / SukiSU) with
  manager-based selection; v2.1 makes the probe permission-aware (local manager
  detection, probe-then-payload after granting, unique payload path); v3.0
  replaces the single-reap write with two reaps — an unaligned enforcing-only
  zero at `selinux_state-7` plus the cred swap — and drops the KernelSU init
  patch (KSU's own `setenforce(1)` is safe because `policycap` stays clean).
  v1.8 (relink-only) and v1.9 remain in the git history.
- Hardware-verified on `yingtian` / `OS4.0.16.0.XBMCNXM` (cold boot, end-to-end).
- `lhasa` / `OS4.0.26.0.XPNCNXM` ships the **bit-identical kernel**
  (`sha256 cde87cd7…`); not device-tested.
- Boot-only: a reboot removes root and KernelSU (legacy `O3P_SELDIRTY=1`
  paths also lose the permissive SELinux state; the v3 default ends in Enforcing).

## Fast commands

```bash
bash scripts/verify.sh            # static checks + optional builds (run before commit)
bash scripts/fetch-toolchain.sh   # android.jar, build-tools r34, Shizuku jars, pyelftools

# payload  (needs an NDK clang)
NDK_CC=/path/to/ndk/.../aarch64-linux-android35-clang bash payload/build-payload.sh
# reference result: 73,768 B payload; libo3p.so 1,492,484 B (three LKMs), sha256 e64217c8…87dfa

# app (needs SDK + Shizuku jars + your keystore; see docs/BUILD.md)
ANDROID_JAR=... BUILD_TOOLS=... SHIZUKU_JARS=... \
KEYSTORE=... KS_PASS=... KEY_ALIAS=... KEY_PASS=... bash app/build.sh
```

## Map

| path | what lives there |
|---|---|
| `payload/o3p.c` | the exploit payload (C): UAF trigger, page reclaim, RB write primitive, two stages, KASLR slide, KernelSU runtime loader (kallsyms first, relink fallback), legacy init patch (`O3P_CODEPATCH=1`), rootd |
| `payload/ksu/` | `ksu_symtab.h` (union of 248 KernelSU symbols from upstream forks), `mksymtab.py` (regen single-LKM table), `relink_ksu.py` (offline relinker reference) |
| `payload/lkm/` | official `android17-6.18` LKMs (GPL-3.0, unmodified): KernelSU v3.3.1, KernelSU-Next v3.4.2, SukiSU-Ultra v4.2.1-r2 |
| `app/src/o3/st/root1/` | Java: `Main` (UI/state machine), `PayloadRunner`, `StateProbe`, `RecoverRunner` (legacy guard; disabled in v3), `ManagerInstaller`, listeners |
| `app/build.sh`, `payload/build-payload.sh`, `scripts/*` | build entry points |
| `docs/` | ARCHITECTURE (design + porting), SECURITY (risks/cleanup), BUILD, APP_O3_ROOT (dev log), OTA/kernel report, CVE feasibility, evidence PNGs |
| `tools/` | OTA extraction / boot image unpack / BTF+disasm checks |
| `release/` | prebuilt APK + SHA256SUMS |

## Invariants — do not "clean up"

1. **Kernel profile constants are load-bearing.** `INIT_USER_NS_STATIC`,
   `SELINUX_STATE_STATIC`, probe offsets, BTF offsets and `ksu_symtab.h` are
   tied to this exact kernel build. Don't reformat or "simplify" them; changing
   them without a matching image = kernel oops.
2. **v3 applies no KernelSU init patch by default — keep it that way.**
   The network break is fixed at the source: stage 0 zeroes only the
   `enforcing` byte (8-byte unaligned zero at `selinux_state-7`, leaving
   `initialized`/`policycap` intact), so when KernelSU restores Enforcing the
   Wi-Fi path stays healthy. `KSU_CODEPATCH skip` is the expected line.
   `O3P_CODEPATCH=1` restores the legacy patch (dynamic `.rela.init.text`
   rewrite, offsets `0x144` tiann / KernelSU-Next, `0x178` SukiSU) for
   comparison only. Do **not** patch the `bl setenforce` itself:
   that slot carries a `CALL26` relocation, the loader rewrites it, and the
   result is `Undefined instruction` with the module stuck in `coming` (see
   `docs/APP_O3_ROOT.md`).
3. **The write primitive can only write `0` or a controlled-page pointer.**
   It writes `[forged_node]` and dereferences `parent = value & ~3`, so `0x100`
   would be dereferenced. Don't "just write 0x0100".
4. **`real_cred` is not replaced.** Never call `finit_module` in-process — a
   fresh exec is required (`fork` + `sh -c insmod`). In-process load BUGs in
   `commit_creds` (`task->cred != task->real_cred`).
5. **v3 stage 0 no longer pollutes `policycap`.** The 8-byte unaligned zero at
   `selinux_state-7` clears only `enforcing`, so Enforcing is safe and the app
   ships no recovery guard. Only the legacy `O3P_SELDIRTY=1` path dirties
   `policycap[]`; if used, any `setenforce 1` freezes Wi-Fi TX until
   `setenforce 0` or reboot.
6. **rootd protocol**: abstract socket `@o3p_rootd`, `SO_PEERCRED` only accepts
   uid 0/2000, one command per connection, terminated by `__EXIT=<n>`. Do not
   reintroduce world-writable command files in `/data/local/tmp`.
7. **Shizuku must be in shell mode** (`uid=2000`, `u:r:shell:s0`). Root-mode
   Shizuku runs in `u:r:ksu:s0`, and KSU's bounded domain rejects exec of the
   payload.
8. **Release hygiene**: `android:debuggable` stays off; the firmware gate in
   `Main.checkFirmware()` must match the kernel/branch the payload was built
   for (`yingtian`/`lhasa`).
9. **KernelSU loading is runtime-resolved by default.** `kallsyms_patch()`
   collects the module's UNDEF names, temporarily writes `0` to
   `/proc/sys/kernel/kptr_restrict`, resolves addresses through
   `/proc/kallsyms`, and always restores the old value before `insmod`.
   `O3P_RELINK_ONLY=1` forces the embedded 248-symbol table + slide path
   (offline fallback if kallsyms ever stops exposing addresses). Upstream LKMs
   (tiann, KernelSU-Next, SukiSU) all load; their manager packages and ksud
   differ, so swapping forks also means swapping the manager APK/ksud.

## Verified / not verified

Verified on hardware (v3.0): full cold-boot chain (`uid=0` + full caps),
KernelSU `Live` with **no init patch**, manager auto-open, Enforcing end state
with healthy network (`ping`/`nc`) and unchanged `boot_id`. v1.8–v2.1 had
additionally verified the old guard/recovery UI and network health while
Permissive (both removed in v3.0). The v2.0 release APK was cold-boot tested; v2.1 was cold-boot tested with a revoked Shizuku permission (grant → probe → automatic payload)
end-to-end on three images (only the tiann manager, only KernelSU-Next, only
SukiSU): each time the app detected the manager, the payload selected the
matching embedded LKM and the matching manager came to the foreground;
`O3P_RELINK_ONLY=1` still falls back to the 248-symbol union.

Not verified: `lhasa` hardware; **original Shizuku** (we use the official
`dev.rikka.shizuku` API, but only ShizukuX 13.7 was exercised); `/system/bin/su`
appears only after KernelSU's first root request.

Negative results — don't repeat without a new idea (details in
`docs/AGENT_HANDOFF.md`):

- io_uring ring pages **cannot** reclaim the freed order-1 `posix_timers` slab
  (setup allocates order-0/vmalloc; measured 0 hits).
- `AF_PACKET` `PACKET_TX_RING` (8 KB blocks) **does** reclaim order-1 pages at
  93.5% — but needs `CAP_NET_RAW`, which the pre-root shell/App domain lacks.
- Forging `cred_security_struct.sid = 1` gives `unlabeled`, and KernelSU's
  `hook_file_open` dereferences the fake blob → oops (SID route unusable with
  KSU loaded).

## Build traps

- `d8` chokes on **anonymous/inner classes** (internal NPE) and doesn't accept
  directories; keep top-level classes and pass `build/classes/**/*.class`.
- Git Bash + `javac`: classpath wildcard `build/cp/*`; `;` does not work.
- Windows: pass `E:/forward/slash` paths to `java`/`aapt2`/`zipalign`
  (`/e/...` is bash-only). The scripts auto-add `.exe`.
- Python-generated C/Java: heredoc turns `\n` into a real newline — build such
  strings with `chr(92)+'n'`, or write the file with the editor tool.
- `aapt2 link` needs a prior `aapt2 compile --dir res`; `-A assets` ships the
  bundled KernelSU manager.
- Don't commit CRLF churn for `payload/*.c`; the index normalizes to LF.

## Device testing checklist

1. Shizuku running in shell mode: `ps -A -o UID,LABEL,ARGS | grep shizuku_plus_server`
   → `2000 u:r:shell:s0`.
2. Install: `adb install -r release/o3root2-v3.0.apk`; if MIUI says
   `INSTALL_FAILED_USER_RESTRICTED`, allow install-via-USB or, with root,
   `pm install -r -d`.
3. Open the app, grant Shizuku permission, press Continue; wait 20–120 s.
4. Success markers: `★ UIDNOW uid=0`, `KSU_INSMOD_OUT:[rc=0]`,
   `★ KSU_MODULE LIVE`, manager in foreground (`am start` log + `top ->`).
5. Health: `getenforce` = Enforcing (v3; legacy dirty write ends Permissive), `ping <gateway>`, `nc 223.5.5.5 443`,
   `cat /proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id` unchanged.
6. Rollback: reboot; optional `rm -f /data/local/tmp/o3p*` and uninstall.

| symptom | meaning / action |
|---|---|
| `VERDICT NO_RECLAIM` repeated | race is probabilistic; retry (1–6 attempts typical), reboot if stuck |
| `固件门禁未通过` | kernel/fingerprint mismatch — new profile needed |
| network dead after KernelSU | only with legacy `O3P_SELDIRTY=1`: polluted policycap + Enforcing → `setenforce 0` or reboot |
| `KSU_CODEPATCH … -2` | only with legacy `O3P_CODEPATCH=1`: the `setenforce` relocation was not found (unique-scan fallback failed) — check the LKM |
| manager not opening | check the app install path/package, and that KSU manager package exists |

## Hygiene before commit

```bash
bash scripts/verify.sh
```

It checks: shell/Python syntax, required files, `ksu_symtab.h` count, release
APK checksum, tracked secrets, and scans every file (including binaries) for
device serials, host paths, keystore names/passwords, packet-capture-style
identifiers (MAC/BSSID/SSID/IP). If it fails, fix the match — do not weaken the
check.

Rules: never commit `tools/` (SDK/NDK/Shizuku jars), `app/build`,
`payload/build`, `*.p12/*.jks`, `env.sh`, OTA zips, `.img`, or real device
identifiers. Third-party licenses (`NOTICE.md`, `third_party/NOTICE.md`) must
stay accurate — KernelSU (LKM + manager APK) is GPL-3.0.

## Where to start for a task

- **Port to another kernel/device**: `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` → "Porting"; then
  regenerate `payload/ksu/ksu_symtab.h`, update constants, re-locate the KSU
  patch offset (only if you keep the legacy `O3P_CODEPATCH=1` path), update the
  app gate, and test cold boot.
- **App/UI/feature work**: `app/src/o3/st/root1/*.java`; build with
  `app/build.sh`; keep the class set free of anonymous classes.
- **Payload behavior**: `payload/o3p.c`; rebuild with
  `payload/build-payload.sh` and compare `libo3p.so` sha against the reference.
- **Open ideas / history of experiments**: `docs/AGENT_HANDOFF.md`.

### v3 (current)

v3 keeps the two-stage exploit but changes both writes: stage 0 is an
**enforcing-only** zero (8-byte unaligned write at `selinux_state-7`), stage 1
is the usual `task_cred` swap. KernelSU is then loaded with the runtime
kallsyms loader and **no init patch**; its own `setenforce(1)` is harmless
because `policycap` stayed clean, so the device ends in Enforcing with working
Wi-Fi. A single-reap three-write variant was tried and continuously panicked;
it is kept only behind `O3P_D3V3` for analysis. Cold-boot tested on yingtian
(`KSU_CODEPATCH skip`, `KALLSYMS_PATCH found=225`, `KSU_MODULE LIVE`,
Enforcing, `ping`/`nc` OK, manager opened).

```

# AGENTS.zh-CN.md — 自动化贡献者入口（中文版）

> 英文原版：[`AGENTS.md`](AGENTS.md)。若两者不一致，以英文版为准，并同步本文件。
> 交接细节（已验证状态、失败实验、待办、复现流程）见
> [`docs/AGENT_HANDOFF.zh-CN.md`](docs/AGENT_HANDOFF.zh-CN.md)。

本仓库交付 **O3 Root**：一个由 Shizuku 驱动的 Android 应用，在小米 `yingtian`
（Pad 9 Pro Max）/ `lhasa`（Xiaomi 18 Fold，HyperOS 4.0.26）、内核 `6.18.21`
上利用 `CVE-2026-98260`，加载 KernelSU LKM，并在当前 boot 内保留一个 root
守护进程。请先读本文件，再读 `README.md`、`docs/AGENT_HANDOFF.md`、
`docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`。

## 状态

- 发布 **v3.0**：`release/o3root2-v3.0.apk`
  （`14,218,768 B`，`sha256 c80f499a6b06ddf92241aa8cb0aa8874767eccdd960e631b28dcff98e7c141dd`）；
  载荷内置 tiann / KernelSU-Next / SukiSU 三种 LKM，按已安装的管理器自动选择（`O3P_KSU_FORK` 可覆盖），
并默认运行时解析符号（kallsyms）；v2.1 让探测先检查 Shizuku 授权、用本地包管理器识别管理器、
授权后先探测再决定是否跑载荷，并使用唯一载荷文件名；v3.0 改为两段式（`selinux_state-7` 只清
enforcing + cred 写入），并默认不打 KernelSU init 补丁（`policycap` 干净，KSU 自己的
`setenforce(1)` 无害）；v1.8/v1.9 保留在 git 历史里。
- 已在 `yingtian` / `OS4.0.16.0.XBMCNXM` 实机验证（冷启动，端到端）。
- `lhasa` / `OS4.0.26.0.XPNCNXM` 携带**逐位相同的内核**
  （`sha256 cde87cd7…`）；未实机测试。
- 仅当前 boot 有效：重启后 root 与 KernelSU 消失（旧 `O3P_SELDIRTY=1` 路径下的 Permissive 也一并消失；v3 默认终态是 Enforcing）。

## 快速命令

```bash
bash scripts/verify.sh            # 静态检查 + 可选构建（提交前运行）
bash scripts/fetch-toolchain.sh   # android.jar、build-tools r34、Shizuku jars、pyelftools

# payload（需要 NDK clang）
NDK_CC=/path/to/ndk/.../aarch64-linux-android35-clang bash payload/build-payload.sh
# 参考结果：payload 73,768 B；含三种 LKM 的 libo3p.so 1,492,484 B，sha256 e64217c8…87dfa

# app（需要 SDK + Shizuku jars + 你自己的 keystore；见 docs/BUILD.md）
ANDROID_JAR=... BUILD_TOOLS=... SHIZUKU_JARS=... \
KEYSTORE=... KS_PASS=... KEY_ALIAS=... KEY_PASS=... bash app/build.sh
```

## 目录地图

| 路径 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| `payload/o3p.c` | 利用载荷（C）：UAF 触发、页回收、RB 写原语、两阶段、KASLR slide、KernelSU 重链接/kallsyms 加载（默认不打补丁，旧 `O3P_CODEPATCH=1` 才有补丁）、rootd |
| `payload/ksu/` | `ksu_symtab.h`（上游各 fork 的 248 个 KernelSU 符号并集，供 relink 回退）、`mksymtab.py`（重新生成单 LKM 表）、`relink_ksu.py`（离线重链接参考实现） |
| `payload/lkm/` | 官方 `android17-6.18` LKM（GPL-3.0，未修改）：KernelSU v3.3.1、KernelSU-Next v3.4.2、SukiSU-Ultra v4.2.1-r2 |
| `app/src/o3/st/root1/` | Java：`Main`（UI/状态机）、`PayloadRunner`、`StateProbe`、`RecoverRunner`（旧守护，v3 已停用）、`ManagerInstaller`、各监听器 |
| `app/build.sh`、`payload/build-payload.sh`、`scripts/*` | 构建入口 |
| `docs/` | ARCHITECTURE（设计+移植）、SECURITY（风险/清理）、BUILD、APP_O3_ROOT（开发日志）、OTA/内核报告、CVE 可行性、证据截图 |
| `tools/` | OTA 提取 / boot 镜像解包 / BTF+反汇编检查 |
| `release/` | 预编译 APK + SHA256SUMS |

## 不变量 —— 不要“整理/清理”

1. **内核 profile 常量是承重结构。** `INIT_USER_NS_STATIC`、
   `SELINUX_STATE_STATIC`、探针偏移、BTF 偏移与 `ksu_symtab.h` 都绑定这个
   确切的内核构建。不要重排或“简化”它们；在没有对应镜像的情况下改动 =
   内核 oops。
2. **v3 默认不给 KernelSU 打 init 补丁 —— 保持这个默认。**
   断网根因已消除：stage0 只把 `enforcing` 字节清零（在 `selinux_state-7` 做 8 字节
   非对齐零写，`initialized`/`policycap` 原样保留），所以 KSU 重新拉回
   Enforcing 后 Wi-Fi 仍正常；日志里 `KSU_CODEPATCH skip` 即正确路径。
   仅用于对比时可用 `O3P_CODEPATCH=1` 回退旧补丁（`.rela.init.text` 动态定位，
   tiann/Next `0x144`、SukiSU `0x178`；文件偏移 `0x22144`/`0x37144`/`0x3e178`）。
   **绝对不要**改 `+0x148` 的 `bl setenforce`：该槽位带 `CALL26` 重定位，加载器会重写它，
   结果是 `Undefined instruction`、模块卡在 `coming`（见 `docs/APP_O3_ROOT.md`）。
3. **写原语只能写 `0` 或“受控页指针”。** 它写入 `[forged_node]` 并解引用
   `parent = value & ~3`，所以 `0x100` 会被当作地址解引用。不要“干脆写
   0x0100”。
4. **`real_cred` 不会被替换。** 绝不要进程内调用 `finit_module` —— 必须
   新起一次 exec（`fork` + `sh -c insmod`）。进程内加载会在 `commit_creds`
   触发 BUG（`task->cred != task->real_cred`）。
5. **v3 的 stage0 不再污染 `policycap`。** 在 `selinux_state-7` 做 8 字节非对齐零写
   只清 `enforcing`，所以 Enforcing 是安全终态、App 不再带恢复守护。只有旧路径
   `O3P_SELDIRTY=1` 会污染 `policycap[]`；用它时任何 `setenforce 1` 都会让 Wi-Fi TX
   冻结，直到 `setenforce 0` 或重启。
6. **rootd 协议**：抽象 socket `@o3p_rootd`，`SO_PEERCRED` 只接受 uid 0/2000，
   每连接一条命令，以 `__EXIT=<n>` 结束。不要把全局可写命令文件重新引入
   `/data/local/tmp`。
7. **Shizuku 必须是 shell 模式**（`uid=2000`，`u:r:shell:s0`）。root 模式的
   Shizuku 运行在 `u:r:ksu:s0`，KSU 的 bounded domain 会拒绝 exec 载荷。
8. **发布卫生**：`android:debuggable` 保持关闭；`Main.checkFirmware()` 里的
   固件门禁必须与载荷所针对的内核/分支一致（`yingtian`/`lhasa`）。

## 已验证 / 未验证

实机已验证（v3.0）：完整冷启动链（`uid=0` + full caps）、KernelSU `Live` 且
**未打 init 补丁**、管理器自动打开、Enforcing 终态下网络正常（`ping`/`nc`）、
`boot_id` 未变。v1.8–v2.1 另外验证过旧的守护/一键恢复与 Permissive 期间网络正常
（这两项在 v3.0 已移除）。v2.1 发布 APK（权限感知探测：先查授权、本地识别管理器、授权后先探测再跑载荷）
已在三种管理器镜像上冷启动验证（仅 tiann / 仅 KernelSU-Next / 仅 SukiSU）：
应用识别管理器、载荷从内置 LKM 中选对应版本、对应管理器被拉到前台。

未验证：`lhasa` 实机；**原版 Shizuku**（我们用的是官方 `dev.rikka.shizuku`
API，但只实测过 ShizukuX 13.7）；`/system/bin/su` 只在 KernelSU 首次收到
root 请求后才出现。

负面结果 —— 没有新思路不要重复（细节见 `docs/AGENT_HANDOFF.md`）：

- io_uring ring 页**不能**回收已释放的 order-1 `posix_timers` slab
  （setup 走 order-0/vmalloc；实测 0 命中）。
- `AF_PACKET` `PACKET_TX_RING`（8 KB block）**可以**回收 order-1 页，命中率
  93.5% —— 但需要 `CAP_NET_RAW`，pre-root 的 shell/App 域没有。
- 伪造 `cred_security_struct.sid = 1` 得到的是 `unlabeled`，且 KernelSU 的
  `hook_file_open` 会解引用假 blob → oops（KSU 加载时该 SID 路线不可用）。

## 构建陷阱

- `d8` 遇到**匿名/内部类**会内部 NPE，也不接受目录输入；保持顶层类，并把
  `build/classes/**/*.class` 传给 d8。
- Git Bash + `javac`：classpath 用通配 `build/cp/*`；`;` 分隔符无效。
- Windows：给 `java`/`aapt2`/`zipalign` 传 `E:/` 正斜杠路径（`/e/...` 只在
  bash 里有效）。脚本会自动补 `.exe`。
- Python 生成 C/Java 源码：heredoc 会把 `\n` 变成真实换行 —— 这类字符串用
  `chr(92)+'n'` 拼，或改用编辑器工具写文件。
- `aapt2 link` 之前必须先 `aapt2 compile --dir res`；`-A assets` 用来打进
  内置的 KernelSU 管理器。
- 不要给 `payload/*.c` 提交 CRLF 变更噪音；索引统一为 LF。

## 设备测试检查表

1. 确认 Shizuku 以 shell 模式运行：
   `ps -A -o UID,LABEL,ARGS | grep shizuku_plus_server` → `2000 u:r:shell:s0`。
2. 安装：`adb install -r release/o3root2-v3.0.apk`；MIUI 若报
   `INSTALL_FAILED_USER_RESTRICTED`，允许“USB 安装”或用 root 执行
   `pm install -r -d`。
3. 打开 App，授予 Shizuku 权限，点 Continue；等待 20–120 秒。
4. 成功标记：`★ UIDNOW uid=0`、`KSU_INSMOD_OUT:[rc=0]`、
   `★ KSU_MODULE LIVE`、管理器在前台（`am start` 日志 + `top ->`）。
5. 健康检查：`getenforce` = Enforcing（v3；旧的脏写路径会停在 Permissive）、`ping <gateway>`、
   `nc 223.5.5.5 443`、`cat /proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id` 未变。
6. 回滚：重启；可选 `rm -f /data/local/tmp/o3p*` 并卸载 App。

| 症状 | 含义 / 处理 |
|---|---|
| 反复 `VERDICT NO_RECLAIM` | 竞态是概率性的；重试（通常 1–6 次），卡住就重启 |
| `固件门禁未通过` | 内核/指纹不匹配 —— 需要新的 profile |
| KernelSU 加载后断网 | 仅旧路径 `O3P_SELDIRTY=1`：policycap 被污染 + Enforcing → `setenforce 0` 或重启 |
| `KSU_CODEPATCH … -2` | 没找到 `setenforce` 重定位（唯一扫描也失败）—— 核对 LKM |
| 管理器打不开 | 检查 App 安装路径/包名，以及 KSU 管理器包是否存在 |

## 提交前卫生检查

```bash
bash scripts/verify.sh
```

它会检查：shell/Python 语法、必需文件、`ksu_symtab.h` 条目数、release APK
校验和、被跟踪的密钥文件，并扫描**每个文件（含二进制）**里的设备序列号、
主机路径、keystore 名/口令、抓包特征（MAC/BSSID/SSID/IP）。若失败，请修掉
命中项 —— 不要削弱检查。

规则：绝不提交 `tools/`（SDK/NDK/Shizuku jars）、`app/build`、
`payload/build`、`*.p12/*.jks`、`env.sh`、OTA zip、`.img`，或真实设备标识。
第三方许可证（`NOTICE.md`、`third_party/NOTICE.md`）必须保持准确 ——
KernelSU（LKM + 管理器 APK）是 GPL-3.0。

## 从哪里开始

- **移植到别的内核/设备**：`docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` → “Porting”；然后重新生成
  `payload/ksu/ksu_symtab.h`、更新常量、重新定位 KSU 补丁偏移（仅当保留旧 `O3P_CODEPATCH=1` 路径时）、更新 App
  门禁，并做冷启动测试。
- **App/UI/功能**：`app/src/o3/st/root1/*.java`；用 `app/build.sh` 构建；
  保持类集合里没有匿名内部类。
- **载荷行为**：`payload/o3p.c`；用 `payload/build-payload.sh` 重建，并把
  `libo3p.so` 的 sha 与参考值对比。
- **开放想法 / 实验历史**：`docs/AGENT_HANDOFF.md`（中文版
  `docs/AGENT_HANDOFF.zh-CN.md`）。

### v3（当前）

v3 保留两段式利用，但两次写都改了：stage0 是**只清 enforcing** 的零写
（在 `selinux_state-7` 做 8 字节非对齐写），stage1 仍是 `task_cred` 换指针。之后用运行时
kallsyms 加载器装 KSU，**不打 init 补丁**；KSU 自己的 `setenforce(1)` 无害（policycap
干净），所以设备停在 Enforcing 且 Wi-Fi 正常。曾尝试“一次 reap 三次写”，连续 panic，
现仅保留在 `O3P_D3V3` 后供分析。yingtian 冷启已验证（`KSU_CODEPATCH skip`、
`KALLSYMS_PATCH found=225`、`KSU_MODULE LIVE`、Enforcing、`ping`/`nc` 正常、管理器已打开）。

# NOTICE — third-party components

> Chinese translation: [`NOTICE.zh-CN.md`](NOTICE.zh-CN.md) — a translation of this file; if the two disagree, this English file is authoritative.

This repository is security research tooling. Run it only on hardware you own.
Several bundled or build-time components are third-party software with their own
licenses and redistribution terms.

## Prior-art credits (research; no code bundled)

- **V4bel / InfinityCore** — CVE-2026-98260 research and the public Ubuntu
  proof-of-concept. Our chain is an independent re-implementation for kernel
  6.18.21; no source code is copied.
- **EAEdward/xiaomi9pmroot** (MIT) — the tracepoint slide measurement,
  PFN-verified reclaim prototype, three-tree write experiment, and the
  unaligned `selinux_state-7` zero are adapted from that project's write-up and
  code (ideas, re-implemented here). See `docs/PRIOR-ART.md` for the itemised
  comparison and the snapshot date.
- KernelSU / KernelSU-Next / SukiSU-Ultra — see the GPL-3.0 sections below.

## KernelSU (GPL-3.0)

- Project: KernelSU — https://github.com/tiann/KernelSU
- Version used: v3.3.1 (kernel API 32680)
- Files in this repository derived from KernelSU release artifacts:
  - `payload/lkm/lkm-aarch64-android17-6.18_kernelsu.ko` — official prebuilt LKM
    for GKI `android17-6.18` (release asset, aarch64).
  - `app/assets/ksu-manager.apk` — KernelSU manager APK from the same release.
  - `release/o3root2-v3.0.apk` embeds both the manager APK and the unmodified
    official LKM; the payload generates a *runtime copy* with rebased symbols
    (via `/proc/kallsyms`); v3 applies no init patch by default (legacy
    `O3P_CODEPATCH=1` restores it), then loads that copy. The offline `payload/ksu/relink_ksu.py` does the same rebasing.
- The runtime/offline rebasing only rewrites ELF symbol values (and, only in
  legacy mode, one `mov` immediate in `.init.text`); it does not modify KernelSU source code. KernelSU itself is not
  vendored here — fetch it from the project if you need the sources, as
  required by GPL-3.0 when redistributing binaries.

## KernelSU-Next (GPL-3.0)

- Project: KernelSU-Next — https://github.com/KernelSU-Next/KernelSU-Next
- Version used: v3.4.2
- Files in this repository derived from KernelSU-Next release artifacts:
  - `payload/lkm/aarch64-next-android17-6.18_kernelsu.ko` — official prebuilt
    LKM for GKI `android17-6.18` (release asset, aarch64, 473,552 bytes).
- The same runtime rebasing (and, only under legacy `O3P_CODEPATCH=1`, the
  one-instruction init patch described above) is applied to a generated copy;
  the file in this repository is unmodified.

## SukiSU-Ultra (GPL-3.0)

- Project: SukiSU-Ultra — https://github.com/SukiSU-Ultra/SukiSU-Ultra
- Version used: v4.2.1-r2 (manager 41068)
- Files in this repository derived from SukiSU-Ultra release artifacts:
  - `payload/lkm/sukisu-aarch64-android17-6.18.ko` — official prebuilt LKM for
    GKI `android17-6.18` (release asset, aarch64, 569,752 bytes).
- Same runtime rebasing (legacy `O3P_CODEPATCH=1`: init patch) on the generated copy.

## Shizuku client libraries (Apache-2.0)

- Project: Shizuku — https://github.com/RikkaApps/Shizuku-API
- Artifacts: `dev.rikka.shizuku:api`, `:provider`, `:aidl`, `:shared` (13.1.5),
  fetched from Maven Central by `scripts/fetch-toolchain.sh`.
- Not committed to this repository; compiled classes from these jars are
  embedded in `release/o3root2-v3.0.apk`.

## Android SDK (Android SDK License)

- `android.jar` (platform 34) and build-tools (`aapt2`, `d8`, `zipalign`,
  `apksigner`) are fetched by `scripts/fetch-toolchain.sh` and are not
  committed here.

## Python dependencies

- `pyelftools` (public domain / Unlicense) is required by
  `payload/ksu/mksymtab.py` and `payload/ksu/relink_ksu.py`.

`PUBLISH.md`:

```md
# Publishing the repository (GitHub)

The tree is GitHub-ready: `main` branch, MIT license, third-party notices,
`scripts/verify.sh` (also run by `.github/workflows/verify.yml`), and no
secrets or device identifiers — the script scans both text and binaries.

## Current publication state (2026-10-25)

- `main` head: `17a8cb6`; tag `v3.0` → `dc06899`.
- Release: <https://github.com/zsw125/o3root/releases/tag/v3.0> with
  `o3root2-v3.0.apk` (14,218,768 B, sha256 `c80f499a…`) and `SHA256SUMS.txt`.
- CI: `verify` succeeded on `main`, on the merge commit, and on tag `v3.0`.

## 1. Create an empty repository

Create `o3root` on GitHub **without** a README/license/gitignore (this repo
already has them), then:

```bash
cd o3root
git remote add origin git@github.com:<you>/o3root.git    # or the https URL
git push -u origin main
```

No SSH key? Use HTTPS with a personal access token (classic, `repo` scope):

```bash
git push https://<user>:<token>@github.com/<user>/o3root.git main
```

## 2. Publish the release

The built APK is committed at `release/o3root2-v3.0.apk` next to
`release/SHA256SUMS.txt`. Also attach it to a GitHub Release:

```bash
git tag -a v3.0 -m "v3: two-reap clean enforcing-only zero, no KSU init patch"
git push origin v3.0
```

Then create the release in the web UI (or `gh release create v3.0 ...`) and
attach `release/o3root2-v3.0.apk`. Keeping the committed copy makes
`scripts/verify.sh` reproducible; if you prefer Releases-only, delete the
`release/*.apk` files from git and relax that check first.

## 3. Repository settings

- Description: `Shizuku-driven CVE-2026-98260 root + KernelSU loader for Xiaomi
  yingtian / lhasa (kernel 6.18.21)`
- Topics: `android`, `security-research`, `kernel`, `kernelsu`, `shizuku`,
  `cve-2026-98260`, `xiaomi`
- Enable Issues; keep Actions on (`verify` runs `scripts/verify.sh`).
- Don't mark it as a template repository unless you want forks to carry history.

## 4. Licensing

- Our code: MIT (`LICENSE`).
- `payload/lkm/*.ko` and `app/assets/ksu-manager.apk` are unmodified upstream
  KernelSU-family builds (GPL-3.0); `NOTICE.md` and `third_party/NOTICE.md`
  link the corresponding sources. Keep these files accurate.
- Shizuku client libraries (Apache-2.0) are compiled into the APK; the jars are
  not vendored.

## 5. Safety

This is a working local-privilege-escalation exploit for one firmware family.
Use it only on devices you own. The CVE is public; the README and
`docs/SECURITY.md` carry the disclaimer — keep it.

## 中文摘要

1. 在 GitHub 建**空**仓库 `o3root`（不要初始化 README）。
2. `git remote add origin <url>` → `git push -u origin main`。
3. `git tag v3.0 && git push origin v3.0`，在 Releases 上传
   `release/o3root2-v3.0.apk` 与 `SHA256SUMS.txt`。
4. 许可证：本项目 MIT；内置 LKM/管理器为 GPL-3.0（`NOTICE.md` 已列上游源码
   链接）；Shizuku 库 Apache-2.0。不要删除这些声明。
5. 仅供自有设备的安全研究；仓库已通过 `scripts/verify.sh` 的脱敏检查。

```

`README.md`:

```md
# O3 Root

[![verify](https://github.com/zsw125/o3root/actions/workflows/verify.yml/badge.svg)](https://github.com/zsw125/o3root/actions/workflows/verify.yml)
[![release](https://img.shields.io/github/v/release/zsw125/o3root?display_name=tag)](https://github.com/zsw125/o3root/releases)
[![license](https://img.shields.io/github/license/zsw125/o3root)](LICENSE)
[![platform](https://img.shields.io/badge/Android%2017-kernel%206.18.21-3DDC84)](docs/ARCHITECTURE.md)
[![devices](https://img.shields.io/badge/devices-yingtian%20%7C%20lhasa-3DDC84)](docs/ARCHITECTURE.md)

> Chinese translation: [`README.zh-CN.md`](README.zh-CN.md) — a translation of this file; if the two disagree, this English file is authoritative.

Local privilege escalation + KernelSU for the Xiaomi **yingtian** (Pad 9 Pro
Max) and **lhasa** (Xiaomi 18 Fold) family on HyperOS 4 (Android 17, kernel
`6.18.21`). The whole chain is delivered as a normal Android app that drives
[Shizuku](https://github.com/RikkaApps/Shizuku) in shell mode, exploits
**CVE-2026-98260**, and loads the KernelSU-family LKM that matches the installed manager
(KernelSU / KernelSU-Next / SukiSU) — no PC, no unlocked bootloader, no
partition changes.

> Research / education / device-repair project. Boot-only (a reboot removes
> it). Only run on devices you own. Read [`docs/SECURITY.md`](docs/SECURITY.md)
> before using it.

**Agents / automated contributors:** start with [`AGENTS.md`](AGENTS.md) (entry
point: invariants, build/test recipes, traps, hygiene), then
[`docs/AGENT_HANDOFF.md`](docs/AGENT_HANDOFF.md) (verified state, failed
experiments, open work). Run `bash scripts/verify.sh` before committing; it also
rebuilds the payload/APK when the toolchain environment is set.

中文版： [`AGENTS.zh-CN.md`](AGENTS.zh-CN.md) /
[`docs/AGENT_HANDOFF.zh-CN.md`](docs/AGENT_HANDOFF.zh-CN.md)

## Status

| | |
|---|---|
| release | **v3.0** (`release/o3root2-v3.0.apk`, sha256 `c80f499a…`) |
| chain | Shizuku shell → CVE-2026-98260 → `uid=0` + full caps → matching KernelSU LKM (KernelSU / KernelSU-Next / SukiSU) → rootd |
| persistence | none (current boot only) |
| tested device | Xiaomi Pad 9 Pro Max `yingtian`, HyperOS `OS4.0.16.0.XBMCNXM` |
| second device | `lhasa` (Xiaomi 18 Fold, `OS4.0.26.0.XPNCNXM`): the boot kernel is **bit-identical** to yingtian's (`sha256 cde87cd7…`), so the same payload applies; not device-tested |

## How it works (short version)

1. The app spawns `/system/bin/sh -c …` through Shizuku's `newProcess`, so the
   payload runs in `u:r:shell:s0` (`uid=2000`).
2. The payload triggers the `CLOCK_THREAD_CPUTIME_ID` + `execve`/`de_thread`
   race (CVE-2026-98260), reclaims the freed `posix_timers_cache` order-1 slab
   page with a sprayed `kmalloc-8k` buffer containing a forged RB-tree node, and
   uses the second `timerqueue_del → rb_erase` to write one 8-byte value to an
   arbitrary kernel address.
3. Stage 0 zeroes only the `enforcing` byte (8-byte unaligned write at
   `selinux_state-7`, keeping `initialized`/`policycap`); stage 1 writes
   `task_struct.cred` to a fake cred page (uid 0, full caps).
4. The payload loads the embedded KernelSU LKM: undefined symbols are resolved
   at runtime through `/proc/kallsyms` (fallback: 248-symbol union relink + the
   current KASLR slide), with **no init patch** — because `policycap` stayed
   clean, KernelSU's own `setenforce(1)` is harmless and the device ends in
   Enforcing. Then it is `insmod`ed and a `rootd` daemon is left behind
   (abstract socket `@o3p_rootd`, peer-uid 0/2000). Upstream KernelSU,
   KernelSU-Next and SukiSU LKMs all work (legacy: `O3P_CODEPATCH=1` patches the
   `setenforce` immediate).

See [`docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`](docs/ARCHITECTURE.md) for offsets, the write
primitive, the KernelSU patch and the porting guide.

## Quick start

1. On the device: enable Developer options → Wireless debugging (or ADB).
   Install **Shizuku** / ShizukuX and start it in **shell mode**:
   `shizuku_plus_server` must run as `uid=2000`, `u:r:shell:s0`.
2. Install `release/o3root2-v3.0.apk` (MIUI may need "Install via USB" allowed).
3. Open **O3 Root**, grant the Shizuku permission, press
   **Continue to UID 0**. The log shows `★ UIDNOW uid=0`,
   `★ KSU_MODULE LIVE`, and the KernelSU manager opens automatically.

No manager installed? The button turns into **Install KernelSU manager** and
installs the bundled APK (accept the system prompts).

docs/       architecture, security, build, OTA analysis, feasibility report,

## Build

```bash
bash scripts/fetch-toolchain.sh
cp scripts/env.example.sh env.sh && . ./env.sh   # edit paths
bash payload/build-payload.sh                    # -> payload/build/libo3p.so
bash app/build.sh                                # -> app/build/o3root2.apk
```

Full prerequisites and troubleshooting: [`docs/BUILD.md`](docs/BUILD.md).

## Limitations

- **Boot-only**: reboot removes root and KernelSU. Persistence would require
  patching `boot`/`init_boot` (blocked by the locked bootloader) and is out of
  scope.
- **Probabilistic**: the UAF race needs 1–6 attempts (occasionally more). The
  app retries; a reboot usually helps.
- **SELinux posture**: v3 ends in **Enforcing** with a clean `selinux_state`
  (`policycap` untouched), so MAC stays on. Only the legacy `O3P_SELDIRTY=1`
  path ends Permissive (MAC off system-wide); see the security notes.
- **Legacy policycap side effect**: only with `O3P_SELDIRTY=1`. That path
  still writes the old dirty value, so any `setenforce 1` stalls Wi-Fi TX until
  `setenforce 0` or reboot. The v3 default avoids it and ships no recovery UI
  (v1.8–v2.1 had a guard and a recovery button).
- **KernelSU LKM ABI**: upstream built the module for `6.18.32-4k`; it is
  relinked for this `6.18.21` kernel and therefore taints the kernel. Do not
  treat it as a production root solution.
- **Profile-bound**: offsets/addresses are compiled for this exact kernel. A
  different build needs a new profile (see the porting section in
  `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`).

> 英文原版：[`README.md`](README.md)。若两者不一致，以英文版为准并同步本文件。

针对 HyperOS 4（Android 17，内核 `6.18.21`）上的小米 **yingtian**（Pad 9 Pro
Max）与 **lhasa**（Xiaomi 18 Fold）系列，做本地提权 + KernelSU。整条链打包成一个
普通 Android 应用：它以 shell 模式驱动
[Shizuku](https://github.com/RikkaApps/Shizuku)，利用 **CVE-2026-98260**，
按已安装的管理器（KernelSU / KernelSU-Next / SukiSU）加载对应 LKM —— 不需要电脑、
不需要解锁 bootloader、不改任何分区。

> 研究 / 教育 / 设备维修项目。仅当前 boot 有效（重启即失效）。只在自己拥有的
> 设备上运行。使用前请读 [`docs/SECURITY.md`](docs/SECURITY.md)（中文版
> [`docs/SECURITY.zh-CN.md`](docs/SECURITY.zh-CN.md)）。

**自动化贡献者 / Agent：** 先读 [`AGENTS.md`](AGENTS.md)（入口：不变量、
构建/测试配方、坑、卫生要求），再读
[`docs/AGENT_HANDOFF.md`](docs/AGENT_HANDOFF.md)（已验证状态、失败实验、待办）。
提交前运行 `bash scripts/verify.sh`；配置好工具链环境时它还会重建 payload/APK。

中文版： [`AGENTS.zh-CN.md`](AGENTS.zh-CN.md) /
[`docs/AGENT_HANDOFF.zh-CN.md`](docs/AGENT_HANDOFF.zh-CN.md)

## 状态

| | |
|---|---|
| 发布 | **v3.0**（`release/o3root2-v3.0.apk`，sha256 `c80f499a…`） |
| 链路 | Shizuku shell → CVE-2026-98260 → `uid=0` + full caps → 对应 KernelSU LKM（KernelSU / KernelSU-Next / SukiSU）→ rootd |
| 持久化 | 无（仅当前 boot） |
| 已验证设备 | 小米 Pad 9 Pro Max `yingtian`，HyperOS `OS4.0.16.0.XBMCNXM` |
| 第二设备 | `lhasa`（Xiaomi 18 Fold，`OS4.0.26.0.XPNCNXM`）：其 boot 内核与 yingtian **逐位相同**（`sha256 cde87cd7…`），因此同一载荷适用；未实机测试 |

## 工作原理（简版）

1. App 通过 Shizuku 的 `newProcess` 拉起 `/system/bin/sh -c …`，因此载荷运行在
   `u:r:shell:s0`（`uid=2000`）。
2. 载荷触发 `CLOCK_THREAD_CPUTIME_ID` + `execve`/`de_thread` 竞态
   （CVE-2026-98260），用一个包含伪造 RB 树节点的 `kmalloc-8k` 喷页缓冲回收已
   释放的 `posix_timers_cache` order-1 slab 页，并借第二次
   `timerqueue_del → rb_erase` 向任意内核地址写入一个 8 字节值。
3. stage 0 只把 `enforcing` 字节清零（在 `selinux_state-7` 做 8 字节非对齐写，
   `initialized`/`policycap` 保留）；stage 1 把 `task_struct.cred` 指向伪造的
   cred 页（uid 0、full caps）。
4. 载荷加载内置 KernelSU LKM：未定义符号默认运行时经 `/proc/kallsyms` 解析
   （回退：248 符号并集重链接 + 当前 KASLR slide），**默认不打 init 补丁**——
   `policycap` 干净，所以 KSU 自己的 `setenforce(1)` 无害、设备停在 Enforcing。
   然后 `insmod` 并留下一个 `rootd` 守护进程（抽象 socket `@o3p_rootd`，
   peer-uid 0/2000）。上游 KernelSU、KernelSU-Next、SukiSU 三种 LKM 都可加载
   （旧行为：`O3P_CODEPATCH=1` 才会去改 `setenforce` 立即数）。

偏移、写原语、KernelSU 补丁与移植指南见
[`docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`](docs/ARCHITECTURE.md)（中文版
[`docs/ARCHITECTURE.zh-CN.md`](docs/ARCHITECTURE.zh-CN.md)）。

## 快速开始

1. 设备上：开启开发者选项 → 无线调试（或 ADB）。安装 **Shizuku** / ShizukuX，
   并以 **shell 模式** 启动：`shizuku_plus_server` 必须是 `uid=2000`、
   `u:r:shell:s0`。
2. 安装 `release/o3root2-v3.0.apk`（MIUI 可能需要允许“USB 安装”）。
3. 打开 **O3 Root**，授予 Shizuku 权限，点 **Continue to UID 0**。日志会显示
   `★ UIDNOW uid=0`、`★ KSU_MODULE LIVE`，随后 KernelSU 管理器自动打开。

没装管理器？按钮会变成 **安装 KernelSU 管理器**，点击后安装内置 APK（按系统
提示确认即可）。

tools/      OTA/内核提取 + 校验脚本

## 构建

```bash
bash scripts/fetch-toolchain.sh
cp scripts/env.example.sh env.sh && . ./env.sh   # 修改路径
bash payload/build-payload.sh                    # -> payload/build/libo3p.so
bash app/build.sh                                # -> app/build/o3root2.apk
```

完整前提与排错见 [`docs/BUILD.md`](docs/BUILD.md)（中文版
[`docs/BUILD.zh-CN.md`](docs/BUILD.zh-CN.md)）。

## 限制

- **仅当前 boot**：重启会移除 root 与 KernelSU。持久化需要改
  `boot`/`init_boot`（被锁定的 bootloader 挡住），不在范围内。
- **概率性**：UAF 竞态需要 1–6 次尝试（偶尔更多）。App 会自动重试；重启通常
  有帮助。
- **SELinux 姿态**：v3 终态是 **Enforcing**，`selinux_state` 干净（`policycap`
  不变），MAC 保持开启。只有旧路径 `O3P_SELDIRTY=1` 会停在 Permissive（全系统
  MAC 关闭）；见安全说明。
- **旧 policycap 副作用**：仅 `O3P_SELDIRTY=1` 时存在。该路径仍写旧的脏值，任何
  `setenforce 1` 都会让 Wi-Fi TX 卡住，直到 `setenforce 0` 或重启；v3 默认不碰
  `policycap`、也不再带恢复 UI（v1.8–v2.1 有守护与一键恢复）。
- **KernelSU LKM ABI**：上游模块是为 `6.18.32-4k` 构建的；这里为 `6.18.21`
  内核重链接，因此会污染内核（taint）。不要把它当生产级 root 方案。
- **绑定 profile**：偏移/地址是按这个确切内核编译的。换内核构建需要新的
  profile（见 `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` 的移植章节）。

## 证据

下的 KernelSU 管理器（`v3_ksu.png`）；旧的 v1.8 守护/恢复截图（`v18risk.png`、
`v18rec.png`）保留作为历史。详细日志与完整记录在
[`docs/APP_O3_ROOT.md`](docs/APP_O3_ROOT.md)。

- **只可用于你本人拥有**、且可以承受丢失或重置的设备；不要在共享、公司或第三方设备上使用。
- 运行本工具可能导致设备**崩溃、重启或软砖**，造成**数据丢失**，并使**保修失效**。它会修改内核内存并加载未签名内核模块；在下一次重启前，设备处于非标准、已污染状态。
- 在你所在司法辖区或设备/运营商条款下，使用本工具可能**违法或违约**；合规责任由使用者自行承担。
- 本软件按**“现状”提供，不附带任何明示或默示担保**，包括但不限于适销性、特定用途适用性与不侵权担保。作者与贡献者**不对**因使用本软件产生的任何索赔、损害或其它责任负责。
- 本项目与小米、KernelSU、Shizuku、Google 无隶属、背书或支持关系；相关商标归其各自所有者。
- 若不接受上述条款，请勿使用本软件。

`SECURITY.md`:

```md
# Security policy

## Scope

This project **intentionally ships a working local privilege-escalation
exploit** for one firmware family, so "the exploit works" is expected and is
not a vulnerability by itself. What we do care about:

- persistence or changes that survive a reboot (by design a reboot removes
  root, KernelSU and the permissive state);
- data loss or corruption outside the documented kernel-memory writes;
- rootd exposure — the daemon must listen on the abstract socket `@o3p_rootd`
  and accept only `SO_PEERCRED` uid 0/2000;
- secrets or device identifiers leaking into the repository or into logs.

## Reporting

Please **do not** open a public issue for a security problem. Use GitHub's
private vulnerability reporting:

1. Open the **Security** tab of this repository.
2. Click **Report a vulnerability**.
3. Include the app version, device, firmware, kernel (`uname -r`) and a minimal
   reproduction. Redact serial numbers, SSIDs and credentials.

If private reporting is unavailable, open a *minimal* public issue asking for a
private channel — without technical details.

## Supported versions

Only the latest release is supported. See [`docs/SECURITY.md`](docs/SECURITY.md)
for risks, cleanup steps, and what the tool changes in kernel memory.

```

`app/build.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Build the O3Root Android app (APK).
#
# Required environment:
#   ANDROID_JAR    platform-34 android.jar
#   BUILD_TOOLS    build-tools dir (aapt2[.exe], zipalign[.exe], lib/d8.jar, lib/apksigner.jar)
#   SHIZUKU_JARS   dir with api-/provider-/aidl-/shared-classes.jar
#   KEYSTORE / KS_PASS / KEY_ALIAS / KEY_PASS   signing material (your own!)
# Optional:
#   JAVA_HOME      JDK 17+ home (default: use javac/java from PATH)
#   D8_JAR         override path to d8.jar
#   PAYLOAD        path to libo3p.so (default ../payload/build/libo3p.so)
#   PY             python interpreter (default python3)
set -euo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")"

: "${ANDROID_JAR:?set ANDROID_JAR}"      ; : "${BUILD_TOOLS:?set BUILD_TOOLS}"
: "${SHIZUKU_JARS:?set SHIZUKU_JARS}"    ; : "${KEYSTORE:?set KEYSTORE}"
: "${KS_PASS:?set KS_PASS}"              ; : "${KEY_ALIAS:?set KEY_ALIAS}"
: "${KEY_PASS:?set KEY_PASS}"
PY=${PY:-python3}
PAYLOAD=${PAYLOAD:-$PWD/../payload/build/libo3p.so}
D8_JAR=${D8_JAR:-$BUILD_TOOLS/lib/d8.jar}
JBIN=${JAVA_HOME:+$JAVA_HOME/bin/}
JAVAC=${JBIN}javac
JAVA=${JBIN}java

pick() { for c in "$1" "$1.exe"; do [ -x "$c" ] && { echo "$c"; return 0; }; done; echo "missing: $1" >&2; return 1; }
AAPT2=$(pick "$BUILD_TOOLS/aapt2")
ZIPALIGN=$(pick "$BUILD_TOOLS/zipalign")
[ -f "$PAYLOAD" ] || { echo "payload not found: $PAYLOAD (build it first: payload/build-payload.sh)" >&2; exit 2; }
[ -f "$D8_JAR" ] || { echo "d8.jar not found: $D8_JAR" >&2; exit 2; }

rm -rf build
mkdir -p build/cp build/classes build/dex build/lib/arm64-v8a
cp "$ANDROID_JAR" build/cp/android.jar
for a in api provider aidl shared; do cp "$SHIZUKU_JARS/$a-classes.jar" "build/cp/$a.jar"; done
cp "$PAYLOAD" build/lib/arm64-v8a/libo3p.so

"$JAVAC" -J-Duser.language=en --release 17 -classpath "build/cp/*" -d build/classes src/o3/st/root1/*.java

"$JAVA" -cp "$D8_JAR" com.android.tools.r8.D8 \
  --release --min-api 31 --lib build/cp/android.jar --output build/dex \
  build/classes/o3/st/root1/*.class build/cp/api.jar build/cp/provider.jar build/cp/aidl.jar build/cp/shared.jar

"$AAPT2" compile --dir res -o build/res.zip
"$AAPT2" link --manifest AndroidManifest.xml -I build/cp/android.jar \
  --min-sdk-version 31 --target-sdk-version 34 -o build/base.apk -A assets build/res.zip

BASE="$PWD/build" "$PY" - <<'PY'
import zipfile, os
base = os.environ['BASE']
z = zipfile.ZipFile(os.path.join(base, 'base.apk'))
with zipfile.ZipFile(os.path.join(base, 'unsigned.apk'), 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as w:
    for it in z.infolist():
        w.writestr(it, z.read(it.filename))
    w.write(os.path.join(base, 'dex', 'classes.dex'), 'classes.dex')
    w.write(os.path.join(base, 'lib', 'arm64-v8a', 'libo3p.so'), 'lib/arm64-v8a/libo3p.so')
print('staged', os.path.getsize(os.path.join(base, 'unsigned.apk')))
PY

"$ZIPALIGN" -f -p 4 build/unsigned.apk build/aligned.apk
"$JAVA" -jar "$BUILD_TOOLS/lib/apksigner.jar" sign \
  --ks "$KEYSTORE" --ks-pass "pass:$KS_PASS" --ks-key-alias "$KEY_ALIAS" \
  --key-pass "pass:$KEY_PASS" --out o3root2.apk build/aligned.apk

ls -l o3root2.apk
"$PY" -c "import hashlib;print('sha256',hashlib.sha256(open('o3root2.apk','rb').read()).hexdigest())"

```

`app/mk_icon.py`:

```py
# Generate O3 Root launcher icon (legacy + adaptive + monochrome).
import os
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont, ImageFilter

ROOT = os.environ.get("RES_DIR") or os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "res")
FONT = os.environ.get("FONT") or (
    r"C:/Windows/Fonts/segoeuib.ttf" if os.name == "nt"
    else "/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSans-Bold.ttf")
SS = 4  # supersample factor for the legacy tile

TOP_BG = (18, 26, 36)
BOT_BG = (9, 12, 17)
G_TOP = (110, 231, 183)   # mint-300
G_BOT = (5, 150, 105)     # emerald-600


def lerp(a, b, t):
    return tuple(int(round(a[i] + (b[i] - a[i]) * t)) for i in range(3))


def vgrad(size, top, bottom):
    w, h = size
    strip = Image.new("RGB", (1, h))
    for y in range(h):
        strip.putpixel((0, y), lerp(top, bottom, y / max(1, h - 1)))
    return strip.resize((w, h), Image.BICUBIC)


def monogram(px, ratio=0.60, ss=4):
    """Emerald gradient 'O3' centered on a transparent canvas of px x px."""
    big = px * ss
    mask = Image.new("L", (big, big), 0)
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(mask)
    target = big * ratio
    size = int(target * 1.4)
    f = None
    while size > 8:
        f = ImageFont.truetype(FONT, size)
        box = d.textbbox((0, 0), "O3", font=f)
        w = box[2] - box[0]
        h = box[3] - box[1]
        if w <= target and h <= target * 0.90:
            break
        size -= 6
    box = d.textbbox((0, 0), "O3", font=f)
    w, h = box[2] - box[0], box[3] - box[1]
    x = (big - w) / 2 - box[0]
    y = (big - h) / 2 - box[1]
    glyph_mask = Image.new("L", (big, big), 0)
    ImageDraw.Draw(glyph_mask).text((x, y), "O3", font=f, fill=255)
    shadow = glyph_mask.filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(big * 0.018)).point(lambda v: int(v * 0.55))
    grad = vgrad((big, big), G_TOP, G_BOT).convert("RGBA")
    out = Image.new("RGBA", (big, big), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    shadow_layer = Image.new("RGBA", (big, big), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    shadow_layer.paste((0, 0, 0, 255), (0, 0), shadow)
    out = Image.alpha_composite(out, shadow_layer)
    glyph = Image.new("RGBA", (big, big), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    glyph.paste(grad, (0, 0), glyph_mask)
    out = Image.alpha_composite(out, glyph)
    return out.resize((px, px), Image.LANCZOS)


def legacy(px):
    """Full tile icon: gradient background, glow, hairline border, monogram."""
    big = px * SS
    img = Image.new("RGBA", (big, big), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    tile = Image.new("L", (big, big), 0)
    ImageDraw.Draw(tile).rounded_rectangle([0, 0, big - 1, big - 1],
                                           radius=int(big * 0.225), fill=255)
    bg = vgrad((big, big), TOP_BG, BOT_BG).convert("RGBA")
    img.paste(bg, (0, 0), tile)
    # soft emerald glow behind the glyph
    glow = Image.new("RGBA", (big, big), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    r = int(big * 0.34)
    ImageDraw.Draw(glow).ellipse([big // 2 - r, big // 2 - r, big // 2 + r, big // 2 + r],
                                 fill=(16, 185, 129, 120))
    glow = glow.filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(big * 0.09))
    img = Image.alpha_composite(img, Image.composite(glow, Image.new("RGBA", (big, big), (0, 0, 0, 0)), tile))
    # monogram rendered at full tile resolution
    img.alpha_composite(monogram(big, 0.62, ss=2))
    # hairline border
    ImageDraw.Draw(img).rounded_rectangle([3, 3, big - 4, big - 4],
                                          radius=int(big * 0.222), outline=(48, 66, 86, 255), width=SS)
    return img.resize((px, px), Image.LANCZOS)


def main():
    os.makedirs(os.path.join(ROOT, "mipmap-anydpi-v26"), exist_ok=True)
    os.makedirs(os.path.join(ROOT, "drawable"), exist_ok=True)
    dens = [("mdpi", 48, 108), ("hdpi", 72, 162), ("xhdpi", 96, 216),
            ("xxhdpi", 144, 324), ("xxxhdpi", 192, 432)]
    for name, legacy_px, fg_px in dens:
        d = os.path.join(ROOT, "mipmap-" + name)
        os.makedirs(d, exist_ok=True)
        legacy(legacy_px).save(os.path.join(d, "ic_launcher.png"))
        monogram(fg_px, 0.58, ss=4).save(os.path.join(d, "ic_fg.png"))
    mono = monogram(432, 0.58, ss=4)
    mono_white = Image.new("RGBA", mono.size, (0, 0, 0, 0))
    mono_white.paste((255, 255, 255, 255), (0, 0), mono.split()[3])
    mono_white.save(os.path.join(ROOT, "mipmap-xxxhdpi", "ic_mono.png"))

    with open(os.path.join(ROOT, "drawable", "ic_bg.xml"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write('<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>\n'
                '<shape xmlns:android="http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android"\n'
                '    android:shape="rectangle">\n'
                '    <gradient android:type="linear" android:angle="270"\n'
                '        android:startColor="#121A24" android:endColor="#090C11"/>\n'
                '</shape>\n')
    with open(os.path.join(ROOT, "mipmap-anydpi-v26", "ic_launcher.xml"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write('<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>\n'
                '<adaptive-icon xmlns:android="http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android">\n'
                '    <background android:drawable="@drawable/ic_bg"/>\n'
                '    <foreground android:drawable="@mipmap/ic_fg"/>\n'
                '    <monochrome android:drawable="@mipmap/ic_mono"/>\n'
                '</adaptive-icon>\n')
    print("icons written to", ROOT)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

package o3.st.root1;

public class AppendTask implements Runnable {
    private final Main main;
    private final String line;

    public AppendTask(Main main, String line) {
        this.main = main;
        this.line = line;
    }

    public void run() {
        main.writeFile(line);
        main.appendNow(line);
    }
}

```java
package o3.st.root1;

import rikka.shizuku.Shizuku;

public class BindListener implements Shizuku.OnBinderReceivedListener, Shizuku.OnBinderDeadListener {
    private final Main main;

    public BindListener(Main main) {
        this.main = main;
    }

    public void onBinderReceived() {
        main.uiBinder(true);
    }

    public void onBinderDead() {
        main.uiBinder(false);
    }
}

`app/src/o3/st/root1/DelayLaunch.java`:

```java
package o3.st.root1;

public class DelayLaunch implements Runnable {
    private final Main main;
    private final int delayMs;

    public DelayLaunch(Main main, int delayMs) {
        this.main = main;
        this.delayMs = delayMs;
    }

    public void run() {
        try {
            Thread.sleep(delayMs);
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        }
        main.launchManager();
    }
}

```
