`app/src/o3/st/root1/InstallReceiver.java`:

```java
package o3.st.root1;

import android.content.BroadcastReceiver;
import android.content.Context;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.content.pm.PackageInstaller;

public class InstallReceiver extends BroadcastReceiver {
    public void onReceive(Context c, Intent i) {
        int status = i.getIntExtra(PackageInstaller.EXTRA_STATUS, -999);
        String msg = i.getStringExtra(PackageInstaller.EXTRA_STATUS_MESSAGE);
        String pkg = i.getStringExtra(PackageInstaller.EXTRA_PACKAGE_NAME);
        Main m = Main.instance();
        if (m == null) {
            return;
        }
        if (status == PackageInstaller.STATUS_PENDING_USER_ACTION) {
            Intent it = i.getParcelableExtra(Intent.EXTRA_INTENT);
            m.installPrompt(it);
            return;
        }
        m.installResult(status, (msg == null ? "" : msg) + " pkg=" + (pkg == null ? "?" : pkg));
    }
}

```

`app/src/o3/st/root1/Main.java`:

```java
package o3.st.root1;

import android.app.Activity;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.content.res.Configuration;
import android.graphics.Typeface;
import android.graphics.drawable.GradientDrawable;
import android.os.Build;
import android.os.Bundle;
import android.os.Handler;
import android.os.Looper;
import android.text.SpannableString;
import android.text.SpannableStringBuilder;
import android.text.Spanned;
import android.text.style.ForegroundColorSpan;
import android.text.style.StyleSpan;
import android.view.Gravity;
import android.view.View;
import android.view.ViewGroup;
import android.view.Window;
import android.widget.Button;
import android.widget.LinearLayout;
import android.widget.ScrollView;
import android.widget.TextView;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileOutputStream;
import java.io.OutputStreamWriter;

import rikka.shizuku.Shizuku;

public class Main extends Activity implements PayloadSink {

    // palette, filled by applyPalette() from the current system night mode
    private int BG, CARD, CARD_EDGE, TEXT, MUTED, ACCENT, WARN, LOG, OK, BAD;
    private int DEV, LOG_BG, LOG_EDGE, BTN_TEXT;

    private LinearLayout rootView;
    private LinearLayout cardView;
    private TextView titleView, subView, devView, warnView, logTitleView;
    private boolean binderAlive;
    private boolean lastNight;

    private TextView tvShizuku;
    private TextView tvSelinux;
    private TextView tvKsu;
    private TextView logView;
    private ScrollView logScroll;
    private Button btn;

    private final Handler ui = new Handler(Looper.getMainLooper());
    private final StringBuilder logBuf = new StringBuilder();
    private final SpannableStringBuilder logSpan = new SpannableStringBuilder();
    private String payloadPath;
    private boolean running;
    private boolean rootOk;
    private boolean ksuOk;
    private String runBootId;
    private boolean fwOk;
    private boolean mgrOk;
    private String mgrFork;
    private String mgrPkg;
    private boolean netRisk;
    private boolean netRiskShown;

    static final String EXPECT_FP =
            "Xiaomi/yingtian/yingtian:17/CP2A.260605.016/OS4.0.16.0.XBMCNXM:user/release-keys";
    static final String EXPECT_KERNEL =
            "6.18.21-android17-5-g805aa6adea19-abogki565292102-4k";
    static final String WARN_DEFAULT =
            "需要 Shizuku 以 shell 模式运行（无线调试启动，无需电脑）";

    private static Main INSTANCE;

    static Main instance() {
        return INSTANCE;
    }

    protected void onCreate(Bundle b) {
        super.onCreate(b);
        INSTANCE = this;
        payloadPath = getApplicationInfo().nativeLibraryDir + "/libo3p.so";
        applyPalette();
        lastNight = isNight();
        setContentView(buildUi());
        applyWindowTheme();
        append("O3 Root v3 · CVE-2026-98260");
        append("payload=" + payloadPath);
        checkFirmware();
        Shizuku.addBinderReceivedListenerSticky(new BindListener(this));
        Shizuku.addBinderDeadListener(new BindListener(this));
        Shizuku.addRequestPermissionResultListener(new PermListener(this));
        uiBinder(Shizuku.pingBinder());
    }

    protected void onResume() {
        super.onResume();
        if (!running) {
            adoptManager(detectManager());
            probeState();
        }
    }

    private boolean isNight() {
        int m = getResources().getConfiguration().uiMode & Configuration.UI_MODE_NIGHT_MASK;
        return m == Configuration.UI_MODE_NIGHT_YES;
    }

    private void applyPalette() {
        if (isNight()) {
            BG = 0xFF0B0E13; CARD = 0xFF151A22; CARD_EDGE = 0xFF232B36;
            TEXT = 0xFFE8EEF6; MUTED = 0xFF8894A3; DEV = 0xFF66727F;
            ACCENT = 0xFF34D399; WARN = 0xFFF5B942;
            LOG = 0xFFB9C6D4; LOG_BG = 0xFF10151C; LOG_EDGE = 0xFF1D2530;
            BTN_TEXT = 0xFF07130E; OK = 0xFF34D399; BAD = 0xFFF26D6D;
        } else {
            BG = 0xFFF5F7FA; CARD = 0xFFFFFFFF; CARD_EDGE = 0xFFDCE3EC;
            TEXT = 0xFF11161D; MUTED = 0xFF5B6675; DEV = 0xFF7A8796;
            ACCENT = 0xFF0E9F6E; WARN = 0xFFB45309;
            LOG = 0xFF2A3440; LOG_BG = 0xFFF1F4F9; LOG_EDGE = 0xFFDCE3EC;
            BTN_TEXT = 0xFFFFFFFF; OK = 0xFF0E9F6E; BAD = 0xFFC0392B;
        }
    }

    private void applyWindowTheme() {
        Window w = getWindow();
        w.setStatusBarColor(BG);
        w.setNavigationBarColor(BG);
        int flags = isNight() ? 0
                : (View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_LIGHT_STATUS_BAR | View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_LIGHT_NAVIGATION_BAR);
        w.getDecorView().setSystemUiVisibility(flags);
    }

    private void restyle() {
        if (rootView == null) {
            return;
        }
        rootView.setBackgroundColor(BG);
        cardView.setBackground(roundStroked(CARD, 14, CARD_EDGE));
        titleView.setTextColor(TEXT);
        subView.setTextColor(MUTED);
        devView.setTextColor(DEV);
        warnView.setTextColor(WARN);
        logTitleView.setTextColor(MUTED);
        logScroll.setBackground(roundStroked(LOG_BG, 12, LOG_EDGE));
        logView.setTextColor(LOG);
        btn.setBackground(round(ACCENT, 14));
        btn.setTextColor(BTN_TEXT);
        tvShizuku.setTextColor(binderAlive ? OK : BAD);
        if (binderAlive) {
            tvSelinux.setTextColor(MUTED);
            tvKsu.setTextColor(MUTED);
        }
        applyWindowTheme();
        if (binderAlive && !running) {
            probeState();
        }
    }

    public void onConfigurationChanged(Configuration cfg) {
        super.onConfigurationChanged(cfg);
        boolean night = (cfg.uiMode & Configuration.UI_MODE_NIGHT_MASK) == Configuration.UI_MODE_NIGHT_YES;
        if (night == lastNight) {
            return;
        }
        lastNight = night;
        applyPalette();
        restyle();
        append("theme -> " + (night ? "dark" : "light") + " (system uiMode)");
    }

    private int dp(int v) {
        float d = getResources().getDisplayMetrics().density;
        return (int) (v * d + 0.5f);
    }

    private GradientDrawable round(int color, int radiusDp) {
        GradientDrawable g = new GradientDrawable();
        g.setColor(color);
        g.setCornerRadius(dp(radiusDp));
        return g;
    }

    private GradientDrawable roundStroked(int color, int radiusDp, int strokeColor) {
        GradientDrawable g = round(color, radiusDp);
        g.setStroke(dp(1), strokeColor);
        return g;
    }

    private TextView text(String s, int sizeSp, int color, boolean bold) {
        TextView t = new TextView(this);
        t.setText(s);
        t.setTextSize(sizeSp);
        t.setTextColor(color);
        if (bold) {
            t.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);
        }
        return t;
    }

    private LinearLayout statusRow(String label, TextView value) {
        LinearLayout row = new LinearLayout(this);
        row.setOrientation(LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL);
        row.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER_VERTICAL);
        row.setPadding(0, dp(6), 0, dp(6));
        TextView l = text(label, 13, MUTED, false);
        LinearLayout.LayoutParams lp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(0,
                ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, 1f);
        row.addView(l, lp);
        value.setTextSize(13);
        value.setTextColor(TEXT);
        row.addView(value, new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
                ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT));
        return row;
    }

    private LinearLayout buildUi() {
        LinearLayout root = new LinearLayout(this);
        rootView = root;
        root.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);
        root.setBackgroundColor(BG);
        root.setPadding(dp(18), dp(26), dp(18), dp(16));

        TextView title = text("O3 Root", 30, TEXT, true);
        titleView = title;
        root.addView(title);

        TextView sub = text("v3 · CVE-2026-98260 · OS4", 12, MUTED, false);
        subView = sub;
        sub.setPadding(0, dp(4), 0, 0);
        root.addView(sub);

        String dev = Build.MODEL + " · " + System.getProperty("os.version");
        TextView devv = text(dev, 11, DEV, false);
        devView = devv;
        devv.setPadding(0, dp(10), 0, 0);
        root.addView(devv);

        LinearLayout card = new LinearLayout(this);
        cardView = card;
        card.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);
        card.setBackground(roundStroked(CARD, 14, CARD_EDGE));
        card.setPadding(dp(14), dp(10), dp(14), dp(10));
        LinearLayout.LayoutParams clp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
                ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT);
        clp.topMargin = dp(16);
        root.addView(card, clp);

        tvShizuku = text("未连接", 13, BAD, false);
        tvSelinux = text("未知", 13, MUTED, false);
        tvKsu = text("检测中…", 13, MUTED, false);
        card.addView(statusRow("Shizuku", tvShizuku));
        card.addView(statusRow("SELinux", tvSelinux));
        card.addView(statusRow("KernelSU", tvKsu));

        TextView warn = text(WARN_DEFAULT, 11, WARN, false);
        warnView = warn;
        warn.setPadding(0, dp(14), 0, 0);
        root.addView(warn);

        btn = new Button(this);
        btn.setText("Continue to UID 0");
        btn.setTextSize(16);
        btn.setTextColor(BTN_TEXT);
        btn.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);
        btn.setBackground(round(ACCENT, 14));
        btn.setEnabled(false);
        btn.setOnClickListener(new ClickStart(this));
        LinearLayout.LayoutParams blp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
                ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, dp(52));
        blp.topMargin = dp(14);
        root.addView(btn, blp);

        TextView lt = text("LogBox", 11, MUTED, false);
        logTitleView = lt;
        LinearLayout lh = new LinearLayout(this);
        lh.setOrientation(LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL);
        lh.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER_VERTICAL);
        lh.setPadding(0, dp(16), 0, dp(6));
        lh.addView(lt, new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(0, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, 1f));
        TextView ex = text("导出日志", 11, ACCENT, false);
        ex.setOnClickListener(new ClickExport(this));
        lh.addView(ex);
        root.addView(lh);

        logScroll = new ScrollView(this);
        logScroll.setBackground(roundStroked(LOG_BG, 12, LOG_EDGE));
        LinearLayout.LayoutParams slp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
                ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, 0, 1f);
        root.addView(logScroll, slp);

        logView = new TextView(this);
        logView.setTextSize(9);
        logView.setTextColor(LOG);
        logView.setTypeface(Typeface.MONOSPACE);
        logView.setPadding(dp(10), dp(10), dp(10), dp(10));
        logScroll.addView(logView);
        return root;
    }

    public void append(final String line) {
        ui.post(new AppendTask(this, line));
    }

    void appendNow(String line) {
        if (logBuf.length() > 200000) {
            logBuf.delete(0, 80000);
            if (logSpan.length() > 80000) {
                logSpan.delete(0, 80000);
            }
        }
        logBuf.append(line).append('\n');
        logSpan.append(decor(line)).append('\n');
        logView.setText(logSpan);
        logScroll.post(new ScrollBottom(this));
    }

    private CharSequence decor(String line) {
        int color = LOG;
        boolean bold = false;
        String pre = "";
        if (line.startsWith("固件门禁通过")) {
            color = OK; bold = true; pre = "✔ ";
        } else if (line.startsWith("固件门禁未通过") || line.startsWith("固件不受支持")) {
            color = BAD; bold = true; pre = "✘ ";
        } else if (line.startsWith("binder received") || line.startsWith("binder uid=")) {
            color = ACCENT; pre = "● ";
        } else if (line.startsWith("=== running payload")) {
            color = ACCENT; bold = true; pre = "▶ ";
        } else if (line.startsWith("UIDNOW uid=0")) {
            color = OK; bold = true; pre = "★ ";
        } else if (line.startsWith("KSU_MODULE LIVE")) {
            color = OK; bold = true; pre = "★ ";
        } else if (line.startsWith("KSU_MODULE ABSENT")) {
            color = WARN; pre = "⚠ ";
        } else if (line.startsWith("ROOTD sock ready") || line.startsWith("ROOTD fork")) {
            color = ACCENT; pre = "· ";
        } else if (line.startsWith("全部完成")) {
            color = OK; bold = true; pre = "✔ ";
        } else if (line.startsWith("未获得 UID 0")) {
            color = BAD; bold = true; pre = "✘ ";
        } else if (line.startsWith("诊断:")) {
            color = WARN; pre = "⚠ ";
        } else if (line.startsWith("installer status=0")) {
            color = OK; bold = true; pre = "✔ ";
        } else if (line.startsWith("installer status=")) {
            color = WARN; pre = "⚠ ";
        } else if (line.startsWith("installer:")) {
            color = MUTED; pre = "· ";
        } else if (line.startsWith("payload exit=") || line.startsWith("am start ->")
                || line.startsWith("top ->") || line.startsWith("probe ->")
                || line.startsWith("KSU_")) {
            color = MUTED;
        } else if (line.startsWith("O3 Root v")) {
            color = ACCENT; bold = true;
        } else if (line.startsWith("checkSelfPermission=") || line.startsWith("requesting permission")
                || line.startsWith("permission result=") || line.startsWith("theme ->")) {
            color = ACCENT;
        }
        CharSequence body = pre + line;
        SpannableString ss = new SpannableString(body);
        ss.setSpan(new ForegroundColorSpan(color), 0, ss.length(), Spanned.SPAN_EXCLUSIVE_EXCLUSIVE);
        if (bold) {
            ss.setSpan(new StyleSpan(Typeface.BOLD), 0, ss.length(), Spanned.SPAN_EXCLUSIVE_EXCLUSIVE);
        }
        return ss;
    }

    void scrollBottomNow() {
        logScroll.fullScroll(ScrollView.FOCUS_DOWN);
    }

    void writeFile(String line) {
        try {
            File f = new File(getFilesDir(), "run.log");
            FileOutputStream out = new FileOutputStream(f, true);
            OutputStreamWriter w = new OutputStreamWriter(out, "UTF-8");
            w.write(line);
            w.write('\n');
            w.close();
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
        }
    }

    public void uiBinder(boolean alive) {
        ui.post(new BinderTask(this, alive));
    }

    void binderNow(boolean alive) {
        binderAlive = alive;
        if (alive) {
            String uid;
            String ctx;
            try {
                uid = String.valueOf(Shizuku.getUid());
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                uid = "?";
            }
            try {
                ctx = Shizuku.getSELinuxContext();
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                ctx = "?";
            }
            tvShizuku.setText("已连接 uid=" + uid + " " + ctx);
            tvShizuku.setTextColor(OK);
            btn.setEnabled(!running);
            append("binder uid=" + uid + " ctx=" + ctx);
            if (!running) {
                adoptManager(detectManager());
                probeState();
            }
        } else {
            tvShizuku.setText("未连接");
            tvShizuku.setTextColor(BAD);
            tvKsu.setText("未知");
            btn.setEnabled(false);
            append("binder dead");
        }
    }

    private void checkFirmware() {
        String fp = Build.FINGERPRINT;
        String kv = System.getProperty("os.version", "");
        append("fingerprint=" + fp);
        append("kernel=" + kv);
        boolean kernelOk = EXPECT_KERNEL.equals(kv);
        boolean yt = fp.contains("/yingtian/");
        boolean lh = fp.contains("/lhasa/");
        if (!kernelOk) {
            fwOk = false;
            append("固件门禁未通过：内核不是已验证的 6.18.21-android17-5-g805aa6adea19");
            if (warnView != null) {
                warnView.setText("当前内核与已验证版本不一致：" + kv + "；为避免写坏内核对象，已禁用一键提权");
                warnView.setTextColor(BAD);
            }
            if (btn != null) {
                btn.setEnabled(false);
            }
            return;
        }
        if (yt && fp.contains("OS4.0.16.0.XBMCNXM")) {
            fwOk = true;
            append("固件门禁通过（yingtian / OS4.0.16.0.XBMCNXM）");
        } else if (lh) {
            fwOk = true;
            append("固件门禁通过（lhasa；内核与 yingtian OS4.0.16 为同一 build，偏移/符号通用）");
            if (!fp.contains("OS4.0.26.0.XPNCNXM")) {
                append("提示：lhasa build 串与已验证的 OS4.0.26.0.XPNCNXM 不同，但内核一致，继续执行");
            }
        } else if (yt) {
            fwOk = true;
            append("固件门禁通过（yingtian；build 串非 4.0.16，但内核一致，继续执行）");
        } else {
            fwOk = false;
            append("固件门禁未通过：未识别的设备（仅支持 yingtian 与 lhasa）");
            if (warnView != null) {
                warnView.setText("未识别的设备：" + fp + "；本版本仅支持 yingtian / lhasa 且内核为 6.18.21-android17-5-g805aa6adea19");
                warnView.setTextColor(BAD);
            }
            if (btn != null) {
                btn.setEnabled(false);
            }
        }
    }

    void installLog(String s) {
        append("installer: " + s);
    }

    void installPrompt(Intent it) {
        if (it == null) {
            append("installer: 系统未返回确认界面");
            return;
        }
        it.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK);
        try {
            startActivity(it);
            append("installer: 等待系统安装确认");
        } catch (Throwable x) {
            append("installer: 打开确认界面失败 " + x);
        }
    }

    void installResult(int status, String msg) {
        append("installer status=" + status + " msg=" + msg);
        if (status == 0) {
            adoptManager(detectManager());
            if (!mgrOk) {
                mgrOk = true;
                mgrFork = "tiann";
                mgrPkg = "me.weishu.kernelsu";
            }
            append("KernelSU 管理器安装完成");
            applyButton();
        } else {
            btn.setText("安装 KernelSU 管理器");
            append("管理器安装未完成：可重试，或用文件管理器手动安装 APK 内 assets/ksu-manager.apk");
        }
        btn.setEnabled(true);
        probeState();
    }

    public void probeState() {
        if (!Shizuku.pingBinder()) {
            return;
        }
        if (!mgrOk) {
            adoptManager(detectManager());
        }
        try {
            if (Shizuku.checkSelfPermission() != 0) {
                probeNoPerm();
                return;
            }
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            probeNoPerm();
            return;
        }
        new Thread(new StateProbe(this), "probe").start();
    }

    void probeNoPerm() {
        append("probe: 需要 Shizuku 授权（点按钮可授权）");
        if (!mgrOk) {
            adoptManager(detectManager());
        }
        tvKsu.setText("未知");
        tvKsu.setTextColor(MUTED);
        tvSelinux.setText("未知");
        tvSelinux.setTextColor(MUTED);
        applyButton();
    }

    void probeFailed(String raw) {
        append("probe -> " + raw);
        adoptManager(detectManager());
        tvKsu.setText("未知");
        tvKsu.setTextColor(MUTED);
        tvSelinux.setText("未知");
        tvSelinux.setTextColor(MUTED);
        applyButton();
        append("probe 失败：若 Shizuku 权限未授予，点按钮授权；否则导出日志反馈");
    }

    private String detectManager() {
        try {
            String[] forks = {"tiann", "next", "sukisu"};
            String[] pkgs = {"me.weishu.kernelsu", "com.rifsxd.ksunext", "com.sukisu.ultra"};
            for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
                try {
                    getPackageManager().getPackageInfo(pkgs[i], 0);
                    return forks[i];
                } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                }
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
        }
        return null;
    }

    private void adoptManager(String fork) {
        if (fork == null) {
            return;
        }
        mgrOk = true;
        mgrFork = fork;
        if (fork.equals("tiann")) {
            mgrPkg = "me.weishu.kernelsu";
        } else if (fork.equals("next")) {
            mgrPkg = "com.rifsxd.ksunext";
        } else {
            mgrPkg = "com.sukisu.ultra";
        }
    }

    private void applyButton() {
        if (running) {
            return;
        }
        if (!fwOk) {
            btn.setEnabled(false);
        } else if (!mgrOk) {
            btn.setText("安装 KernelSU 管理器");
            btn.setEnabled(true);
        } else if (netRisk) {
            btn.setText("恢复 SELinux 宽容模式");
            btn.setEnabled(Shizuku.pingBinder());
        } else if (ksuOk) {
            btn.setText("打开 KernelSU");
            btn.setEnabled(true);
        } else {
            btn.setText("Continue to UID 0");
            btn.setEnabled(Shizuku.pingBinder());
        }
    }

    public void probeNow(String out) {
        ui.post(new ProbeTask(this, out));
    }

    void probeApply(String s) {
        if (s == null) {
            s = "";
        }
        String raw = s.trim();
        if (raw.startsWith("ERR")) {
            probeFailed(raw);
            return;
        }
        String[] parts = raw.split("\n");
        int cnt = -1;
        if (parts.length > 0) {
            try {
                cnt = Integer.parseInt(parts[0].trim());
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {
            }
        }
        String enf = parts.length > 1 ? parts[1].trim() : "";
        StringBuilder pd = new StringBuilder();
        for (int i = 0; i < parts.length; i++) {
            if (i > 0) {
                pd.append("|");
            }
            pd.append(parts[i].trim());
        }
        append("probe -> " + pd);
        String fork = null;
        for (int i = 2; i < parts.length; i++) {
            String f = parts[i].trim();
            if (f.equals("tiann") || f.equals("next") || f.equals("sukisu")) {
                fork = f;
                break;
            }
        }
        if (fork == null) {
            fork = detectManager();
        }
        adoptManager(fork);
        if (mgrOk) {
            append("manager -> fork=" + mgrFork + " pkg=" + mgrPkg);
        }
        if (enf.length() > 0) {
            tvSelinux.setText(enf);
            tvSelinux.setTextColor(enf.indexOf("Permissive") >= 0 ? OK : MUTED);
        }
        if (cnt > 0) {
            ksuOk = true;
            tvKsu.setText("已加载");
            tvKsu.setTextColor(OK);
        } else if (cnt == 0) {
            ksuOk = false;
            tvKsu.setText("未加载");
            tvKsu.setTextColor(MUTED);
        }
        boolean risk = false; /* v3: Enforcing is the normal end state (clean policycap) */
        netRisk = risk;
        if (risk) {
            tvSelinux.setText("Enforcing ⚠");
            tvSelinux.setTextColor(WARN);
            if (!netRiskShown) {
                netRiskShown = true;
                append("⚠ SELinux 已回到 Enforcing：policycap 仍是被污染的，网络会中断");
                append("⚠ 点按钮可一键恢复（先 su setenforce 0，失败则重跑载荷）");
            }
            if (warnView != null) {
                warnView.setText("SELinux 已回到 Enforcing：网络可能中断，点按钮恢复 Permissive");
                warnView.setTextColor(BAD);
            }
        } else {
            netRiskShown = false;
            if (warnView != null) {
                warnView.setText(WARN_DEFAULT);
                warnView.setTextColor(WARN);
            }
        }
        applyButton();
    }

    public void uiPermission(int grant) {
        ui.post(new PermTask(this, grant));
    }

    void permNow(int grant) {
        append("permission result=" + grant);
        if (grant == 0) {
            adoptManager(detectManager());
            new Thread(new PermProbe(this), "permprobe").start();
        }
    }

    void startAfterGrant() {
        if (running || netRisk) {
            return;
        }
        append("已授权：KSU 未加载，开始执行载荷");
        startPayload();
    }

    public void onStartClicked() {
        if (!fwOk) {
            append("固件不受支持，已阻止执行");
            return;
        }
        if (!mgrOk) {
            adoptManager(detectManager());
        }
        if (!mgrOk) {
            append("KernelSU 管理器未安装：开始安装内置管理器…");
            btn.setEnabled(false);
            btn.setText("正在安装管理器…");
            new Thread(new ManagerInstaller(this), "installer").start();
            return;
        }
        if (running) {
            append("正在运行，请稍候");
            return;
        }
        if (!Shizuku.pingBinder()) {
            append("Shizuku 未运行：请先在 Shizuku 里用无线调试启动");
            return;
        }
        int perm = Shizuku.checkSelfPermission();
        append("checkSelfPermission=" + perm);
        if (perm != 0) {
            append("requesting permission...");
            Shizuku.requestPermission(1001);
            return;
        }
        if (netRisk) {
            recoverNow();
            return;
        }
        if (ksuOk) {
            append("KernelSU 已加载，直接打开");
            new Thread(new DelayLaunch(this, 100), "launch").start();
            return;
        }
        startPayload();
    }

    void recoverNow() {
        btn.setEnabled(false);
        btn.setText("恢复中…");
        append("== 恢复 SELinux 宽容模式：su -c setenforce 0");
        new Thread(new RecoverRunner(this), "recover").start();
    }

    public void recoverDone(String out) {
        ui.post(new RecoverTask(this, out));
    }

    void recoverApply(String out) {
        String[] lines = out == null ? new String[0] : out.split("\n");
        for (int i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
            String t = lines[i].trim();
            if (t.length() > 0) {
                append("recover: " + t);
            }
        }
        boolean ok = out != null && out.indexOf("Permissive") >= 0;
        if (ok) {
            append("✔ 已恢复 SELinux 宽容模式（网络应已恢复）");
            tvSelinux.setText("Permissive");
            tvSelinux.setTextColor(OK);
            netRisk = false;
            netRiskShown = false;
            btn.setEnabled(true);
            btn.setText("打开 KernelSU");
            probeState();
        } else {
            append("⚠ su 恢复失败，改为重跑载荷（会重新执行提权链）");
            btn.setEnabled(true);
            startPayload();
        }
    }

    void startPayload() {
        if (running) {
            return;
        }
        running = true;
        rootOk = false;
        btn.setEnabled(false);
        btn.setText("运行中…");
        append("=== running payload as Shizuku shell ===");
        runBootId = readBootId();
        if (runBootId != null) {
            append("boot_id=" + runBootId);
        }
        new Thread(new PayloadRunner(payloadPath, this, mgrFork), "payload").start();
    }

    public void onLine(String line) {
        append(line);
        if (line.indexOf("UIDNOW uid=0") >= 0) {
            rootOk = true;
            ui.post(new StatusTask(this, 2));
        }
        if (line.indexOf("Permissive") >= 0) {
            ui.post(new StatusTask(this, 1));
        }
        if (line.indexOf("KSU_MODULE LIVE") >= 0) {
            ksuOk = true;
            ui.post(new StatusTask(this, 3));
        }
        if (line.indexOf("KSU_PATCH_OK") >= 0) {
            ui.post(new StatusTask(this, 4));
        }
    }

    public void onExit(int code) {
        ui.post(new ExitTask(this, code));
    }

    void exitNow(int code) {
        running = false;
        append("payload exit=" + code);
        diagnose();
        btn.setEnabled(true);
        if (ksuOk) {
            btn.setText("打开 KernelSU");
            tvKsu.setText("已加载");
            tvKsu.setTextColor(OK);
            append("全部完成，正在打开 KernelSU…");
            new Thread(new DelayLaunch(this, 1200), "launch").start();
        } else {
            probeState();
            if (rootOk) {
                btn.setText("重试 KernelSU");
                append("已获得 UID 0，但 KernelSU 未加载");
            } else {
                btn.setText("重试");
                append("未获得 UID 0，可再试一次");
            }
        }
    }

    void statusNow(int what) {
        if (what == 1) {
            tvSelinux.setText("Permissive");
            tvSelinux.setTextColor(OK);
        } else if (what == 2) {
            tvSelinux.setText("UID 0 已获得");
            tvSelinux.setTextColor(OK);
        } else if (what == 3) {
            tvKsu.setText("已加载");
            tvKsu.setTextColor(OK);
        } else if (what == 4) {
            tvKsu.setText("打补丁完成");
            tvKsu.setTextColor(WARN);
        }
    }

    String readBootId() {
        try {
            java.io.FileInputStream in = new java.io.FileInputStream("/proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id");
            byte[] b = new byte[64];
            int n = in.read(b);
            in.close();
            if (n > 0) {
                return new String(b, 0, n, "UTF-8").trim();
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
        }
        return null;
    }

    void exportLog() {
        try {
            File dir = getExternalFilesDir(null);
            if (dir == null) {
                dir = getFilesDir();
            }
            File f = new File(dir, "o3root-log-" + System.currentTimeMillis() + ".log");
            FileOutputStream out = new FileOutputStream(f);
            OutputStreamWriter w = new OutputStreamWriter(out, "UTF-8");
            w.write(logBuf.toString());
            w.close();
            append("log -> " + f.getAbsolutePath());
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            append("导出失败: " + t);
        }
    }

    void diagnose() {
        if (rootOk && ksuOk) {
            return;
        }
        String s = logBuf.toString();
        int mark = s.lastIndexOf("=== running payload");
        if (mark >= 0) {
            s = s.substring(mark);
        }
        String now = readBootId();
        if (runBootId != null && now != null && !runBootId.equals(now)) {
            append("诊断: 运行期间设备重启过（boot_id 变化）——请重新运行");
            return;
        }
        int vUaf = s.lastIndexOf("VERDICT NO_UAF_TRIGGER");
        int vRec = s.lastIndexOf("VERDICT NO_RECLAIM");
        if (vRec < 0) {
            vRec = s.lastIndexOf("PAGERECLAIM_MISS");
        }
        int vStale = s.lastIndexOf("VERDICT NO_STALE_LINK");
        int vOff = s.lastIndexOf("BAD_OFFSET");
        int vSlide = s.lastIndexOf("KSU_SKIP no_slide");
        int best = Math.max(Math.max(vUaf, vRec), Math.max(vStale, vOff));
        best = Math.max(best, vSlide);
        String tip = null;
        if (best < 0) {
            if (s.indexOf("KSU_INSMOD_OUT rc=0") < 0 && s.indexOf("KSU_INSMOD_OUT") >= 0) {
                tip = "KSU 模块加载失败（insmod 返回非 0）——检查内核版本或已有其它 root 方案";
            } else if (s.indexOf("KSU_PATCH symtabs=") >= 0 && s.indexOf("missing=0") < 0) {
                tip = "KSU 符号缺失——内核与内置模块版本不匹配";
            } else if (!rootOk) {
                tip = "载荷未取得成功标志，请查看上方日志";
            }
        } else if (best == vRec) {
            tip = "页回收失败——先清理后台应用再重试；连续失败建议重启设备";
        } else if (best == vUaf) {
            tip = "竞态未命中（UAF 未触发）——再点一次通常就能中";
        } else if (best == vStale) {
            tip = "内核对象状态异常（stale link 校验失败）——重试";
        } else if (best == vOff) {
            tip = "对象偏移不匹配——当前固件/内核可能不受支持";
        } else {
            tip = "未取到 KASLR slide——重试";
        }
        if (tip != null) {
            append("诊断: " + tip);
        }
    }

    public void launchManager() {
        String pkg = mgrPkg != null ? mgrPkg : "me.weishu.kernelsu";
        String comp = oneLine(PayloadRunner.runShellSync(
                "cmd package resolve-activity --brief " + pkg + " 2>/dev/null | tail -1"));
        String target = comp.indexOf('/') > 0 ? comp : (pkg + "/.ui.MainActivity");
        String out = PayloadRunner.runShellSync(
                "am start -W -n " + target + " 2>&1 | head -6");
        append("am start -> " + oneLine(out) + " [" + target + "]");
        boolean bad = out == null || out.indexOf("Error") >= 0 || out.indexOf("Exception") >= 0
                || out.indexOf("Warning") >= 0;
        if (bad) {
            try {
                Intent i = new Intent(Intent.ACTION_MAIN);
                i.addCategory(Intent.CATEGORY_LAUNCHER);
                i.setPackage(pkg);
                i.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK);
                startActivity(i);
                append("intent 兜底已发送");
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                append("intent 兜底失败: " + t);
            }
        }
        String top = PayloadRunner.runShellSync(
                "dumpsys activity activities 2>/dev/null | grep -m1 topResumedActivity");
        append("top -> " + oneLine(top));
    }

    private static String oneLine(String s) {
        if (s == null) {
            return "";
        }
        return s.trim().replace('\n', ' ');
    }
}

```

`app/src/o3/st/root1/ManagerInstaller.java`:

```java
package o3.st.root1;

import android.app.PendingIntent;
import android.content.Context;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.content.pm.PackageInstaller;
import android.os.Build;

import java.io.InputStream;
import java.io.OutputStream;

public class ManagerInstaller implements Runnable {
    private final Main main;

    public ManagerInstaller(Main main) {
        this.main = main;
    }

    public void run() {
        Context ctx = main.getApplicationContext();
        try {
            PackageInstaller pi = ctx.getPackageManager().getPackageInstaller();
            PackageInstaller.SessionParams sp =
                    new PackageInstaller.SessionParams(PackageInstaller.SessionParams.MODE_FULL_INSTALL);
            int id = pi.createSession(sp);
            PackageInstaller.Session s = pi.openSession(id);
            InputStream in = ctx.getAssets().open("ksu-manager.apk");
            OutputStream out = s.openWrite("base.apk", 0, -1);
            byte[] buf = new byte[65536];
            long total = 0;
            int n;
            while ((n = in.read(buf)) > 0) {
                out.write(buf, 0, n);
                total += n;
            }
            out.flush();
            out.close();
            in.close();
            Intent cb = new Intent(ctx, InstallReceiver.class);
            cb.setPackage(ctx.getPackageName());
            int flags = PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT;
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 31) {
                flags |= PendingIntent.FLAG_MUTABLE;
            }
            PendingIntent pit = PendingIntent.getBroadcast(ctx, 0, cb, flags);
            s.commit(pit.getIntentSender());
            s.close();
            main.installLog("session " + id + " wrote " + total + " bytes");
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            main.installLog("fail " + t);
        }
    }
}

```

`app/src/o3/st/root1/PayloadRunner.java`:

```java
package o3.st.root1;

import java.lang.reflect.Method;

import rikka.shizuku.Shizuku;

public class PayloadRunner implements Runnable {
    private final String payloadPath;
    private final PayloadSink sink;
    private final String fork;

    public PayloadRunner(String payloadPath, PayloadSink sink) {
        this(payloadPath, sink, null);
    }

    public PayloadRunner(String payloadPath, PayloadSink sink, String fork) {
        this.payloadPath = payloadPath;
        this.sink = sink;
        this.fork = fork;
    }

    public void run() {
        try {
            String env = fork == null ? "" : ("export O3P_KSU_FORK=" + fork + "; ");
            String bin = "/data/local/tmp/o3p_" + System.currentTimeMillis() + ".bin";
            String cmd = env + "cp '" + payloadPath + "' " + bin
                    + " && chmod 755 " + bin
                    + " && cd /data/local/tmp"
                    + " && " + bin + " 10000 14000 300 1 6 1 8000 800";
            sink.onLine("payload=" + payloadPath + " fork=" + (fork == null ? "auto" : fork));
            Process p = newProcess(new String[]{"/system/bin/sh", "-c", cmd},
                    new String[]{}, "/data/local/tmp");
            StreamPump out = new StreamPump(p.getInputStream(), sink, "payload-out");
            StreamPump err = new StreamPump(p.getErrorStream(), sink, "payload-err");
            out.start();
            err.start();
            int code = p.waitFor();
            out.join(3000);
            err.join(3000);
            sink.onExit(code);
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            sink.onLine("RUNNER " + t);
            sink.onExit(-1);
        }
    }

    private static Process newProcess(String[] cmd, String[] env, String dir) throws Exception {
        Method m = Shizuku.class.getDeclaredMethod("newProcess",
                String[].class, String[].class, String.class);
        m.setAccessible(true);
        Object o = m.invoke(null, cmd, env, dir);
        return (Process) o;
    }

    public static String runShellSync(String cmd) {
        try {
            Process p = newProcess(new String[]{"/system/bin/sh", "-c", cmd},
                    new String[]{}, "/data/local/tmp");
            java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream bos = new java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream();
            byte[] buf = new byte[4096];
            int n;
            while ((n = p.getInputStream().read(buf)) > 0) {
                bos.write(buf, 0, n);
            }
            p.waitFor();
            return new String(bos.toByteArray(), "UTF-8");
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            Throwable c = t;
            while (c.getCause() != null) {
                c = c.getCause();
            }
            return "ERR " + t.getClass().getSimpleName() + ": " + c;
        }
    }
}

`app/src/o3/st/root1/PermProbe.java`:

```java
package o3.st.root1;

public class PermProbe implements Runnable {
    private final Main main;

    public PermProbe(Main main) {
        this.main = main;
    }

    public void run() {
        String out = PayloadRunner.runShellSync(StateProbe.probeCommand());
        main.probeNow(out);
        if (out == null) {
            return;
        }
        String s = out.trim();
        if (s.length() == 0 || s.startsWith("ERR")) {
            return;
        }
        int nl = s.indexOf('\n');
        String first = (nl < 0 ? s : s.substring(0, nl)).trim();
        int cnt;
        try {
            cnt = Integer.parseInt(first);
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            return;
        }
        if (cnt == 0) {
            main.startAfterGrant();
        }
    }
}

`app/src/o3/st/root1/StateProbe.java`:

```java
package o3.st.root1;

import rikka.shizuku.Shizuku;

public class StateProbe implements Runnable {
    private final Main main;

    public StateProbe(Main main) {
        this.main = main;
    }

    static String probeCommand() {
        return "grep -c kernelsu /proc/modules 2>/dev/null; getenforce 2>/dev/null; "
                + "pm path me.weishu.kernelsu >/dev/null 2>&1 && echo tiann; "
                + "pm path com.rifsxd.ksunext >/dev/null 2>&1 && echo next; "
                + "pm path com.sukisu.ultra >/dev/null 2>&1 && echo sukisu; true";
    }

    public void run() {
        try {
            if (Shizuku.checkSelfPermission() != 0) {
                main.probeNoPerm();
                return;
            }
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            main.probeNoPerm();
            return;
        }
        String out = PayloadRunner.runShellSync(probeCommand());
        main.probeNow(out);
    }
}

`app/src/o3/st/root1/StreamPump.java`:

```java
package o3.st.root1;

import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.io.InputStreamReader;

public class StreamPump extends Thread {
    private final InputStream in;
    private final PayloadSink sink;

    public StreamPump(InputStream in, PayloadSink sink, String name) {
        super(name);
        this.in = in;
        this.sink = sink;
        setDaemon(true);
    }

    public void run() {
        try {
            BufferedReader r = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(in, "UTF-8"));
            String l;
            while ((l = r.readLine()) != null) {
                sink.onLine(l);
            }
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            sink.onLine("PUMP " + t);
        }
    }
}

```

`docs/AGENT_HANDOFF.md`:

```md
# Agent handoff — state, history, and open work

> 中文版：[`AGENT_HANDOFF.zh-CN.md`](AGENT_HANDOFF.zh-CN.md)。若中英文不一致，以英文版为准。

This is the second document an automated contributor should read after
`AGENTS.md`. It records what has actually been verified on hardware, the
experiments that already failed (so they are not repeated), and the remaining
work with rough effort estimates.

## Project state

| item | value |
|---|---|
| release | v3.0, `release/o3root2-v3.0.apk` (14,218,768 B, sha256 `c80f499a…`) |
| verified device | Xiaomi Pad 9 Pro Max `yingtian`, HyperOS `OS4.0.16.0.XBMCNXM`, kernel `6.18.21-android17-5-g805aa6adea19-abogki565292102-4k` |
| second target | `lhasa` (Xiaomi 18 Fold) `OS4.0.26.0.XPNCNXM` — same kernel sha256 `cde87cd7…` (boot header/ramdisk differ); **not device-tested** |
| persistence | none (current boot only) |
| entry path | Shizuku **shell** server (`uid=2000`, `u:r:shell:s0`) → app → payload |

Success markers in the app log (in order):
`★ UIDNOW uid=0` → `KSU_INSMOD_OUT:[rc=0]` → `★ KSU_MODULE LIVE` →
`✔ 全部完成，正在打开 KernelSU…` → `top -> …me.weishu.kernelsu/.ui.MainActivity`.
Health: `getenforce` = `Enforcing` (v3; legacy `O3P_SELDIRTY=1` ends
Permissive), network works, `boot_id` unchanged.
Dates/commits/credits: `docs/PRIOR-ART.md` §5–7; superseded reports:
`docs/history/README.md`.

## How it got here (compressed timeline)

1. **Shell-domain payload** (`payload/legacy/t32.c`, `t34.c`): CVE-2026-98260
   UAF → order-1 slab reclaim via kmalloc-8k spray → forged RB node → single
   8-byte write. Stage 0 wrote `selinux_state.enforcing=0`; stage 1 replaced
   `task_struct.cred` with a fake cred (uid 0, full caps) → `uid=0`, rootd.
2. **KernelSU**: official LKM for `android17-6.18`; the loader resolves all
   undefined symbols at runtime via `/proc/kallsyms` (no embedded table needed),
   falling back to the 248-symbol union + KASLR slide from `init_user_ns`, then
   `insmod` from a forked shell.
3. **APK**: Shizuku `newProcess` runs the payload in the shell domain; status
   probes (`/proc/modules`, `getenforce`, `pm path`); firmware gate; bundled
   KernelSU manager APK + `PackageInstaller` flow; themed UI; log export.
4. **v1.7 fix (network)**: KernelSU's init called `getenforce()==0 →
   setenforce(1)`; combined with the polluted `policycap[]` this froze Wi-Fi TX.
   The loader patches that immediate (`mov w0,#1`→`#0`) at the `setenforce`
   relocation in `.init.text` minus 4 (0x144 tiann/Next, 0x178 SukiSU), so KSU
   does not re-enable enforcing. Cold-boot verified: KSU Live, manager opens,
   network OK. (v3.0 no longer patches — see item 7.)
5. **v1.8 guard (historical; removed in v3.0)**: v1.8–v2.1 warned when KSU
   was loaded and SELinux had rebounded to Enforcing, offering one-click
   `su -c setenforce 0`. v3 removes it: stage 0 clears only `enforcing`, so
   Enforcing is a safe end state (see §5 and the v3.0 notes).
6. **v1.9 runtime loader**: the payload resolves KernelSU symbols through
   `/proc/kallsyms` at load time (fallback: 248-symbol union relink + slide),
   locates the `setenforce` patch point from `.rela.init.text`, and works with
   tiann, KernelSU-Next and SukiSU LKMs; the v1.9 APK was cold-boot verified.

7. **v3.0 (current, 2026-10-25)**: two reaps instead of one. Stage 0 is an
   unaligned 8-byte zero at `selinux_state-7` (clears only `enforcing`, keeps
   `initialized`/`policycap`), stage 1 is the `task_cred` swap; KernelSU loads
   through the runtime kallsyms loader with **no init patch**, so its own
   `setenforce(1)` leaves the network healthy and the device in Enforcing. The
   single-reap three-write variant (v3a–v3c) panicked three times and stays
   only behind `O3P_D3V3`. See `docs/V3-DESIGN.md` and the "v3.0" section below.

The full narrative with logs is in `docs/APP_O3_ROOT.md` (Chinese, original dev
log; directory names there are historical: `work/`→`payload/`, `app5/`→`app/`).

## Experiments already done — do not repeat

### 1. io_uring ring pages as an order-1 reclaim source — **negative**

Goal: replace the kmalloc-8k spray (which needs 8 KB of controlled content)
with `io_uring` mmap'able ring pages, which are unmovable `GFP_KERNEL` pages.

- `io_uring_setup` succeeds from the shell domain (no seccomp, `io_uring_disabled=0`).
- kprobes on `io_allocate_scq_urings`, `__alloc_pages_noprof`,
  `__alloc_frozen_pages_noprof`, `__vmalloc_node_range_noprof`,
  `vm_area_alloc_pages` saw **zero** calls during setup.
- buddy tracepoint `kmem:mm_page_alloc` with filter `order>=1`: **zero** events
  for 100 rings of 128 entries.
- Full reclaim test (4000 leaked timers + 1500 rings of nr=64, MAGIC fill):
  0/4000 hits; ring pages are order-0/vmalloc.
- Conclusion: io_uring ring pages cannot reclaim the freed `posix_timers`
  order-1 slab. The current spray path (`send(...,7872)` → kmalloc-8k) already
  has the right order.

### 2. `AF_PACKET` TX ring — **positive but unusable pre-root**

600 sockets × `tp_block_size=8192` mmap'ed and filled: reclaim hit **3738/4000
(93.5%)**, the strongest order-1 writable-page primitive found. But:

- shell domain `CapEff=0`; `socket(AF_PACKET)` → `EPERM`.
- So it is only available after root is already obtained — no help for the
  pre-root reclaim step; could still be useful for a second-stage write or as a
  faster post-root primitive.

### 3. Forged `cred_security_struct.sid=1` (kernel SID route) — **negative**

Goal: avoid writing `selinux_state` at all; make the payload process kernel
domain (sid=1) through a fake SELinux blob, then `setenforce 0` via sysfs.

- SID 1 maps to `u:object_r:unlabeled:s0` in this policy (not kernel).
- KernelSU's `hook_file_open` dereferenced the fake blob and oopsed:
  `pc : hook_file_open.cold+0x4`, `Comm: yt2-parent`, avc shows
  `scontext=u:object_r:unlabeled:s0`.
- With KSU loaded the route is unusable. Without KSU it might not crash, but it
  still grants at most `unlabeled`, not kernel.

### 4. Zero-write to `selinux_state` (avoid policycap pollution) — **negative**

Writing 8 zero bytes gives `enforcing=0, initialized=0, policycap=0`. Network
stays fine and KernelSU loads, but `initialized=0` breaks `zygote`'s
`selinux_android_setcontext` for new apps (`com.xiaomi.bluetooth` fatal), so the
KSU manager cannot start and the device is unusable until reboot.

Why not write `0x0100` (initialized=1, policycap=0)? The primitive writes
`[node]` and dereferences `parent = value & ~3`; only `0` keeps parent 0. A
pointer value is the only other safe option, and its high bytes land in
`policycap` anyway.

### 5. Legacy `policycap` side effect (pre-v3 paths) — **historical**

The old stage 0 wrote 8 bytes at `selinux_state`, so `policycap[]` received
address garbage; any later `setenforce 1` froze `wlan0` TX (RX kept working) and
the shell domain could not `setenforce 0` (no `mac_admin`) — recovery was
KernelSU `su -c setenforce 0`, or reboot. **v3 default avoids all of this** by
zeroing a single byte at `selinux_state-7` (see the v3.0 notes); the old
behaviour survives only behind `O3P_SELDIRTY=1`.

### 6. Label repair — **positive, operational note**

`/data/local/tmp` files created during the abandoned zero-write boot were
`u:object_r:unlabeled:s0` and blocked shell access. With root + non-enforcing:
`chcon -R u:object_r:shell_data_file:s0 /data/local/tmp` works (labels are
persistent xattrs); `restorecon -R` skips the directory itself. Normal v1.7+ (including v3.0)
runs create correctly labeled `shell_data_file` files.

## Environment notes (device work)

- adb: `adb -s <serial> ...`; on Windows-bash `export MSYS2_ARG_CONV_EXCL='*'`
  before remote paths.
- Shizuku must be **shell mode**; with `su` present ShizukuX restarts in root
  mode (`uid=0`, `u:r:ksu:s0`) and the chain cannot exec the payload from that
  bounded domain. Test cold boots that way.
- `/system/bin/su` appears only after KernelSU's first root request (manager
  "superuser" grant); absence before that is expected.
- MIUI: `adb install` may return `INSTALL_FAILED_USER_RESTRICTED`; allow
  install-via-USB or use root `pm install -r -d`.
- tracefs: `kprobe_events` is 0777 but `events/*/enable` is root-only; probes
  must be enabled by a root process.
- memlock default 64 KB limits `io_uring` contexts (`ulimit -l` needed);
  irrelevant for the shipped payload.
- seccomp (clean cold boot): init/adbd/zygote/shizuku/rootd 0/0; system_server,
  systemui, apps 2/1. The app's own seccomp is not changed by the exploit.
- kernel flags relevant here: `CONFIG_MODULES=y`, `MODULE_SIG_FORCE` unset,
  `MODVERSIONS`, `CFI`, `TRIM_UNUSED_KSYMS`, `KALLSYMS_ALL`, `DEBUG_INFO_BTF`,
  `kptr_restrict=2`, `panic_on_warn=0`, `panic_on_oops=0`.

## Open work / candidates

Ordered by value per effort. None are required for v3.0 to work; all are
research-grade improvements.

1. **Eliminate the second `timerqueue_del`** (easy → medium). Currently the
   fake tree causes a second deletion that runs as a `WARN` (oops with
   `panic_on_warn=0` tolerated). Design a tree shape where the first erase is
   terminal (e.g. write 0 with `[N]=0`, `[V]=0` invariants) so the reap never
   touches the corrupted queue again. This reduces crash surface and may allow
   `panic_on_warn=1` scenarios.
2. **rootd hardening** (easy): random socket suffix + a token in the first
   message in addition to `SO_PEERCRED`; restrict to the calling app's uid
   instead of all of 0/2000; keep logs 0600.
3. **Validate original Shizuku** (easy-medium): the code uses the official
   `dev.rikka.shizuku` 13.1.5 API (api/provider/aidl/shared + `newProcess`);
   only ShizukuX 13.7 was exercised. Test on a device with stock Shizuku in
   shell mode, both as provider and permission flows.
4. **Cold-boot success-rate harness** (medium): script N reboots + taps; record
   attempts-to-success, `VERDICT` failure distribution, time-to-root, network
   health. Useful to tune `g_att`, spray sizing and `g_mode`.
5. **Kernel profile JSON** (medium): move constants/offsets out of `o3p.c` into
   a generated header keyed by `(kernel release, fingerprint)`, and generate
   `ksu_symtab.h` per profile. This is the main prerequisite for supporting
   more devices (e.g. other XRING O3 products) instead of a rebuild per kernel.
6. **KSU userspace reliability** (medium): manager auto-open and `su` work in
   the normal flow, but with `initialized=0` (not shipped) they break; also the
   first `su` installation is lazy. Consider making the app explicitly run
   `ksud install` (or trigger a root request) as part of setup.
7. **App features** (easy): in-app root shell (the rootd socket already
   supports `--shell`), KSU allow-list shortcut, copy/share exported log,
   i18n, tablet two-pane layout, "restart required" guidance after failures.
8. **Crash forensics** (blocked): pstore/ramoops is empty and only readable
   from a kernel-domain context; our kernel-SID route is blocked (see above).
   Without a new way to read `/sys/fs/pstore`, panics cannot be captured.
9. **LKM ABI risk** (hard): the upstream LKM was built for `6.18.32-4k`; it is
   relinked and loaded on `6.18.21`. Building the module from KernelSU source
   against this kernel's config (or a matching GKI) would remove the ABI
   mismatch and the "modified module" concern; needs kernel sources/config.

## Reproduction workflow (dev loop)

```bash
# payload
NDK_CC=... bash payload/build-payload.sh          # -> libo3p.so (sha must match)
# app
ANDROID_JAR=... BUILD_TOOLS=... SHIZUKU_JARS=... \
KEYSTORE=... KS_PASS=... KEY_ALIAS=... KEY_PASS=... bash app/build.sh
# device
adb install -r app/o3root2.apk
adb shell 'am start -n o3.st.root1/.Main'
# (UI tap) then watch log:
adb shell 'tail -f /data/data/o3.st.root1/files/run.log'   # only with debuggable builds
# or use the in-app "Export log" -> /sdcard/Android/data/o3.st.root1/files/
```

The payload can be tested standalone (root or shell): push `libo3p.so` as
`o3p.bin`, `chmod 755`, run with `10000 14000 300 1 6 1 8000 800`.

## Do not

- Do not commit `tools/` toolchain, `app/build`, `payload/build`, keystores,
  `env.sh`, OTA zips, `.img` files, or device identifiers.
- Do not patch the KernelSU `bl setenforce` call site (CALL26 relocation).
- Do not "fix" the write value to `0x100` (dereference crash).
- Do not use the legacy `O3P_SELDIRTY=1` path on a device you cannot reboot or
  `su`-recover; the v3 default ends Enforcing and is safe.
- Do not run this on devices you don't own; see `docs/SECURITY.md`.

## v2.0 — adaptive LKM (2026-10-25)

- The blob now carries three LKMs (KernelSU v3.3.1 375,328 B, KernelSU-Next
  v3.4.2 473,552 B, SukiSU v4.2.1-r2 569,752 B) in a v2 container (per-entry
  tag table + `O3KSKM02`/count trailer; legacy `O3KSKO01` still readable).
- The payload detects the installed manager (`me.weishu.kernelsu` → tag 1,
  `com.rifsxd.ksunext` → 2, `com.sukisu.ultra` → 3; `O3P_KSU_FORK`
  overrides), slices out that LKM and loads it; the app probes the same manager,
  passes the fork to the payload and opens the matching manager through a
  resolved activity.
- Cold-boot verified on three images (only KernelSU / only KernelSU-Next / only
  SukiSU installed); `/data/local/tmp/o3p_ksu.ko` sizes and manager foregrounds
  matched the selection every time; SELinux Permissive (v2.x era) and Wi-Fi
  working.

## v2.1 — permission-aware probe (2026-10-25)

- The probe runs only after Shizuku is authorized (`checkSelfPermission`),
  detects the manager through the local `PackageManager` (three packages), and
  re-probes after a successful `PackageInstaller` install; payload files get a
  unique `o3p_<millis>.bin` name to avoid `ETXTBSY`.
- Fixes the 18 Fold deadlock (`ERR java.lang.reflect.InvocationTargetException`
  was parsed as a normal probe result, so "install manager" looped).

## v3.0 — two-reap, no KernelSU init patch (2026-10-25)

- Stage 0 now zeroes a single byte: 8-byte unaligned write at
  `selinux_state-7` (clears `enforcing`, keeps `initialized`/`policycap`); the
  old dirty write remains behind `O3P_SELDIRTY=1`.
- Stage 1 is still the `task_cred` swap; the two stages use two independent
  reaps (single-reap three-write panicked three times — kept only behind
  `O3P_D3V3`).
- KernelSU loads through the runtime kallsyms loader with **no init patch**
  (`KSU_CODEPATCH skip`); its `setenforce(1)` leaves Wi-Fi healthy because
  `policycap` stayed clean. The app therefore drops the v1.8 guard/recovery UI.
- Cold-boot verified (yingtian): `KALLSYMS_PATCH found=225 missing=0`,
  `KSU_INSMOD_OUT:[rc=0]`, `KSU_MODULE LIVE`, Enforcing, `ping` 22 ms,
  `nc 443` rc=0, manager auto-opened, `boot_id` unchanged.

```
